    <DotsMark table="sales" x="category" y="individual_sales" 
              preferredXScaleType="linear" />  <!-- Will get band instead -->
  </ChartContainer>
  
  <!-- This would error - incompatible requirements -->
  <ChartContainer xLabel="sales" yLabel="category">
    <!-- This mandates x must be linear -->
    <SomeLinearOnlyMark x="sales" y="category" />
    
    <!-- This mandates x must be band - CONFLICT! -->  
    <BarXMark x="sales" y="category" />  <!-- Error: incompatible requirements -->
  </ChartContainer>
  */
  
  // ===== API Benefits =====
  
  // 1. **Explicit Requirements**
  //    - Each mark clearly states what it needs (MANDATE/PREFER/ACCEPT)
  //    - Clear error messages when requirements conflict
  //    - Visible validation logic in mark components
  
  // 2. **Flexible Adaptation**
  //    - Marks can adapt to resolved scale types
  //    - Warnings when marks get something different than preferred
  //    - Graceful degradation for compatible scale types
  
  // 3. **No Hidden Magic**
  //    - All scale checking happens in mark components
  //    - Developer can see exactly what's happening
  //    - Easy to debug scale resolution issues
  
  // 4. **Clear Error Messages**
  //    - "BarXMark requires band scale for x-axis, got: linear"
  //    - "Scale types resolved differently than preferred"
  //    - Helps developers understand mark requirements
  
  // 5. **Composable and Predictable**
  //    - Can mix marks with different requirements
  //    - Priority system resolves conflicts deterministically  
  //    - Clear rules for what works together
</src/lib/components/marks/RectMarks.md>

<src/lib/components/marks/BarMark.svelte>
  <script>
      import { DataClient } from '$lib/data-clients/DataClient.svelte.js';
      import { getContext, onMount, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
      import { getScaleState } from '$lib/scales';
      import { SCALE_PRIORITIES } from '$lib/scales/ScalePriorities';
      // import { Rects } from '$lib/components';
      import { Dots } from '$lib/components';
    
      let { 
        table, 
        selection, 
        x, 
        y, 
        color, 
        alpha = 0.8,
        orientation = 'vertical', // 'vertical' | 'horizontal'
        limit = 5000 
      } = $props();
    
      const coordinator = getContext('mosaic-coordinator');
      const c = getContext('chart');
    
      const markId = `bar-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
    
      // Get color scale for the color encoding
      let colorScale = $derived(getScaleState(color, 'ordinal'));
      let client = $state(null);
    
      // Register scale types based on orientation
      // Vertical bars: x=categorical (band), y=continuous (linear)
      // Horizontal bars: x=continuous (linear), y=categorical (band)
      if (orientation === 'vertical') {
        c.registerScaleType('x', 'band', SCALE_PRIORITIES.MANDATE, markId);
        c.registerScaleType('y', 'linear', SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER, markId);
      } else {
        c.registerScaleType('x', 'linear', SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER, markId);
        c.registerScaleType('y', 'band', SCALE_PRIORITIES.MANDATE, markId);
      }
    
      function createClient() {
        if (client) {
          client.destroy();
          client = null;
        }
    
        client = new DataClient(table, selection, {
          select: { x, y },
          // select: { x, y, color },
          limit,
          // filterNulls: [x, y],
          dataFormat: 'array',
          name: markId
        });
    
        if (coordinator) {
          coordinator.connect(client);
        }
      }
  
      // In BarMark.svelte or any mark needing band scales
      async function fetchCategoricalDomain() {
          console.log('fetching categories')
  
          if (c.scaleTypes?.x !== 'band' && c.scaleTypes?.y !== 'band') {
              return; // No categorical scales needed
          }
          
          // Fetch distinct values for x-axis if it's categorical
          if (c.scaleTypes?.x === 'band') {
              const distinctQuery = Query.from(table)
              .select({ x: sqlColumn(x) })
              .distinct();
              
              const result = await coordinator.query(distinctQuery);
              const categories = Array.from(result).map(row => row.x);
              
              c.xScaleState.addMark(categories, markId);
          }
  
          if (c.scaleTypes?.y === 'band') {
              const distinctQuery = Query.from(table)
              .select({ y: sqlColumn(y) })
              .distinct();
              
              const result = await coordinator.query(distinctQuery);
              const categories = Array.from(result).map(row => row.y);
              
              c.xScaleState.addMark(categories, markId);
          }
      }
    
      onMount(async () => {
          await fetchCategoricalDomain();
          createClient();
      });
    
      $effect(() => {
        void x; void y; void color; void table; void orientation;
        untrack(() => {
          if (client) createClient();
        })
      });
    
      onDestroy(() => {
        c.unregisterScaleType('x', markId);
        c.unregisterScaleType('y', markId);
    
        if (client) {
          client.destroy();
        }
      });
  
      $inspect(client?.data)
    
    </script>
    
    {#if client?.arrowTable}
      <Dots
        data={client.arrowTable}
        dataVersion={client.dataVersion}
        alpha={alpha}
        orientation={orientation}
      />
    {:else if client?.error}
      <text x={c.marginLeft ?? 20} y={c.marginTop ?? 20} fill="red" font-size="14">
        Error: {client.error.message}
      </text>
    {:else if client?.delayedLoading}
      <text x={c.marginLeft ?? 20} y={c.marginTop ?? 20} fill="currentColor" font-size="14">
        Loading...
      </text>
    {:else}
      <text x={c.marginLeft ?? 20} y={c.marginTop ?? 20} fill="currentColor" font-size="14">
        No data available
      </text>
    {/if}
</src/lib/components/marks/BarMark.svelte>

<src/lib/components/rendering/Dots.svelte>
  <script>
    import { getContext, onMount, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
    import { getScaleState } from '$lib/scales';
    import { BaseGPURenderer } from './BaseGPURenderer.svelte.js';
    import { getGPUContext } from '$lib/gpu';
  
    let { 
      data, // Arrow Table
      dataVersion,
      r = 3,
      fill = 'blue',
      alpha = .5,
      zIndex = 0,
    } = $props();
  
    let colorScale = $derived(getScaleState('color', 'ordinal'));
    
    const c = getContext('chart');
    const webGPUContext = getContext('webgpu-canvas');
    const gpuCoordinator = getGPUContext();
  
    // Use BaseGPURenderer for common functionality
    let renderer = $state(null);
    let initializationError = $state(null);
  
    // Dots-specific buffers (managed through base renderer)
    let xBuffer;
    let yBuffer;
    let radiusBuffer;
    let alphaBuffer;
    let colorBuffer;
    let quadGeometryBuffer;
    
    const quadVertexCount = 6;
  
    // Component-specific shader code - much simpler now!
    const dotsShaderCode = `
      struct VertexOutput {
        @builtin(position) position: vec4<f32>,
        @location(0) color: vec4<f32>,
        @location(1) uv: vec2<f32>,
        @location(2) radius: f32,
        @location(3) alpha: f32,
      };
  
      @group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> resolution: vec2<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> xValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> yValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read> radiusValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(4) var<storage, read> alphaValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(5) var<storage, read> colors: array<vec4<f32>>;
      @group(0) @binding(6) var<uniform> scaleParams: ScaleParams;
      @group(0) @binding(7) var<uniform> devicePixelRatio: f32;
      
      @vertex
      fn vertexMain(
        @location(0) quadPosition: vec2<f32>,
        @builtin(instance_index) instanceIdx: u32,
      ) -> VertexOutput {
        // Get values from separate buffers
        let x = xValues[instanceIdx];
        let y = yValues[instanceIdx];
        let radius = radiusValues[instanceIdx];
        let alpha = alphaValues[instanceIdx];
        let color = colors[instanceIdx];
        
        // Use centralized scale functions - works for any scale type!
        let scaledX = scaleX(x, scaleParams);
        let scaledY = scaleY(y, scaleParams);
        
        // Rest of dots-specific positioning logic stays the same
        let displayRadius = radius * devicePixelRatio;
        let radiusWithPadding = displayRadius * 1.2;
        let worldPos = vec2<f32>(scaledX * devicePixelRatio, scaledY * devicePixelRatio) + quadPosition * radiusWithPadding;
        let normalizedPos = (worldPos / resolution) * 2.0 - 1.0;
        
        var output: VertexOutput;
        output.position = vec4<f32>(normalizedPos.x, -normalizedPos.y, 0.0, 1.0);
        output.color = color;
        output.uv = quadPosition;
        output.radius = displayRadius;
        output.alpha = alpha;
        return output;
      }
  
      @fragment
      fn fragmentMain(input: VertexOutput) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
        let dist = length(input.uv);
        let sdf = dist - 1.0;
        let pixelSize = devicePixelRatio / (input.radius * 2.0);
        let alpha = 1.0 - smoothstep(-pixelSize, pixelSize, sdf);
        let finalAlpha = input.color.a * alpha * input.alpha;
        return vec4<f32>(input.color.rgb, finalAlpha);
      }
    `;
  
    // Generate complete shader with centralized scale support
    const shaderCode = BaseGPURenderer.generateShader(dotsShaderCode);
  
    // Dots-specific vertex buffer layout
    const vertexBufferLayout = [{
      arrayStride: 2 * 4, // 2 floats * 4 bytes
      attributes: [{
        shaderLocation: 0,
        offset: 0,
        format: 'float32x2',
      }],
    }];
  
    // Create quad geometry for dot rendering
    function createQuadGeometry() {
      return new Float32Array([
        -1, -1,  // bottom-left
         1, -1,  // bottom-right
        -1,  1,  // top-left
         1, -1,  // bottom-right
         1,  1,  // top-right
        -1,  1,  // top-left
      ]);
    }
  
    // Initialize dots-specific geometry
    async function initializeDotsGeometry() {
      const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
      if (!device) {
        throw new Error('No WebGPU device available');
      }
  
      // Create quad geometry buffer
      const quadVertices = createQuadGeometry();
      quadGeometryBuffer = device.createBuffer({
        size: quadVertices.byteLength,
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.VERTEX | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
      device.queue.writeBuffer(quadGeometryBuffer, 0, quadVertices);
    }
  
    // Update instance data using base renderer's buffer management
    function updateInstanceData(arrowTable) {
      try {
        if (!arrowTable || arrowTable.numRows === 0 || !renderer?.resourcesCreated) {
          console.debug('⚠️ Skipping dots instance data update - no data or resources not ready');
          return;
        }
  
        const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
        if (!device) {
          throw new Error('No WebGPU device available');
        }
  
        const startTime = performance.now();
        const instanceCount = arrowTable.numRows;
  
        console.debug(`📊 Updating dots instance data for ${instanceCount} dots`);
  
        // Get Arrow columns with error checking
        const xColumn = arrowTable.getChild('x');
        const yColumn = arrowTable.getChild('y');
        const colorColumn = arrowTable.getChild('color');
  
        if (!xColumn || !yColumn) {
          throw new Error('Required x and y columns not found in Arrow table');
        }
  
        // Process data through BaseGPURenderer for proper scale handling
        const xValues = xColumn.toArray();
        const yValues = yColumn.toArray();
        
        // Use BaseGPURenderer's data processing for scale-aware conversion
        const xData = renderer.processDataForAxis(xValues, 'x');
        const yData = renderer.processDataForAxis(yValues, 'y');
  
        // Update position buffers using base renderer's smart buffer management
        xBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(xBuffer, xData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(xBuffer, 0, xData);
  
        yBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(yBuffer, yData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(yBuffer, 0, yData);
  
        // Create radius and alpha data
        const radiusData = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
        const alphaData = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
        
        if (typeof r === 'function' || typeof alpha === 'function') {
          for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
            radiusData[i] = typeof r === 'function' ? r({x: xValues[i], y: yValues[i]}) : r;
            alphaData[i] = typeof alpha === 'function' ? alpha({x: xValues[i], y: yValues[i]}) : alpha;
          }
        } else {
          radiusData.fill(r);
          alphaData.fill(alpha);
        }
  
        // Update attribute buffers
        radiusBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(radiusBuffer, radiusData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(radiusBuffer, 0, radiusData);
  
        alphaBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(alphaBuffer, alphaData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(alphaBuffer, 0, alphaData);
  
        // Handle colors with error checking
        const colorData = new Float32Array(instanceCount * 4);
        
        if (colorColumn) {
          const colorArray = colorColumn.toArray();
          for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
            const fillColor = colorScale(colorArray[i]);
            const rgba = renderer.parseColor(fillColor);
            colorData.set(rgba, i * 4);
          }
        } else {
          // Fallback to single color
          const rgba = renderer.parseColor(fill);
          for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
            colorData.set(rgba, i * 4);
          }
        }
  
        colorBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(colorBuffer, colorData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(colorBuffer, 0, colorData);
  
        // Create bind group after all buffers are ready
        createDotsBindGroup();
  
        // Update instance count
        renderer.updateInstanceCount(instanceCount);
  
        const updateTime = performance.now() - startTime;
        console.debug(`✅ Dots instance data updated in ${updateTime.toFixed(2)}ms`);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to update dots instance data:', error);
        initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Create dots-specific bind group
    function createDotsBindGroup() {
      // Validate all buffers exist
      if (!xBuffer || !yBuffer || !radiusBuffer || !alphaBuffer || !colorBuffer) {
        console.warn('⚠️ Cannot create dots bind group - missing data buffers');
        return;
      }
  
      // Shape-specific bindings (bindings 1-5)
      const dotsBindings = [
        { binding: 1, resource: { buffer: xBuffer } },
        { binding: 2, resource: { buffer: yBuffer } },
        { binding: 3, resource: { buffer: radiusBuffer } },
        { binding: 4, resource: { buffer: alphaBuffer } },
        { binding: 5, resource: { buffer: colorBuffer } },
      ];
  
      // Use base renderer to create bind group (includes common bindings)
      renderer.createBindGroup(dotsBindings);
    }
  
    // Dots-specific draw function
    function drawDots(passEncoder) {
      try {
        if (!renderer?.pipeline || !renderer?.bindGroup || renderer.instanceCount === 0) {
          console.debug('⚠️ Skipping dots draw - resources not ready or no instances');
          return;
        }
  
        passEncoder.setPipeline(renderer.pipeline);
        passEncoder.setBindGroup(0, renderer.bindGroup);
        passEncoder.setVertexBuffer(0, quadGeometryBuffer);
        passEncoder.draw(quadVertexCount, renderer.instanceCount);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Dots draw operation failed:', error);
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Wait for WebGPU context with better error handling
    async function waitForWebGPU() {
      let attempts = 0;
      const maxAttempts = 100;
      
      while (!webGPUContext.isInitialized() && attempts < maxAttempts) {
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
        attempts++;
      }
      
      if (!webGPUContext.isInitialized()) {
        throw new Error(`WebGPU context failed to initialize within ${maxAttempts * 50}ms`);
      }
      
      await initializeDots();
    }
  
    // Initialize dots renderer
    async function initializeDots() {
      try {
        console.log('🚀 Initializing Dots component with centralized scale support');
  
        // Check GPU coordinator is available
        if (!gpuCoordinator) {
          throw new Error('GPU Coordinator context missing. Make sure setCoordinators() was called.');
        }
  
        // Create base renderer with scale type information
        renderer = new BaseGPURenderer('dots', webGPUContext, gpuCoordinator, c, { 
          zIndex,
          xScaleType: c.scaleTypes?.x || 'linear',
          yScaleType: c.scaleTypes?.y || 'linear'
        });
  
        // Initialize with dots-specific configuration using centralized shader
        await renderer.initialize({
          shaderCode, // This now includes universal scale support!
          vertexBufferLayout,
        });
  
        // Initialize dots-specific geometry
        await initializeDotsGeometry();
  
        // Update instance data if available
        if (data) {
          updateInstanceData(data);
        }
        
        // Register layer with base renderer
        renderer.registerLayer(drawDots);
  
        console.log('✅ Dots component initialized successfully with centralized scales');
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Dots component initialization failed:', error);
        initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    onMount(async () => {
      try {
        await waitForWebGPU();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Dots component mount failed:', error);
        initializationError = error.message;
      }
    });
  
    onDestroy(() => {
      console.log('🧹 Cleaning up Dots component');
      
      try {
        // Clean up dots-specific resources
        if (quadGeometryBuffer && !quadGeometryBuffer.destroyed) {
          quadGeometryBuffer.destroy();
        }
  
        // Base renderer handles all other cleanup
        if (renderer) {
          renderer.destroy();
        }
  
        console.log('✅ Dots component cleanup complete');
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Error during Dots component cleanup:', error);
      }
    });
  
    // Enhanced reactive effect for data updates
    $effect(() => {
      void dataVersion;
      
      untrack(() => {
        if (renderer?.resourcesCreated && !initializationError && data) {
          try {
            updateInstanceData(data);
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('❌ Dots data update effect failed:', error);
            initializationError = error.message;
          }
        }
      });
    });
  
    // Reactive effect for scale changes - BaseGPURenderer handles the heavy lifting
    $effect(() => {
      if (!renderer) return;
      
      // Access scale properties to make this reactive
      const xScale = c?.xScale;
      const yScale = c?.yScale;
      
      // Track scale domains and ranges
      void xScale?.domain?.();
      void yScale?.domain?.();
      void xScale?.range?.();
      void yScale?.range?.();
      
      // Track band scale properties for completeness
      void xScale?.bandwidth?.();
      void yScale?.bandwidth?.();
      
      untrack(() => {
        renderer.handleScaleChange();
      });
    });
  
    // Reactive effect for resolution changes
    $effect(() => {
      if (!renderer) return;
      
      const { width, height } = webGPUContext.getCanvasDimensions();
      
      untrack(() => {
        renderer.handleResolutionChange();
      });
    });
  </script>
  
  {#if initializationError}
    <div class="dots-error">
      <p>Dots component failed to initialize:</p>
      <p class="error-message">{initializationError}</p>
    </div>
  {/if}
  
  <style>
    .dots-error {
      position: absolute;
      top: 10px;
      left: 10px;
      background-color: rgba(255, 240, 240, 0.9);
      border: 1px solid #ffcccb;
      border-radius: 4px;
      padding: 8px 12px;
      font-size: 12px;
      color: #d32f2f;
      z-index: 1000;
      max-width: 300px;
    }
  
    .error-message {
      font-family: monospace;
      background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8);
      padding: 4px 6px;
      border-radius: 2px;
      margin-top: 4px;
      font-size: 11px;
    }
  </style>
</src/lib/components/rendering/Dots.svelte>

<src/lib/components/rendering/Dots copy.svelte>
  <script>
    import { getContext, onMount, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
    import { getScaleState } from '$lib/scales';
  
    let { 
      data, // This would be an Arrow Table
      dataVersion,
      r = 3,
      fill = 'blue',
      alpha = .5,
      zIndex = 0,
    } = $props();
  
    let colorScale = $derived(getScaleState('color', 'ordinal'));
    
    const c = getContext('chart');
    const webGPUContext = getContext('webgpu-canvas');
  
    // WebGPU resources
    let pipeline;
    let bindGroup;
    let resourcesCreated = false;
  
    // Separate buffers for Arrow columns
    let xBuffer;
    let yBuffer;
    let radiusBuffer;
    let alphaBuffer;
    let colorBuffer;
    let scaleParamsBuffer;
    let resolutionBuffer;
    let dprBuffer;
    let instanceCount = 0;
    
    let layerControl;
    let quadGeometryBuffer;
    const quadVertexCount = 6;
  
    let xDomain = $derived(c.xScale?.domain() ?? []);
    let yDomain = $derived(c.yScale?.domain() ?? []);
    let xRange = $derived(c.xScale?.range() ?? []);
    let yRange = $derived(c.yScale?.range() ?? []);
  
    const devicePixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  
    // Updated shader to work with separate buffers
    const shaderCode = `
      struct VertexOutput {
        @builtin(position) position: vec4<f32>,
        @location(0) color: vec4<f32>,
        @location(1) uv: vec2<f32>,
        @location(2) radius: f32,
        @location(3) alpha: f32,
      };
  
      struct ScaleParams {
        domainMin: vec2<f32>,
        domainMax: vec2<f32>,
        rangeMin: vec2<f32>,
        rangeMax: vec2<f32>,
      };
  
      @group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> resolution: vec2<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> xValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> yValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read> radiusValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(4) var<storage, read> alphaValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(5) var<storage, read> colors: array<vec4<f32>>;
      @group(0) @binding(6) var<uniform> scaleParams: ScaleParams;
      @group(0) @binding(7) var<uniform> devicePixelRatio: f32;
      
      fn scaleValue(value: f32, domainMin: f32, domainMax: f32, rangeMin: f32, rangeMax: f32) -> f32 {
        let normalized = (value - domainMin) / (domainMax - domainMin);
        return rangeMin + normalized * (rangeMax - rangeMin);
      }
      
      @vertex
      fn vertexMain(
        @location(0) quadPosition: vec2<f32>,
        @builtin(instance_index) instanceIdx: u32,
      ) -> VertexOutput {
        // Get values from separate buffers
        let x = xValues[instanceIdx];
        let y = yValues[instanceIdx];
        let radius = radiusValues[instanceIdx];
        let alpha = alphaValues[instanceIdx];
        let color = colors[instanceIdx];
        
        // Apply scales on GPU
        let scaledX = scaleValue(x, scaleParams.domainMin.x, scaleParams.domainMax.x, 
                                 scaleParams.rangeMin.x, scaleParams.rangeMax.x);
        let scaledY = scaleValue(y, scaleParams.domainMin.y, scaleParams.domainMax.y, 
                                 scaleParams.rangeMin.y, scaleParams.rangeMax.y);
        
        let displayRadius = radius * devicePixelRatio;
        let radiusWithPadding = displayRadius * 1.2;
        let worldPos = vec2<f32>(scaledX * devicePixelRatio, scaledY * devicePixelRatio) + quadPosition * radiusWithPadding;
        let normalizedPos = (worldPos / resolution) * 2.0 - 1.0;
        
        var output: VertexOutput;
        output.position = vec4<f32>(normalizedPos.x, -normalizedPos.y, 0.0, 1.0);
        output.color = color;
        output.uv = quadPosition;
        output.radius = displayRadius;
        output.alpha = alpha;
        return output;
      }
  
      @fragment
      fn fragmentMain(input: VertexOutput) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
        let dist = length(input.uv);
        let sdf = dist - 1.0;
        let pixelSize = devicePixelRatio / (input.radius * 2.0);
        let alpha = 1.0 - smoothstep(-pixelSize, pixelSize, sdf);
        let finalAlpha = input.color.a * alpha * input.alpha;
        return vec4<f32>(input.color.rgb, finalAlpha);
      }
    `;
  
    function createQuadGeometry() {
      return new Float32Array([
        -1, -1,  // bottom-left
         1, -1,  // bottom-right
        -1,  1,  // top-left
         1, -1,  // bottom-right
         1,  1,  // top-right
        -1,  1,  // top-left
      ]);
    }
  
    async function createGPUResources(device) {
      const shaderModule = device.createShaderModule({
        code: shaderCode,
      });
      const msaaSampleCount = webGPUContext.getMsaaSampleCount();
  
      pipeline = device.createRenderPipeline({
        layout: 'auto',
        vertex: {
          module: shaderModule,
          entryPoint: 'vertexMain',
          buffers: [{
            arrayStride: 2 * 4,
            attributes: [{
              shaderLocation: 0,
              offset: 0,
              format: 'float32x2',
            }],
          }],
        },
        fragment: {
          module: shaderModule,
          entryPoint: 'fragmentMain',
          targets: [{
            format: webGPUContext.getPresentationFormat(),
            blend: {
              color: {
                srcFactor: 'src-alpha',
                dstFactor: 'one-minus-src-alpha',
                operation: 'add',
              },
              alpha: {
                srcFactor: 'one',
                dstFactor: 'one-minus-src-alpha',
                operation: 'add',
              },
            },
          }],
        },
        primitive: {
          topology: 'triangle-list',
        },
        multisample: {
          count: msaaSampleCount > 1 ? msaaSampleCount : 1  // Must be at least 1
        },
      });
  
      const quadVertices = createQuadGeometry();
      quadGeometryBuffer = device.createBuffer({
        size: quadVertices.byteLength,
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.VERTEX | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
      device.queue.writeBuffer(quadGeometryBuffer, 0, quadVertices);
  
      resolutionBuffer = device.createBuffer({
        size: 8,
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
  
      dprBuffer = device.createBuffer({
        size: 4,
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
  
      const { width, height } = webGPUContext.getCanvasDimensions();
      device.queue.writeBuffer(resolutionBuffer, 0, new Float32Array([width, height]));
      device.queue.writeBuffer(dprBuffer, 0, new Float32Array([devicePixelRatio]));
  
      resourcesCreated = true;
    }
  
    function updateInstanceData(device, arrowTable) {
      const tsStartUpdate = Date.now();
      if (!arrowTable || arrowTable.numRows === 0 || !resourcesCreated) return;
  
      instanceCount = arrowTable.numRows;
  
      // Get Arrow columns
      const xColumn = arrowTable.getChild('x');
      const yColumn = arrowTable.getChild('y');
      const colorColumn = arrowTable.getChild('color');
  
      // Convert Float64Array to Float32Array
      const xData = new Float32Array(xColumn.toArray());
      const yData = new Float32Array(yColumn.toArray());
  
      // Create or update buffers with proper size and alignment
      function createOrUpdateBuffer(buffer, data, minSize = 16384) {
        const size = Math.max(data.byteLength, minSize);
        if (!buffer || buffer.size < size) {
          if (buffer) buffer.destroy();
          return device.createBuffer({
            size,
            usage: GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
          });
        }
        return buffer;
      }
  
      // Update x buffer
      xBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(xBuffer, xData);
      device.queue.writeBuffer(xBuffer, 0, xData);
  
      // Update y buffer  
      yBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(yBuffer, yData);
      device.queue.writeBuffer(yBuffer, 0, yData);
  
      // Create radius and alpha data
      const radiusData = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
      const alphaData = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
      
      if (typeof r === 'function' || typeof alpha === 'function') {
        for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
          radiusData[i] = typeof r === 'function' ? r({x: xData[i], y: yData[i]}) : r;
          alphaData[i] = typeof alpha === 'function' ? alpha({x: xData[i], y: yData[i]}) : alpha;
        }
      } else {
        radiusData.fill(r);
        alphaData.fill(alpha);
      }
  
      // Update radius buffer
      radiusBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(radiusBuffer, radiusData);
      device.queue.writeBuffer(radiusBuffer, 0, radiusData);
  
      // Update alpha buffer
      alphaBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(alphaBuffer, alphaData);
      device.queue.writeBuffer(alphaBuffer, 0, alphaData);
  
      // Handle colors
      const colorData = new Float32Array(instanceCount * 4);
      const colorArray = colorColumn.toArray();
      
      for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
        const fillColor = colorScale(colorArray[i]);
        const rgba = parseColor(fillColor);
        colorData.set(rgba, i * 4);
      }
  
      colorBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(colorBuffer, colorData);
      device.queue.writeBuffer(colorBuffer, 0, colorData);
  
      // Create scale parameters buffer if it doesn't exist
      if (!scaleParamsBuffer) {
        scaleParamsBuffer = device.createBuffer({
          size: 32,
          usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
        });
      }
      updateScaleParameters(device);
  
      // Create bind group only after all buffers are ready
      if (!bindGroup) {
        createBindGroup(device);
      }
    }
  
    function createBindGroup(device) {
      // Make sure all buffers exist before creating bind group
      if (!xBuffer || !yBuffer || !radiusBuffer || !alphaBuffer || !colorBuffer || !scaleParamsBuffer || !resolutionBuffer || !dprBuffer || !pipeline) {
        console.warn('Not all buffers are ready for bind group creation');
        return;
      }
  
      bindGroup = device.createBindGroup({
        layout: pipeline.getBindGroupLayout(0),
        entries: [
          { binding: 0, resource: { buffer: resolutionBuffer } },
          { binding: 1, resource: { buffer: xBuffer } },
          { binding: 2, resource: { buffer: yBuffer } },
          { binding: 3, resource: { buffer: radiusBuffer } },
          { binding: 4, resource: { buffer: alphaBuffer } },
          { binding: 5, resource: { buffer: colorBuffer } },
          { binding: 6, resource: { buffer: scaleParamsBuffer } },
          { binding: 7, resource: { buffer: dprBuffer } },
        ],
      });
    }
  
    function updateScaleParameters(device) {
      if (!scaleParamsBuffer) return;
  
      const scaleParams = new Float32Array(8);
      scaleParams[0] = xDomain[0] ?? 0;
      scaleParams[1] = yDomain[0] ?? 0;
      scaleParams[2] = xDomain[1] ?? 1;
      scaleParams[3] = yDomain[1] ?? 1;
      scaleParams[4] = xRange[0] ?? 0;
      scaleParams[5] = yRange[0] ?? 0;
      scaleParams[6] = xRange[1] ?? 1;
      scaleParams[7] = yRange[1] ?? 1;
  
      device.queue.writeBuffer(scaleParamsBuffer, 0, scaleParams);
    }
  
    function draw(passEncoder, device, presentationFormat) {
      if (!pipeline || !bindGroup || instanceCount === 0) return;
  
      passEncoder.setPipeline(pipeline);
      passEncoder.setBindGroup(0, bindGroup);
      passEncoder.setVertexBuffer(0, quadGeometryBuffer);
      passEncoder.draw(quadVertexCount, instanceCount);
    }
  
    function parseColor(color) {
      if (color.startsWith('#')) {
        const r = parseInt(color.slice(1, 3), 16) / 255;
        const g = parseInt(color.slice(3, 5), 16) / 255;
        const b = parseInt(color.slice(5, 7), 16) / 255;
        return [r, g, b, 1.0];
      }
      return [0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0];
    }
  
    async function initialize() {
      const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
      if (device) {
        await createGPUResources(device);
        updateInstanceData(device, data);
        
        // Register this component as a WebGPU layer
        layerControl = webGPUContext.registerLayer({
          zIndex,
          draw,
          visible: true
        });
      }
    }
  
    // Wait for WebGPU to be initialized before setting up resources
    async function waitForWebGPU() {
      while (!webGPUContext.isInitialized()) {
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
      }
      await initialize();
    }
  
    onMount(async () => {
      await waitForWebGPU();
    });
  
    onDestroy(() => {
      // Clean up WebGPU resources
      if (quadGeometryBuffer) quadGeometryBuffer.destroy();
      if (xBuffer) xBuffer.destroy();
      if (yBuffer) yBuffer.destroy();
      if (radiusBuffer) radiusBuffer.destroy();
      if (alphaBuffer) alphaBuffer.destroy();
      if (colorBuffer) colorBuffer.destroy();
      if (scaleParamsBuffer) scaleParamsBuffer.destroy();
      if (resolutionBuffer) resolutionBuffer.destroy();
      if (dprBuffer) dprBuffer.destroy();
      
      // Clean up layer
      if (layerControl) {
        layerControl.remove();
      }
    });
  
    // Update instance data when dataVersion changes;
    $effect(() => {
      void dataVersion;
      
      untrack(() => {
        const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
        if (device && layerControl && resourcesCreated) {
          updateInstanceData(device, data);
          layerControl.invalidate();
        }
      });
    });
  
    // Update the scale change effect to only update scale parameters
    $effect(() => {
      void xDomain, yDomain, xRange, yRange;
      const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
      if (device && layerControl && resourcesCreated && scaleParamsBuffer) {
        // Only update scale parameters, not the entire bind group
        updateScaleParameters(device);
        layerControl.invalidate();
      }
    });
  
    // Update resolution when canvas size changes
    $effect(() => {
      // Trigger on width and height changes from the chart context
      // void c.innerWidth;
      // void c.innerHeight;
      const { width, height } = webGPUContext.getCanvasDimensions();
      const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
      if (resolutionBuffer && device && layerControl && resourcesCreated) {
        device.queue.writeBuffer(resolutionBuffer, 0, new Float32Array([width, height]));
        // layerControl.invalidate();
        // console.log('resolution buffer updated')
      }
    });
  </script>
</src/lib/components/rendering/Dots copy.svelte>

<src/lib/components/rendering/index.js>
  export { default as Canvas } from './Canvas.svelte';
  export { default as WebGPUCanvas } from './WebGPUCanvas.svelte';
  export { default as Dots } from './Dots.svelte';
</src/lib/components/rendering/index.js>

<src/lib/components/rendering/WebGPUCanvas.svelte>
  <script>
    import { onMount, onDestroy, setContext, getContext } from "svelte";
    import { getGPUContext } from '$lib/gpu';
  
    let {
      canvasComponents,
      msaaSampleCount = 4 
    } = $props();
  
    const c = getContext('chart');
    let width = $derived(c.innerWidth);
    let height = $derived(c.innerHeight);
    let margin = $derived(c.margin);
  
    let canvas;
    let context;
    let gpuCoordinator;
    let chartControl;
    let contextConfigured = $state(false);
    let initializationError = $state(null);
  
    const chartId = `chart-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
    const devicePixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    const layers = [];
  
    // Enhanced initialization with comprehensive error handling
    async function initWebGPU() {
      try {
        console.log(`🚀 Initializing WebGPUCanvas ${chartId}`);
        
        // Get shared GPU coordinator
        gpuCoordinator = getGPUContext();
        
        if (!gpuCoordinator) {
          throw new Error('GPUCoordinator not found. Ensure setCoordinators() was called in a parent component.');
        }
  
        // Wait for coordinator initialization with timeout
        let attempts = 0;
        const maxAttempts = 100; // 5 seconds at 50ms intervals
        
        while (!gpuCoordinator.isInitialized && attempts < maxAttempts) {
          await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
          attempts++;
        }
  
        if (!gpuCoordinator.isInitialized) {
          throw new Error(`GPUCoordinator failed to initialize within ${maxAttempts * 50}ms timeout`);
        }
  
        // Configure context with proper error handling
        await configureContext();
        
        // Register with coordinator after context is ready
        registerWithCoordinator();
        
        console.log(`✅ WebGPUCanvas ${chartId} initialized successfully`);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ WebGPUCanvas ${chartId} initialization failed:`, error);
        initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Separate context configuration with proper error handling
    async function configureContext() {
      if (!canvas) {
        throw new Error('Canvas element not available for context configuration');
      }
  
      try {
        // Get WebGPU context
        context = canvas.getContext('webgpu');
        if (!context) {
          throw new Error('Failed to get WebGPU context from canvas. WebGPU may not be supported.');
        }
  
        const device = gpuCoordinator.device;
        const presentationFormat = gpuCoordinator.presentationFormat;
  
        if (!device) {
          throw new Error('No WebGPU device available from coordinator');
        }
  
        // Configure context
        context.configure({
          device,
          format: presentationFormat,
          alphaMode: 'premultiplied',
        });
        
        contextConfigured = true;
        console.log(`🎨 Context configured successfully for chart ${chartId}`);
        
        // Setup initial canvas size
        setupCanvas();
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Context configuration failed for chart ${chartId}:`, error);
        contextConfigured = false;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Clean registration with coordinator
    function registerWithCoordinator() {
      if (!contextConfigured) {
        console.error(`⚠️ Attempting to register chart ${chartId} before context is configured`);
        return;
      }
  
      console.log(`📋 Registering chart ${chartId} with coordinator`);
      
      try {
        chartControl = gpuCoordinator.registerChart(chartId, canvas, {
          drawFunction: drawLayers,
          zIndex: 0,
          markTypes: layers.map(layer => layer.markType || 'unknown'),
        });
  
        // Immediately notify coordinator that context is ready
        chartControl.update({ contextConfigured: true });
        console.log(`✅ Chart ${chartId} registered and ready`);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Chart registration failed for ${chartId}:`, error);
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Robust canvas setup with dimension change detection
    function setupCanvas() {
      if (!canvas || !contextConfigured) {
        console.debug(`⚠️ setupCanvas called before canvas/context ready for ${chartId}`);
        return;
      }
      
      try {
        // Calculate new dimensions
        const canvasWidth = width * devicePixelRatio;
        const canvasHeight = height * devicePixelRatio;
        
        // Only update if dimensions actually changed to avoid unnecessary work
        const dimensionsChanged = 
          canvas.width !== canvasWidth || 
          canvas.height !== canvasHeight;
        
        const positionChanged = 
          canvas.style.transform !== `translate(${margin.left}px, ${margin.top}px)`;
  
        if (dimensionsChanged) {
          canvas.width = canvasWidth;
          canvas.height = canvasHeight;
          canvas.style.width = width + 'px';
          canvas.style.height = height + 'px';
          
          console.debug(`📐 Canvas ${chartId} resized to ${canvasWidth}x${canvasHeight}`);
        }
  
        if (positionChanged) {
          canvas.style.transform = `translate(${margin.left}px, ${margin.top}px)`;
          console.debug(`📍 Canvas ${chartId} repositioned to (${margin.left}, ${margin.top})`);
        }
  
        // Notify coordinator of changes
        if (chartControl && (dimensionsChanged || positionChanged)) {
          chartControl.update({ 
            canvasResized: dimensionsChanged,
            canvasRepositioned: positionChanged 
          });
        }
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Canvas setup failed for ${chartId}:`, error);
        initializationError = `Canvas setup failed: ${error.message}`;
      }
    }
  
    // Enhanced layer rendering with comprehensive error handling
    function drawLayers(passEncoder, sharedResources) {
      const visibleLayers = layers.filter(layer => layer.visible !== false);
      
      if (visibleLayers.length === 0) {
        console.debug(`📝 No visible layers to render for chart ${chartId}`);
        return;
      }
  
      console.debug(`🎨 Rendering ${visibleLayers.length} layers for chart ${chartId}`);
      
      let successfulLayers = 0;
      let failedLayers = 0;
      
      for (let i = 0; i < visibleLayers.length; i++) {
        const layer = visibleLayers[i];
        try {
          layer.draw(passEncoder, gpuCoordinator.device, gpuCoordinator.presentationFormat);
          successfulLayers++;
        } catch (error) {
          console.error(`❌ Layer ${i} (${layer.markType || 'unknown'}) render failed:`, error);
          failedLayers++;
          // Continue rendering other layers even if one fails
        }
      }
  
      if (failedLayers > 0) {
        console.warn(`⚠️ Chart ${chartId}: ${successfulLayers} layers rendered successfully, ${failedLayers} failed`);
      }
    }
  
    // Enhanced layer management with better tracking
    function createLayerControl(layer) {
      const layerId = `layer-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 6)}`;
      console.log(`📋 Registering layer ${layerId} (${layer.markType || 'unknown'}) for chart ${chartId}`);
      
      const zIndex = layer.zIndex || 0;
      let insertIndex = layers.findIndex(l => (l.zIndex || 0) > zIndex);
      if (insertIndex === -1) insertIndex = layers.length;
      
      const layerWithId = { ...layer, id: layerId, chartId };
      layers.splice(insertIndex, 0, layerWithId);
      
      console.log(`✅ Layer ${layerId} registered. Total layers for ${chartId}: ${layers.length}`);
      
      if (chartControl) {
        chartControl.update({ 
          markTypes: layers.map(l => l.markType || 'unknown')
        });
        chartControl.invalidate();
      }
      
      return {
        update(newProps) {
          const oldMarkType = layerWithId.markType;
          Object.assign(layerWithId, newProps);
          
          // Update chart registration if mark types changed
          if (newProps.markType && newProps.markType !== oldMarkType && chartControl) {
            chartControl.update({ 
              markTypes: layers.map(l => l.markType || 'unknown')
            });
          }
          
          chartControl?.invalidate();
        },
        
        remove() {
          const index = layers.indexOf(layerWithId);
          if (index !== -1) {
            layers.splice(index, 1);
            console.log(`🗑️ Layer ${layerId} removed. Total layers for ${chartId}: ${layers.length}`);
            
            if (chartControl) {
              chartControl.update({ 
                markTypes: layers.map(l => l.markType || 'unknown')
              });
              chartControl.invalidate();
            }
          }
        },
        
        invalidate() {
          chartControl?.invalidate();
        },
        
        // Additional debugging info
        getInfo() {
          return {
            layerId,
            chartId,
            markType: layerWithId.markType,
            zIndex: layerWithId.zIndex,
            visible: layerWithId.visible
          };
        }
      };
    }
  
    // Enhanced context with better state management and error handling
    setContext('webgpu-canvas', {
      registerLayer(layer) {
        try {
          return createLayerControl(layer);
        } catch (error) {
          console.error(`❌ Layer registration failed for chart ${chartId}:`, error);
          // Return a dummy control to prevent crashes
          return {
            update: () => {},
            remove: () => {},
            invalidate: () => {},
            getInfo: () => ({ error: error.message })
          };
        }
      },
      
      getCanvasDimensions() {
        return { 
          width: width * devicePixelRatio, 
          height: height * devicePixelRatio,
          cssWidth: width,
          cssHeight: height,
          devicePixelRatio
        };
      },
  
      getMsaaSampleCount() {
        return gpuCoordinator?.msaaSampleCount || msaaSampleCount;
      },
  
      getDevice() {
        return gpuCoordinator?.device;
      },
  
      getPresentationFormat() {
        return gpuCoordinator?.presentationFormat;
      },
  
      isInitialized() {
        return contextConfigured && gpuCoordinator?.isInitialized;
      },
      
      invalidate() {
        chartControl?.invalidate();
      },
  
      // Enhanced debugging support
      getDebugInfo() {
        return {
          chartId,
          contextConfigured,
          initializationError,
          layerCount: layers.length,
          layers: layers.map(l => ({
            id: l.id,
            markType: l.markType,
            zIndex: l.zIndex,
            visible: l.visible
          })),
          canvasDimensions: {
            width: canvas?.width,
            height: canvas?.height,
            cssWidth: canvas?.style.width,
            cssHeight: canvas?.style.height
          }
        };
      }
    });
  
    onMount(async () => {
      try {
        await initWebGPU();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ WebGPUCanvas ${chartId} mount failed:`, error);
        // Error is already stored in initializationError state
      }
    });
  
    onDestroy(() => {
      console.log(`🧹 Destroying WebGPUCanvas ${chartId}`);
      
      try {
        // Clean up chart registration
        if (chartControl) {
          chartControl.remove();
          chartControl = null;
        }
        
        // Context cleanup happens automatically when canvas is removed
        contextConfigured = false;
        
        // Clear references
        gpuCoordinator = null;
        context = null;
        
        console.log(`✅ WebGPUCanvas ${chartId} destroyed successfully`);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Error during WebGPUCanvas ${chartId} destruction:`, error);
      }
    });
  
    // Reactive effect for dimension changes with proper debouncing
    let resizeTimeoutId = null;
    
    $effect(() => {
      // Only react to meaningful dimension changes
      if (width > 0 && height > 0 && contextConfigured) {
        // Debounce rapid resize events
        if (resizeTimeoutId) {
          clearTimeout(resizeTimeoutId);
        }
        
        resizeTimeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
          setupCanvas();
          resizeTimeoutId = null;
        }, 8); // ~120fps throttling
      }
    });
  
    // Cleanup resize timeout on destroy
    onDestroy(() => {
      if (resizeTimeoutId) {
        clearTimeout(resizeTimeoutId);
      }
    });
  </script>
  
  {#if initializationError}
    <div class="webgpu-error">
      <p>WebGPU initialization failed for chart {chartId}:</p>
      <p class="error-message">{initializationError}</p>
      <p class="error-help">
        Please ensure your browser supports WebGPU and try refreshing the page.
      </p>
    </div>
  {:else}
    <canvas bind:this={canvas}></canvas>
    {#if contextConfigured}
      {@render canvasComponents?.()}
    {:else}
      <div class="webgpu-loading">
        <p>Initializing WebGPU context for chart {chartId}...</p>
      </div>
    {/if}
  {/if}
  
  <style>
    canvas {
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
    }
  
    .webgpu-error,
    .webgpu-loading {
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      justify-content: center;
      align-items: center;
      background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
      border: 1px dashed #ccc;
      font-size: 12px;
      color: #666;
      z-index: 1000;
    }
  
    .webgpu-error {
      background-color: rgba(255, 240, 240, 0.9);
      border-color: #ffcccb;
      color: #d32f2f;
    }
  
    .error-message {

      padding: 4px 8px;

    }

      background-color: rgba(240, 248, 255, 0.9);
      border-color: #bbdefb;
      color: #1976d2;
    }

<src/lib/components/rendering/Dots copy 2.svelte>
  <script>
    import { getContext, onMount, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
    import { getScaleState } from '$lib/scales';
    import { getGPUContext } from '$lib/gpu';
  
    let { 
      data, // Arrow Table
      dataVersion,
      r = 3,
      fill = 'blue',
      alpha = .5,
      zIndex = 0,
    } = $props();
  
    let colorScale = $derived(getScaleState('color', 'ordinal'));
    
    const c = getContext('chart');
    const webGPUContext = getContext('webgpu-canvas');
    const gpuCoordinator = getGPUContext();
  
    // Enhanced resource tracking for proper cleanup
    let ownedResources = {
      pipeline: false,
      shader: false,
      buffers: [],
      layerControl: null
    };
  
    // WebGPU resources
    let pipeline;
    let bindGroup;
    let resourcesCreated = false;
    let initializationError = $state(null);
  
    // Separate buffers for Arrow columns
    let xBuffer;
    let yBuffer;
    let radiusBuffer;
    let alphaBuffer;
    let colorBuffer;
    let scaleParamsBuffer;
    let resolutionBuffer;
    let dprBuffer;
    let instanceCount = 0;
    
    let quadGeometryBuffer;
    const quadVertexCount = 6;
  
    // Derived reactive state
    let xDomain = $derived(c.xScale?.domain() ?? []);
    let yDomain = $derived(c.yScale?.domain() ?? []);
    let xRange = $derived(c.xScale?.range() ?? []);
    let yRange = $derived(c.yScale?.range() ?? []);
  
    const devicePixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  
    // Enhanced shader with better structure and comments
    const shaderCode = `
      struct VertexOutput {
        @builtin(position) position: vec4<f32>,
        @location(0) color: vec4<f32>,
        @location(1) uv: vec2<f32>,
        @location(2) radius: f32,
        @location(3) alpha: f32,
      };
  
      struct ScaleParams {
        domainMin: vec2<f32>,
        domainMax: vec2<f32>,
        rangeMin: vec2<f32>,
        rangeMax: vec2<f32>,
      };
  
      @group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> resolution: vec2<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> xValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> yValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read> radiusValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(4) var<storage, read> alphaValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(5) var<storage, read> colors: array<vec4<f32>>;
      @group(0) @binding(6) var<uniform> scaleParams: ScaleParams;
      @group(0) @binding(7) var<uniform> devicePixelRatio: f32;
      
      fn scaleValue(value: f32, domainMin: f32, domainMax: f32, rangeMin: f32, rangeMax: f32) -> f32 {
        let normalized = (value - domainMin) / (domainMax - domainMin);
        return rangeMin + normalized * (rangeMax - rangeMin);
      }
      
      @vertex
      fn vertexMain(
        @location(0) quadPosition: vec2<f32>,
        @builtin(instance_index) instanceIdx: u32,
      ) -> VertexOutput {
        // Get values from separate buffers
        let x = xValues[instanceIdx];
        let y = yValues[instanceIdx];
        let radius = radiusValues[instanceIdx];
        let alpha = alphaValues[instanceIdx];
        let color = colors[instanceIdx];
        
        // Apply scales on GPU
        let scaledX = scaleValue(x, scaleParams.domainMin.x, scaleParams.domainMax.x, 
                                 scaleParams.rangeMin.x, scaleParams.rangeMax.x);
        let scaledY = scaleValue(y, scaleParams.domainMin.y, scaleParams.domainMax.y, 
                                 scaleParams.rangeMin.y, scaleParams.rangeMax.y);
        
        let displayRadius = radius * devicePixelRatio;
        let radiusWithPadding = displayRadius * 1.2;
        let worldPos = vec2<f32>(scaledX * devicePixelRatio, scaledY * devicePixelRatio) + quadPosition * radiusWithPadding;
        let normalizedPos = (worldPos / resolution) * 2.0 - 1.0;
        
        var output: VertexOutput;
        output.position = vec4<f32>(normalizedPos.x, -normalizedPos.y, 0.0, 1.0);
        output.color = color;
        output.uv = quadPosition;
        output.radius = displayRadius;
        output.alpha = alpha;
        return output;
      }
  
      @fragment
      fn fragmentMain(input: VertexOutput) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
        let dist = length(input.uv);
        let sdf = dist - 1.0;
        let pixelSize = devicePixelRatio / (input.radius * 2.0);
        let alpha = 1.0 - smoothstep(-pixelSize, pixelSize, sdf);
        let finalAlpha = input.color.a * alpha * input.alpha;
        return vec4<f32>(input.color.rgb, finalAlpha);
      }
    `;
  
    function createQuadGeometry() {
      return new Float32Array([
        -1, -1,  // bottom-left
         1, -1,  // bottom-right
        -1,  1,  // top-left
         1, -1,  // bottom-right
         1,  1,  // top-right
        -1,  1,  // top-left
      ]);
    }
  
    // Enhanced resource creation with proper sharing and error handling
    async function createGPUResources(device) {
      try {
        console.log('🔧 Creating GPU resources for Dots component');
        
        if (!gpuCoordinator) {
          throw new Error('GPU coordinator not available');
        }
  
        // Create or get shared shader with reference counting
        const shaderModule = await gpuCoordinator.getOrCreateSharedShader('dots', shaderCode);
        ownedResources.shader = true;
  
        // Create or get shared pipeline with proper lifecycle
        pipeline = await gpuCoordinator.getOrCreateSharedPipeline('dots', async () => {
          const msaaSampleCount = webGPUContext.getMsaaSampleCount();
  
          return device.createRenderPipeline({
            layout: 'auto',
            vertex: {
              module: shaderModule,
              entryPoint: 'vertexMain',
              buffers: [{
                arrayStride: 2 * 4,
                attributes: [{
                  shaderLocation: 0,
                  offset: 0,
                  format: 'float32x2',
                }],
              }],
            },
            fragment: {
              module: shaderModule,
              entryPoint: 'fragmentMain',
              targets: [{
                format: webGPUContext.getPresentationFormat(),
                blend: {
                  color: {
                    srcFactor: 'src-alpha',
                    dstFactor: 'one-minus-src-alpha',
                    operation: 'add',
                  },
                  alpha: {
                    srcFactor: 'one',
                    dstFactor: 'one-minus-src-alpha',
                    operation: 'add',
                  },
                },
              }],
            },
            primitive: { topology: 'triangle-list' },
            multisample: { count: msaaSampleCount > 1 ? msaaSampleCount : 1 },
          });
        });
        ownedResources.pipeline = true;
  
        // Create quad geometry buffer
        const quadVertices = createQuadGeometry();
        quadGeometryBuffer = device.createBuffer({
          size: quadVertices.byteLength,
          usage: GPUBufferUsage.VERTEX | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
        });
        device.queue.writeBuffer(quadGeometryBuffer, 0, quadVertices);
        ownedResources.buffers.push(quadGeometryBuffer);
  
        // Create chart-specific uniform buffers
        resolutionBuffer = device.createBuffer({
          size: 8,
          usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
        });
        ownedResources.buffers.push(resolutionBuffer);
  
        // Use shared DPR buffer if available, create own if needed
        dprBuffer = gpuCoordinator?.getSharedUniformBuffer('devicePixelRatio');
        
        if (!dprBuffer) {
          console.warn('🔶 Creating own DPR buffer - coordinator should provide this');
          dprBuffer = device.createBuffer({
            size: 4,
            usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
          });
          device.queue.writeBuffer(dprBuffer, 0, new Float32Array([devicePixelRatio]));
          ownedResources.buffers.push(dprBuffer);
        }
  
        // Update initial values
        const { width, height } = webGPUContext.getCanvasDimensions();
        device.queue.writeBuffer(resolutionBuffer, 0, new Float32Array([width, height]));
  
        resourcesCreated = true;
        console.log('✅ GPU resources created successfully for Dots component');
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to create GPU resources for Dots component:', error);
        initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Enhanced buffer management with smart sizing and reuse
    function createOrUpdateBuffer(existingBuffer, data, usage = GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST) {
      const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
      if (!device) {
        throw new Error('No WebGPU device available');
      }
      
      const requiredSize = data.byteLength;
      
      // Only recreate if actually needed, with smart sizing strategy
      if (!existingBuffer || existingBuffer.size < requiredSize) {
        if (existingBuffer) {
          // Remove from tracking before destroying
          const index = ownedResources.buffers.indexOf(existingBuffer);
          if (index !== -1) {
            ownedResources.buffers.splice(index, 1);
          }
          existingBuffer.destroy();
        }
  
        // Define a memory block size to prevent thrashing with tiny allocations.
        const MEMORY_BLOCK_SIZE = 256; // 256 bytes is a reasonable default
        // Calculate the desired size with 50% headroom for growth.
        const desiredSize = Math.ceil(requiredSize * 1.5);
        // Round up the desired size to the next multiple of MEMORY_BLOCK_SIZE.
        // This ensures we grow in sensible steps without over-allocating for small plots.
        const roundedUpSize = Math.ceil(desiredSize / MEMORY_BLOCK_SIZE) * MEMORY_BLOCK_SIZE;
        // Final buffer size must be at least the required size, and padded to a multiple of 4.
        let bufferSize = Math.max(requiredSize, roundedUpSize);
        bufferSize = (bufferSize + 3) & ~3; // Ensure 4-byte alignment
  
        const newBuffer = device.createBuffer({
          size: bufferSize,
          usage,
        });
        
        ownedResources.buffers.push(newBuffer);
        console.debug(`📊 Created buffer of size ${bufferSize} bytes (required: ${requiredSize})`);
        return newBuffer;
      }
      
      return existingBuffer;
    }
  
    // Enhanced instance data management with better error handling
    function updateInstanceData(device, arrowTable) {
      try {
        if (!arrowTable || arrowTable.numRows === 0 || !resourcesCreated) {
          console.debug('⚠️ Skipping instance data update - no data or resources not ready');
          return;
        }
  
        const startTime = performance.now();
        instanceCount = arrowTable.numRows;
  
        console.debug(`📊 Updating instance data for ${instanceCount} dots`);
  
        // Get Arrow columns with error checking
        const xColumn = arrowTable.getChild('x');
        const yColumn = arrowTable.getChild('y');
        const colorColumn = arrowTable.getChild('color');
  
        if (!xColumn || !yColumn) {
          throw new Error('Required x and y columns not found in Arrow table');
        }
  
        // Convert data with type safety
        const xData = new Float32Array(xColumn.toArray());
        const yData = new Float32Array(yColumn.toArray());
  
        // Update position buffers
        xBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(xBuffer, xData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(xBuffer, 0, xData);
  
        yBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(yBuffer, yData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(yBuffer, 0, yData);
  
        // Create radius and alpha data
        const radiusData = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
        const alphaData = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
        
        if (typeof r === 'function' || typeof alpha === 'function') {
          for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
            radiusData[i] = typeof r === 'function' ? r({x: xData[i], y: yData[i]}) : r;
            alphaData[i] = typeof alpha === 'function' ? alpha({x: xData[i], y: yData[i]}) : alpha;
          }
        } else {
          radiusData.fill(r);
          alphaData.fill(alpha);
        }
  
        // Update attribute buffers
        radiusBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(radiusBuffer, radiusData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(radiusBuffer, 0, radiusData);
  
        alphaBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(alphaBuffer, alphaData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(alphaBuffer, 0, alphaData);
  
        // Handle colors with error checking
        const colorData = new Float32Array(instanceCount * 4);
        
        if (colorColumn) {
          const colorArray = colorColumn.toArray();
          for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
            const fillColor = colorScale(colorArray[i]);
            const rgba = parseColor(fillColor);
            colorData.set(rgba, i * 4);
          }
        } else {
          // Fallback to single color
          const rgba = parseColor(fill);
          for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
            colorData.set(rgba, i * 4);
          }
        }
  
        colorBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(colorBuffer, colorData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(colorBuffer, 0, colorData);
  
        // Create scale parameters buffer if needed
        if (!scaleParamsBuffer) {
          scaleParamsBuffer = device.createBuffer({
            size: 32,
            usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
          });
          ownedResources.buffers.push(scaleParamsBuffer);
        }
        
        updateScaleParameters(device);
  
        // Create bind group after all buffers are ready
        if (!bindGroup) {
          createBindGroup(device);
        }
  
        const updateTime = performance.now() - startTime;
        console.debug(`✅ Instance data updated in ${updateTime.toFixed(2)}ms`);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to update instance data:', error);
        initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Enhanced bind group creation with comprehensive validation
    function createBindGroup(device) {
      try {
        // Comprehensive validation of all required resources
        const requiredBuffers = {
          resolutionBuffer,
          xBuffer,
          yBuffer,
          radiusBuffer,
          alphaBuffer,
          colorBuffer,
          scaleParamsBuffer,
          dprBuffer
        };
  
        const missingBuffers = Object.entries(requiredBuffers)
          .filter(([name, buffer]) => !buffer)
          .map(([name]) => name);
  
        if (missingBuffers.length > 0 || !pipeline) {
          console.warn(`⚠️ Cannot create bind group - missing resources: ${missingBuffers.join(', ')}${!pipeline ? ', pipeline' : ''}`);
          return;
        }
  
        bindGroup = device.createBindGroup({
          layout: pipeline.getBindGroupLayout(0),
          entries: [
            { binding: 0, resource: { buffer: resolutionBuffer } },
            { binding: 1, resource: { buffer: xBuffer } },
            { binding: 2, resource: { buffer: yBuffer } },
            { binding: 3, resource: { buffer: radiusBuffer } },
            { binding: 4, resource: { buffer: alphaBuffer } },
            { binding: 5, resource: { buffer: colorBuffer } },
            { binding: 6, resource: { buffer: scaleParamsBuffer } },
            { binding: 7, resource: { buffer: dprBuffer } },
          ],
        });
  
        console.debug('✅ Bind group created successfully');
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to create bind group:', error);
        initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Enhanced scale parameter updates
    function updateScaleParameters(device) {
      if (!scaleParamsBuffer) {
        console.warn('⚠️ Cannot update scale parameters - buffer not available');
        return;
      }
  
      try {
        const scaleParams = new Float32Array(8);
        scaleParams[0] = xDomain[0] ?? 0;
        scaleParams[1] = yDomain[0] ?? 0;
        scaleParams[2] = xDomain[1] ?? 1;
        scaleParams[3] = yDomain[1] ?? 1;
        scaleParams[4] = xRange[0] ?? 0;
        scaleParams[5] = yRange[0] ?? 0;
        scaleParams[6] = xRange[1] ?? 1;
        scaleParams[7] = yRange[1] ?? 1;
  
        device.queue.writeBuffer(scaleParamsBuffer, 0, scaleParams);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to update scale parameters:', error);
      }
    }
  
    // Enhanced draw function with comprehensive error handling
    function draw(passEncoder, device, presentationFormat) {
      try {
        if (!pipeline || !bindGroup || instanceCount === 0) {
          console.debug('⚠️ Skipping draw - resources not ready or no instances');
          return;
        }
  
        passEncoder.setPipeline(pipeline);
        passEncoder.setBindGroup(0, bindGroup);
        passEncoder.setVertexBuffer(0, quadGeometryBuffer);
        passEncoder.draw(quadVertexCount, instanceCount);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Draw operation failed:', error);
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Enhanced color parsing with better error handling
    function parseColor(color) {
      try {
        if (typeof color === 'string' && color.startsWith('#')) {
          const r = parseInt(color.slice(1, 3), 16) / 255;
          const g = parseInt(color.slice(3, 5), 16) / 255;
          const b = parseInt(color.slice(5, 7), 16) / 255;
          return [r, g, b, 1.0];
        }
        // Add support for other color formats if needed
        console.warn(`⚠️ Unrecognized color format: ${color}, using default blue`);
        return [0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0];
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Color parsing failed:', error);
        return [0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0];
      }
    }
  
    // Enhanced initialization with comprehensive error handling
    async function initialize() {
      try {
        console.log('🚀 Initializing Dots component');
        
        const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
        if (!device) {
          throw new Error('No WebGPU device available from context');
        }
  
        await createGPUResources(device);
        updateInstanceData(device, data);
        
        // Register layer with enhanced metadata
        ownedResources.layerControl = webGPUContext.registerLayer({
          zIndex,
          draw,
          visible: true,
          markType: 'dots',
          componentId: `dots-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 6)}`
        });
  
        console.log('✅ Dots component initialized successfully');
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Dots component initialization failed:', error);
        initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Wait for WebGPU context with better error handling
    async function waitForWebGPU() {
      let attempts = 0;
      const maxAttempts = 100;
      
      while (!webGPUContext.isInitialized() && attempts < maxAttempts) {
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
        attempts++;
      }
      
      if (!webGPUContext.isInitialized()) {
        throw new Error(`WebGPU context failed to initialize within ${maxAttempts * 50}ms`);
      }
      
      await initialize();
    }
  
    onMount(async () => {
      try {
        await waitForWebGPU();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Dots component mount failed:', error);
        initializationError = error.message;
      }
    });
  
    // Comprehensive cleanup with resource tracking
    onDestroy(() => {
      console.log('🧹 Cleaning up Dots component resources');
      
      try {
        // Clean up chart-specific buffers
        ownedResources.buffers.forEach(buffer => {
          if (buffer && !buffer.destroyed) {
            buffer.destroy();
          }
        });
        ownedResources.buffers = [];
  
        // Release shared resource references
        if (ownedResources.pipeline && gpuCoordinator) {
          gpuCoordinator.releaseSharedPipeline('dots');
        }
        
        if (ownedResources.shader && gpuCoordinator) {
          gpuCoordinator.releaseSharedShader('dots');
        }
  
        // Release shared uniform buffer reference
        if (dprBuffer && !ownedResources.buffers.includes(dprBuffer) && gpuCoordinator) {
          gpuCoordinator.releaseSharedUniformBuffer('devicePixelRatio');
        }
  
        // Clean up layer registration
        if (ownedResources.layerControl) {
          ownedResources.layerControl.remove();
        }
  
        console.log('✅ Dots component cleanup complete');
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Error during Dots component cleanup:', error);
      }
    });
  
    // Enhanced reactive effect for data updates
    $effect(() => {
      void dataVersion;
      
      untrack(() => {
        const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
        if (device && ownedResources.layerControl && resourcesCreated && !initializationError) {
          try {
            updateInstanceData(device, data);
            ownedResources.layerControl.invalidate();
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('❌ Data update effect failed:', error);
            initializationError = error.message;
          }
        }
      });
    });
  
    // Enhanced reactive effect for scale changes
    $effect(() => {
      void xDomain, yDomain, xRange, yRange;
      
      const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
      if (device && ownedResources.layerControl && resourcesCreated && scaleParamsBuffer && !initializationError) {
        try {
          updateScaleParameters(device);
          ownedResources.layerControl.invalidate();
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('❌ Scale update effect failed:', error);
        }
      }
    });
  
    // Enhanced reactive effect for resolution changes
    $effect(() => {
      const { width, height } = webGPUContext.getCanvasDimensions();
      const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
      
      if (resolutionBuffer && device && ownedResources.layerControl && resourcesCreated && !initializationError) {
        try {
          device.queue.writeBuffer(resolutionBuffer, 0, new Float32Array([width, height]));
          // Note: Don't invalidate for resolution changes to avoid unnecessary redraws
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('❌ Resolution update effect failed:', error);
        }
      }
    });
  </script>
  
  {#if initializationError}
    <div class="dots-error">
      <p>Dots component failed to initialize:</p>
      <p class="error-message">{initializationError}</p>
    </div>
  {/if}
