</src/lib/components/rendering/Dots copy 2.svelte>

<src/lib/components/rendering/BaseGPURenderer.svelte.js>
  // src/lib/components/rendering/BaseGPURenderer.svelte.js
  import { untrack } from 'svelte';
  
  /**
   * Base class for GPU-based renderers that handles common patterns:
   * - Resource lifecycle management
   * - Buffer creation and updates
   * - Scale parameter management with universal scale support
   * - GPU coordinator integration
   * - Error handling and cleanup
   */
  export class BaseGPURenderer {
    // Core properties
    #markType;
    #webGPUContext;
    #gpuCoordinator;
    #chartContext;
  
    // Resource tracking
    #ownedResources = {
      pipeline: false,
      shader: false,
      buffers: [],
      layerControl: null
    };
  
    // State management
    #resourcesCreated = $state(false);
    #initializationError = $state(null);
    #instanceCount = $state(0);
  
    // GPU resources
    #pipeline = $state(null);
    #bindGroup = $state(null);
    #scaleParamsBuffer = $state(null);
    #resolutionBuffer = $state(null);
    #dprBuffer = $state(null);
  
    // Configuration
    #zIndex;
    #devicePixelRatio;
    #xScaleType;
    #yScaleType;
  
    constructor(markType, webGPUContext, gpuCoordinator, chartContext, options = {}) {
      this.#markType = markType;
      this.#webGPUContext = webGPUContext;
      this.#gpuCoordinator = gpuCoordinator;
      this.#chartContext = chartContext;
      this.#zIndex = options.zIndex || 0;
      this.#devicePixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
      
      // Store explicit scale types passed from mark components
      this.#xScaleType = options.xScaleType || 'linear';
      this.#yScaleType = options.yScaleType || 'linear';
    }
  
    // ===== STATIC METHODS FOR CENTRALIZED SCALE SUPPORT =====
  
    /**
     * Get standardized scale shader fragment that all components can use
     * This contains the struct definition and universal scale functions
     */
    static getScaleShaderFragment() {
      return `
        struct ScaleParams {
          domainMin: vec2<f32>,       // [0-1] Domain minimums
          domainMax: vec2<f32>,       // [2-3] Domain maximums
          rangeMin: vec2<f32>,        // [4-5] Range minimums
          rangeMax: vec2<f32>,        // [6-7] Range maximums
          scaleTypes: vec2<f32>,      // [8-9] Scale type constants (0=linear, 1=band, 3=identity)
          bandwidth: vec2<f32>,       // [10-11] Band scale bandwidths
          padding: vec2<f32>,         // [12-13] Band scale padding
          categoryCount: vec2<f32>,   // [14-15] Number of categories for band scales
        };
  
        // Universal scaling function that handles all scale types
        fn scaleValue(
          value: f32, 
          scaleType: f32,
          domainMin: f32, 
          domainMax: f32, 
          rangeMin: f32, 
          rangeMax: f32,
          bandwidth: f32,
          categoryCount: f32
        ) -> f32 {
          if (scaleType == 0.0) {
            // Linear scale
            let normalized = (value - domainMin) / (domainMax - domainMin);
            return rangeMin + normalized * (rangeMax - rangeMin);
            
          } else if (scaleType == 1.0) {
            // Band scale: value is category index, position at center of band
            let categoryIndex = floor(value);
            let totalRange = rangeMax - rangeMin;
            let step = totalRange / categoryCount;
            
            // Position at center of the band
            return rangeMin + categoryIndex * step + bandwidth * 0.5;
            
          } else if (scaleType == 2.0) {
            // Point scale: evenly distributed points
            let categoryIndex = floor(value);
            let totalRange = rangeMax - rangeMin;
            let step = totalRange / max(1.0, categoryCount - 1.0);
            
            return rangeMin + categoryIndex * step;
            
          } else if (scaleType == 3.0) {
            // Identity scale: pass through (for pixel coordinates)
            return value;
            
          } else if (scaleType == 4.0) {
            // Log scale (future enhancement)
            let logValue = log(value);
            let logMin = log(domainMin);
            let logMax = log(domainMax);
            let normalized = (logValue - logMin) / (logMax - logMin);
            return rangeMin + normalized * (rangeMax - rangeMin);
          }
          
          // Fallback to linear for unknown scale types
          let normalized = (value - domainMin) / (domainMax - domainMin);
          return rangeMin + normalized * (rangeMax - rangeMin);
        }
  
        // Convenience functions for X and Y axes - components just call these!
        fn scaleX(value: f32, scaleParams: ScaleParams) -> f32 {
          return scaleValue(
            value,
            scaleParams.scaleTypes.x,
            scaleParams.domainMin.x,
            scaleParams.domainMax.x,
            scaleParams.rangeMin.x,
            scaleParams.rangeMax.x,
            scaleParams.bandwidth.x,
            scaleParams.categoryCount.x
          );
        }
  
        fn scaleY(value: f32, scaleParams: ScaleParams) -> f32 {
          return scaleValue(
            value,
            scaleParams.scaleTypes.y,
            scaleParams.domainMin.y,
            scaleParams.domainMax.y,
            scaleParams.rangeMin.y,
            scaleParams.rangeMax.y,
            scaleParams.bandwidth.y,
            scaleParams.categoryCount.y
          );
        }
      `;
    }
  
    /**
     * Get standard binding constants that all components should use
     * This ensures consistent binding layout across all shaders
     */
    static getStandardBindings() {
      return {
        RESOLUTION: 0,
        // Data bindings 1-5 are component-specific
        SCALE_PARAMS: 6,
        DEVICE_PIXEL_RATIO: 7
      };
    }
  
    /**
     * Generate complete shader code by combining scale fragment with component code
     * This is the main function components use to get their complete shader
     */
    static generateShader(componentSpecificCode) {
      const scaleFragment = BaseGPURenderer.getScaleShaderFragment();
      
      return `
        ${scaleFragment}
        
        ${componentSpecificCode}
      `;
    }
  
    // ===== DATA PROCESSING HELPERS =====
  
    /**
     * Process data for GPU consumption based on scale types
     * @param {Array} data - Raw data array
     * @param {string} axis - 'x' or 'y'
     * @returns {Float32Array} Processed data ready for GPU
     */
    processDataForAxis(data, axis) {
      const scaleType = axis === 'x' ? this.#xScaleType : this.#yScaleType;
      const scaleState = axis === 'x' ? this.#chartContext.xScaleState : this.#chartContext.yScaleState;
  
      switch (scaleType) {
        case 'linear':
          case 'identity':
            // Pass through numeric values - GPU will handle scaling
          return new Float32Array(data.map(d => d ?? 0));
          
        case 'band':
        case 'point':
          // CPU: Convert strings to indices, GPU: Convert indices to positions
          return scaleState.processDataForGPU(data, this.#markType);
          // return this.#convertCategoriesToIndices(data, scale, axis);  TODO: remove old method, logic moved to scaleState where it belongs!
          
        case 'log':
          // Future: logarithmic scale support
          return new Float32Array(data.map(d => Math.log(Math.max(d || 1, 1))));
          
        case 'time':
          // Future: temporal scale support  
          return new Float32Array(data.map(d => d instanceof Date ? d.getTime() : d || 0));
          
        default:
          console.warn(`Unknown scale type: ${scaleType}, treating as linear`);
          return new Float32Array(data.map(d => d ?? 0));
      }
    }
  
    /**
     * Convert categorical data to indices for band/point scales
     * @param {Array} categoryData - Array of category strings
     * @param {Object} scale - D3 scale object
     * @param {string} axis - 'x' or 'y'
     * @returns {Float32Array} Array of indices
     */
    #convertCategoriesToIndices(categoryData, scale, axis) {
      console.log(categoryData)
      const domain = scale?.domain?.() || [];
      console.log(domain)
      
      // Create category → index mapping with performance optimization
      const categoryMap = new Map();
      domain.forEach((category, index) => {
        categoryMap.set(category, index);
      });
  
      // Track unmapped categories for debugging
      const unmappedCategories = new Set();
      
      const indices = new Float32Array(categoryData.map(category => {
        const index = categoryMap.get(category);
        if (index !== undefined) {
          return index;
        } else {
          unmappedCategories.add(category);
          return 0; // Default to first category
        }
      }));
  
      // Warn about unmapped categories (only in development)
      if (unmappedCategories.size > 0 && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
        console.warn(
          `🔶 ${this.#markType}: Unmapped categories for ${axis}-axis:`, 
          Array.from(unmappedCategories).slice(0, 5), // Limit output
          unmappedCategories.size > 5 ? `... and ${unmappedCategories.size - 5} more` : ''
        );
      }
  
      return indices;
    }
  
    // ===== PUBLIC GETTERS =====
  
    get xScaleType() { return this.#xScaleType; }
    get yScaleType() { return this.#yScaleType; }
  
    get resourcesCreated() { return this.#resourcesCreated; }
    get initializationError() { return this.#initializationError; }
    get instanceCount() { return this.#instanceCount; }
    get pipeline() { return this.#pipeline; }
    get bindGroup() { return this.#bindGroup; }
    get markType() { return this.#markType; }
  
    // ===== INITIALIZATION =====
  
    /**
     * Initialize the renderer with shape-specific configuration
     * @param {Object} config - Shape-specific configuration
     * @param {string} config.shaderCode - WGSL shader code (should be generated with generateShader)
     * @param {Array} config.vertexBufferLayout - Bind group layout configuration
     */
    async initialize(config) {
      try {
        console.log(`🔧 Initializing ${this.#markType} renderer`);
        
        const device = this.#webGPUContext.getDevice();
        if (!device) {
          throw new Error('No WebGPU device available from context');
        }
  
        await this.#createSharedResources(device, config);
        await this.#createCommonBuffers(device);
        
        this.#resourcesCreated = true;
        console.log(`✅ ${this.#markType} renderer initialized successfully`);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ ${this.#markType} renderer initialization failed:`, error);
        this.#initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    /**
     * Register this renderer as a layer with the WebGPU canvas
     * @param {Function} drawFunction - Shape-specific draw function
     */
    registerLayer(drawFunction) {
      try {
        this.#ownedResources.layerControl = this.#webGPUContext.registerLayer({
          zIndex: this.#zIndex,
          draw: drawFunction,
          visible: true,
          markType: this.#markType,
          componentId: `${this.#markType}-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 6)}`
        });
  
        console.log(`📋 ${this.#markType} layer registered successfully`);
        return this.#ownedResources.layerControl;
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ ${this.#markType} layer registration failed:`, error);
        this.#initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // ===== SHARED RESOURCE MANAGEMENT =====
  
    async #createSharedResources(device, config) {
      if (!this.#gpuCoordinator) {
        throw new Error('GPU coordinator not available');
      }
  
      // Create or get shared shader
      const shaderModule = await this.#gpuCoordinator.getOrCreateSharedShader(
        this.#markType, 
        config.shaderCode
      );
      this.#ownedResources.shader = true;
  
      // Create or get shared pipeline
      this.#pipeline = await this.#gpuCoordinator.getOrCreateSharedPipeline(
        this.#markType, 
        async () => {
          const msaaSampleCount = this.#webGPUContext.getMsaaSampleCount();
  
          return device.createRenderPipeline({
            layout: 'auto',
            vertex: {
              module: shaderModule,
              entryPoint: 'vertexMain',
              buffers: config.vertexBufferLayout || [],
            },
            fragment: {
              module: shaderModule,
              entryPoint: 'fragmentMain',
              targets: [{
                format: this.#webGPUContext.getPresentationFormat(),
                blend: {
                  color: {
                    srcFactor: 'src-alpha',
                    dstFactor: 'one-minus-src-alpha',
                    operation: 'add',
                  },
                  alpha: {
                    srcFactor: 'one',
                    dstFactor: 'one-minus-src-alpha',
                    operation: 'add',
                  },
                },
              }],
            },
            primitive: { topology: 'triangle-list' },
            multisample: { count: msaaSampleCount > 1 ? msaaSampleCount : 1 },
          });
        }
      );
      this.#ownedResources.pipeline = true;
    }
  
    async #createCommonBuffers(device) {
      // Create chart-specific uniform buffers
      this.#resolutionBuffer = device.createBuffer({
        size: 8, // vec2<f32>
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
      this.#ownedResources.buffers.push(this.#resolutionBuffer);
  
      this.#scaleParamsBuffer = device.createBuffer({
        size: 64, // 16 f32 values for enhanced scale parameters
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
      this.#ownedResources.buffers.push(this.#scaleParamsBuffer);
  
      // Use shared DPR buffer if available, create own if needed
      this.#dprBuffer = this.#gpuCoordinator?.getSharedUniformBuffer('devicePixelRatio');
      
      if (!this.#dprBuffer) {
        console.warn(`🔶 Creating own DPR buffer for ${this.#markType} - coordinator should provide this`);
        this.#dprBuffer = device.createBuffer({
          size: 4, // f32
          usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
        });
        device.queue.writeBuffer(this.#dprBuffer, 0, new Float32Array([this.#devicePixelRatio]));
        this.#ownedResources.buffers.push(this.#dprBuffer);
      }
  
      // Update initial values
      this.updateResolution();
      this.updateScaleParameters();
    }
  
    // ===== BUFFER MANAGEMENT =====
  
    /**
     * Create or update a buffer with smart sizing to prevent thrashing
     * @param {GPUBuffer} existingBuffer - Existing buffer to potentially reuse
     * @param {TypedArray} data - Data to store in buffer
     * @param {number} usage - GPU buffer usage flags
     * @returns {GPUBuffer} New or existing buffer
     */
    createOrUpdateBuffer(existingBuffer, data, usage = GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST) {
      const device = this.#webGPUContext.getDevice();
      if (!device) {
        throw new Error('No WebGPU device available');
      }
      
      const requiredSize = data.byteLength;
      
      // Only recreate if actually needed, with smart sizing strategy
      if (!existingBuffer || existingBuffer.size < requiredSize) {
        if (existingBuffer) {
          // Remove from tracking before destroying
          const index = this.#ownedResources.buffers.indexOf(existingBuffer);
          if (index !== -1) {
            this.#ownedResources.buffers.splice(index, 1);
          }
          existingBuffer.destroy();
        }
  
        // Smart sizing: grow in sensible chunks to prevent thrashing
        const MEMORY_BLOCK_SIZE = 256; // 256 bytes minimum block
        const desiredSize = Math.ceil(requiredSize * 1.5); // 50% headroom
        const roundedUpSize = Math.ceil(desiredSize / MEMORY_BLOCK_SIZE) * MEMORY_BLOCK_SIZE;
        let bufferSize = Math.max(requiredSize, roundedUpSize);
        bufferSize = (bufferSize + 3) & ~3; // Ensure 4-byte alignment
  
        const newBuffer = device.createBuffer({
          size: bufferSize,
          usage,
        });
        
        this.#ownedResources.buffers.push(newBuffer);
        console.debug(`📊 Created ${this.#markType} buffer of size ${bufferSize} bytes (required: ${requiredSize})`);
        return newBuffer;
      }
      
      return existingBuffer;
    }
  
    /**
     * Update instance count and trigger layer invalidation
     * @param {number} count - New instance count
     */
    updateInstanceCount(count) {
      this.#instanceCount = count;
      this.invalidateLayer();
    }
  
    // ===== ENHANCED SCALE INTEGRATION =====
  
    /**
     * Enhanced scale parameters update with complete scale type support
     * This is where all the magic happens for universal scale support!
     */
    updateScaleParameters() {
      if (!this.#scaleParamsBuffer || !this.#chartContext) {
        return;
      }
  
      try {
        const device = this.#webGPUContext.getDevice();
        if (!device) return;
  
        const xScale = this.#chartContext.xScale;
        const yScale = this.#chartContext.yScale;
        
        if (!xScale || !yScale) return;
  
        // Convert scale type strings to GPU constants
        const xScaleType = this.#getGPUScaleType(this.#xScaleType);
        const yScaleType = this.#getGPUScaleType(this.#yScaleType);
  
        // Enhanced scale parameters (16 floats) - matches GPU struct exactly
        const scaleParams = new Float32Array(16);
        
        // Positions 0-7: Core scale parameters (backward compatible)
        scaleParams[0] = this.#getDomainMin(xScale, this.#xScaleType);
        scaleParams[1] = this.#getDomainMin(yScale, this.#yScaleType);
        scaleParams[2] = this.#getDomainMax(xScale, this.#xScaleType);
        scaleParams[3] = this.#getDomainMax(yScale, this.#yScaleType);
        scaleParams[4] = this.#getRangeMin(xScale);
        scaleParams[5] = this.#getRangeMin(yScale);
        scaleParams[6] = this.#getRangeMax(xScale);
        scaleParams[7] = this.#getRangeMax(yScale);
  
        // Positions 8-15: Enhanced scale parameters
        scaleParams[8] = xScaleType; // X scale type constant
        scaleParams[9] = yScaleType; // Y scale type constant
        scaleParams[10] = this.#getBandwidth(xScale, this.#xScaleType); // X bandwidth
        scaleParams[11] = this.#getBandwidth(yScale, this.#yScaleType); // Y bandwidth
        scaleParams[12] = this.#getPadding(xScale, this.#xScaleType); // X padding
        scaleParams[13] = this.#getPadding(yScale, this.#yScaleType); // Y padding
        scaleParams[14] = this.#getCategoryCount(xScale, this.#xScaleType); // X category count
        scaleParams[15] = this.#getCategoryCount(yScale, this.#yScaleType); // Y category count
  
        device.queue.writeBuffer(this.#scaleParamsBuffer, 0, scaleParams);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Failed to update scale parameters for ${this.#markType}:`, error);
      }
    }
  
    // ===== SCALE TYPE HELPERS =====
  
    /**
     * Convert scale type string to GPU constant
     * @param {string} scaleType - Scale type ('linear', 'band', 'point', 'identity', 'log')
     * @returns {number} GPU scale type constant
     */
    #getGPUScaleType(scaleType) {
      switch (scaleType) {
        case 'linear': return 0;
        case 'band': return 1;
        case 'point': return 2;
        case 'identity': return 3;
        case 'log': return 4;
        case 'time': return 5;
        default:
          console.warn(`Unknown scale type: ${scaleType}, defaulting to linear`);
          return 0;
      }
    }
  
    /**
     * Get domain minimum for different scale types
     */
    #getDomainMin(scale, scaleType) {
      if (scaleType === 'identity') return 0.0; // Identity: pass-through
      if (scaleType === 'band' || scaleType === 'point') return 0.0; // Band/point: use indices
      if (scaleType === 'log') {
        const domain = scale?.domain?.() || [1, 10];
        return Math.log(Math.max(domain[0] || 1, 1));
      }
      const domain = scale?.domain?.() || [0, 1];
      return domain[0] ?? 0.0;
    }
  
    /**
     * Get domain maximum for different scale types
     */
    #getDomainMax(scale, scaleType) {
      if (scaleType === 'identity') return 1.0; // Identity: pass-through
      if (scaleType === 'band' || scaleType === 'point') {
        const domain = scale?.domain?.() || [];
        return Math.max(0, domain.length - 1); // Number of categories - 1
      }
      if (scaleType === 'log') {
        const domain = scale?.domain?.() || [1, 10];
        return Math.log(Math.max(domain[1] || 10, 1));
      }
      const domain = scale?.domain?.() || [0, 1];
      return domain[1] ?? 1.0;
    }
  
    /**
     * Get range minimum
     */
    #getRangeMin(scale) {
      const range = scale?.range?.() || [0, 1];
      return range[0] ?? 0.0;
    }
  
    /**
     * Get range maximum
     */
    #getRangeMax(scale) {
      const range = scale?.range?.() || [0, 1];
      return range[1] ?? 1.0;
    }
  
    /**
     * Get bandwidth for band scales
     */
    #getBandwidth(scale, scaleType) {
      if (scaleType === 'band') {
        return scale?.bandwidth?.() ?? 0.0;
      }
      return 0.0;
    }
  
    /**
     * Get padding for band scales
     */
    #getPadding(scale, scaleType) {
      if (scaleType === 'band') {
        return scale?.paddingInner?.() ?? 0.1;
      }
      return 0.0;
    }
  
    /**
     * Get category count for band/point scales
     */
    #getCategoryCount(scale, scaleType) {
      if (scaleType === 'band' || scaleType === 'point') {
        const domain = scale?.domain?.() || [];
        return domain.length;
      }
      return 0.0;
    }
  
    /**
     * Update resolution buffer with current canvas dimensions
     */
    updateResolution() {
      if (!this.#resolutionBuffer) return;
  
      try {
        const device = this.#webGPUContext.getDevice();
        if (!device) return;
  
        const { width, height } = this.#webGPUContext.getCanvasDimensions();
        device.queue.writeBuffer(this.#resolutionBuffer, 0, new Float32Array([width, height]));
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Failed to update resolution for ${this.#markType}:`, error);
      }
    }
  
    // ===== BIND GROUP MANAGEMENT =====
  
    /**
     * Create bind group with common bindings plus shape-specific ones
     * @param {Array} additionalBindings - Shape-specific bindings to add
     */
    createBindGroup(additionalBindings = []) {
      try {
        const device = this.#webGPUContext.getDevice();
        if (!device || !this.#pipeline) {
          console.warn(`⚠️ Cannot create bind group for ${this.#markType} - missing device or pipeline`);
          return;
        }
  
        // Common bindings that all shapes need (matches the binding constants)
        const commonBindings = [
          { binding: 0, resource: { buffer: this.#resolutionBuffer } },
          // Note: bindings 1-5 are typically reserved for shape-specific data buffers
          { binding: 6, resource: { buffer: this.#scaleParamsBuffer } },
          { binding: 7, resource: { buffer: this.#dprBuffer } },
        ];
  
        // Merge common and shape-specific bindings
        const allBindings = [...additionalBindings, ...commonBindings];
  
        this.#bindGroup = device.createBindGroup({
          layout: this.#pipeline.getBindGroupLayout(0),
          entries: allBindings,
        });
  
        console.debug(`✅ Bind group created for ${this.#markType}`);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Failed to create bind group for ${this.#markType}:`, error);
        this.#initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // ===== REACTIVE EFFECTS HELPERS =====
  
    /**
     * Helper method to update scales (called from component's $effect)
     * @param {Function} callback - Optional callback when scales change
     */
    handleScaleChange(callback) {
      if (this.#resourcesCreated && !this.#initializationError) {
        this.updateScaleParameters();
        if (callback) callback();
        this.invalidateLayer();
      }
    }
  
    /**
     * Helper method to update resolution (called from component's $effect)  
     * @param {Function} callback - Optional callback when resolution changes
     */
    handleResolutionChange(callback) {
      if (this.#resourcesCreated && !this.#initializationError) {
        this.updateResolution();
        if (callback) callback();
        // Note: Don't invalidate for resolution changes to avoid unnecessary redraws
      }
    }
  
    // ===== LAYER MANAGEMENT =====
  
    /**
     * Invalidate the layer to trigger a redraw
     */
    invalidateLayer() {
      this.#ownedResources.layerControl?.invalidate();
    }
  
    /**
     * Update layer properties
     * @param {Object} props - Properties to update
     */
    updateLayer(props) {
      this.#ownedResources.layerControl?.update(props);
    }
  
    // ===== UTILITIES =====
  
    /**
     * Parse color string to RGBA array
     * @param {string} color - Color string (hex, named, etc.)
     * @returns {Array<number>} RGBA values [0-1]
     */
    parseColor(color) {
      try {
        if (typeof color === 'string' && color.startsWith('#')) {
          const r = parseInt(color.slice(1, 3), 16) / 255;
          const g = parseInt(color.slice(3, 5), 16) / 255;
          const b = parseInt(color.slice(5, 7), 16) / 255;
          return [r, g, b, 1.0];
        }
        console.warn(`⚠️ Unrecognized color format: ${color}, using default blue`);
        return [0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0];
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Color parsing failed for ${this.#markType}:`, error);
        return [0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0];
      }
    }
  
    // ===== CLEANUP =====
  
    /**
     * Clean up all resources owned by this renderer
     */
    destroy() {
      console.log(`🧹 Cleaning up ${this.#markType} renderer resources`);
      
      try {
        // Clean up chart-specific buffers
        this.#ownedResources.buffers.forEach(buffer => {
          if (buffer && !buffer.destroyed) {
            buffer.destroy();
          }
        });
        this.#ownedResources.buffers = [];
  
        // Release shared resource references
        if (this.#ownedResources.pipeline && this.#gpuCoordinator) {
          this.#gpuCoordinator.releaseSharedPipeline(this.#markType);
        }
        
        if (this.#ownedResources.shader && this.#gpuCoordinator) {
          this.#gpuCoordinator.releaseSharedShader(this.#markType);
        }
  
        // Release shared uniform buffer reference
        if (this.#dprBuffer && !this.#ownedResources.buffers.includes(this.#dprBuffer) && this.#gpuCoordinator) {
          this.#gpuCoordinator.releaseSharedUniformBuffer('devicePixelRatio');
        }
  
        // Clean up layer registration
        if (this.#ownedResources.layerControl) {
          this.#ownedResources.layerControl.remove();
        }
  
        console.log(`✅ ${this.#markType} renderer cleanup complete`);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Error during ${this.#markType} renderer cleanup:`, error);
      }
    }
  
    // ===== DEBUG UTILITIES =====
  
    /**
     * Get debug information about the renderer state
     * @returns {Object} Debug information
     */
    getDebugInfo() {
      return {
        markType: this.#markType,
        resourcesCreated: this.#resourcesCreated,
        initializationError: this.#initializationError,
        instanceCount: this.#instanceCount,
        scaleTypes: {
          x: this.#xScaleType,
          y: this.#yScaleType
        },
        ownedResources: {
          pipeline: this.#ownedResources.pipeline,
          shader: this.#ownedResources.shader,
          bufferCount: this.#ownedResources.buffers.length,
          hasLayerControl: !!this.#ownedResources.layerControl
        },
        bufferSizes: this.#ownedResources.buffers.map(buffer => ({
          size: buffer.size,
          usage: buffer.usage
        }))
      };
    }
  }
</src/lib/components/rendering/BaseGPURenderer.svelte.js>

<src/lib/components/rendering/Canvas.svelte>
  <script>
    import { onMount, onDestroy, setContext, getContext } from "svelte";
  
    let {
      canvasComponents
    } = $props();
  
    const c = getContext('chart');
    let width = $derived(c.innerWidth)
    let height = $derived(c.innerHeight)
    let margin = $derived(c.margin)
  
    let canvas;
    let ctx;
    let rafId = null;
    let needsRedraw = false;
  
    // Layer management system
    const layers = [];
  
    function scaleCanvas(canvas, ctx, width, height, margin) {
      const devicePixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  
      canvas.width = width * devicePixelRatio;
      canvas.height = height * devicePixelRatio;
      canvas.style.width = width + 'px';
      canvas.style.height = height + 'px';
      canvas.style.transform = `translate(${margin.left}px, ${margin.top}px)`;
  
      ctx.scale(devicePixelRatio, devicePixelRatio);
      
      needsRedraw = true;
    }
  
    function update() {
      if (!ctx || !needsRedraw) return;
      
      // Clear the canvas
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
      
      // Draw all registered layers in order
      for (let i = 0; i < layers.length; i++) {
        const layer = layers[i];
        if (layer.visible !== false) {
          ctx.save();
          layer.draw(ctx);
          ctx.restore();
        }
      }
      
      needsRedraw = false;
      rafId = null;
    }
  
    function requestRedraw() {
      if (rafId !== null) return;
      needsRedraw = true;
      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(update);
    }
  
    // Canvas context that child components can use
    setContext('canvas', {
      // Register a layer that can contain multiple elements
      registerLayer(layer) {
        // Allow specifying a z-index for ordering
        const zIndex = layer.zIndex || 0;
        let insertIndex = layers.length;
        
        // Find the correct position to insert based on zIndex
        for (let i = 0; i < layers.length; i++) {
          if ((layers[i].zIndex || 0) > zIndex) {
            insertIndex = i;
            break;
          }
        }
        
        layers.splice(insertIndex, 0, layer);
        requestRedraw();
        
        // Return functions to interact with this layer
        return {
          // Update layer properties
          update(newProps) {
            Object.assign(layer, newProps);
            requestRedraw();
          },
          // Remove this layer
          remove() {
            const index = layers.indexOf(layer);
            if (index !== -1) {
              layers.splice(index, 1);
              requestRedraw();
            }
          },
          // Request a redraw (if layer content changed)
          invalidate() {
            requestRedraw();
          }
        };
      },
      
      // Expose canvas dimensions
      getCanvasDimensions() {
        return { width, height };
      },
      
      // Force a redraw
      invalidate() {
        requestRedraw();
      }
    });
  
    onMount(() => {
      ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', {
        willReadFrequently: false,  // Hint that we won't read pixel data often
        desynchronized: true        // Lower latency rendering
      });
      
      scaleCanvas(canvas, ctx, width, height, margin);
      requestRedraw();
    });
  
    onDestroy(() => {
      if (rafId !== null) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
      }
    });
  
    $effect(() => {
      if (canvas && ctx) {
        scaleCanvas(canvas, ctx, width, height, margin);
      }
    });
  </script>
  
  <canvas bind:this={canvas}></canvas>
  {@render canvasComponents?.()}
  
  
  <!-- <script>
    import { onMount, onDestroy, setContext, getContext } from "svelte";
  	let {
      canvasComponents
  	} = $props();
  
  	const c = getContext('chart');
    let width = $derived(c.innerWidth)
    let height = $derived(c.innerHeight)
    let margin = $derived(c.margin)
  
    const drawFunctions = [];
  
    let canvas; // state needed here?
    let ctx;
    let pendingInvalidation = false;
    let frameId;
  
    function scaleCanvas(canvas, ctx, width, height, margin) {
      const devicePixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  
      canvas.width = width * devicePixelRatio;
      canvas.height = height * devicePixelRatio;
      canvas.style.width = width + 'px';
      canvas.style.height = height + 'px';
      canvas.style.transform = `translate(${margin.left}px, ${margin.top}px)`;
  
      ctx.scale(devicePixelRatio, devicePixelRatio);
    }
  
    function update() {
      if (!ctx) return;
  
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
  
      drawFunctions.forEach((fn) => {
        ctx.save();
        fn(ctx);
        ctx.restore();
      });
  
      pendingInvalidation = false
    }
  
    onMount(() => {
      ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', {
        // alpha: false,            // Disable transparency if not needed
        // willReadFrequently: false, // Hint that we won't read pixel data often
        // desynchronized: true     // Lower latency rendering
      })
    })
  
    onDestroy(() => {
      if (frameId) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
      }
    })
  
    setContext('canvas', {
      register(fn) { 
        drawFunctions.push(fn);
      },
      deregister(fn) {
        drawFunctions.splice(drawFunctions.indexOf(fn), 1);
      },
      invalidate() {
        if (pendingInvalidation) return;
        pendingInvalidation = true;
        frameId = requestAnimationFrame(update);
      }
    })
  
    $effect(() => {
      if (canvas && ctx) scaleCanvas(canvas, ctx, width, height, margin)
    })
  </script>
  
  <canvas bind:this={canvas}></canvas>
  {@render canvasComponents?.()} -->
</src/lib/components/rendering/Canvas.svelte>

<src/lib/components/rendering/Dots.svelte>
  <script>
    import { getContext, onMount, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
    import { getScaleState } from '$lib/scales';
    import { BaseGPURenderer } from './BaseGPURenderer.svelte.js';
    import { getGPUContext } from '$lib/gpu';
  
    let { 
      data, // Arrow Table
      dataVersion,
      r = 3,
      fill = 'blue',
      alpha = .5,
      zIndex = 0,
    } = $props();
  
    let colorScale = $derived(getScaleState('color', 'ordinal'));
    
    const c = getContext('chart');
    const webGPUContext = getContext('webgpu-canvas');
    const gpuCoordinator = getGPUContext();
  
    // Use BaseGPURenderer for common functionality
    let renderer = $state(null);
    let initializationError = $state(null);
  
    // Dots-specific buffers (managed through base renderer)
    let xBuffer;
    let yBuffer;
    let radiusBuffer;
    let alphaBuffer;
    let colorBuffer;
    let quadGeometryBuffer;
    
    const quadVertexCount = 6;
  
    // Component-specific shader code - much simpler now!
    const dotsShaderCode = `
      struct VertexOutput {
        @builtin(position) position: vec4<f32>,
        @location(0) color: vec4<f32>,
        @location(1) uv: vec2<f32>,
        @location(2) radius: f32,
        @location(3) alpha: f32,
      };
  
      @group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> resolution: vec2<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> xValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> yValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read> radiusValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(4) var<storage, read> alphaValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(5) var<storage, read> colors: array<vec4<f32>>;
      @group(0) @binding(6) var<uniform> scaleParams: ScaleParams;
      @group(0) @binding(7) var<uniform> devicePixelRatio: f32;
      
      @vertex
      fn vertexMain(
        @location(0) quadPosition: vec2<f32>,
        @builtin(instance_index) instanceIdx: u32,
      ) -> VertexOutput {
        // Get values from separate buffers
        let x = xValues[instanceIdx];
        let y = yValues[instanceIdx];
        let radius = radiusValues[instanceIdx];
        let alpha = alphaValues[instanceIdx];
        let color = colors[instanceIdx];
        
        // Use centralized scale functions - works for any scale type!
        let scaledX = scaleX(x, scaleParams);
        let scaledY = scaleY(y, scaleParams);
        
        // Rest of dots-specific positioning logic stays the same
        let displayRadius = radius * devicePixelRatio;
        let radiusWithPadding = displayRadius * 1.2;
        let worldPos = vec2<f32>(scaledX * devicePixelRatio, scaledY * devicePixelRatio) + quadPosition * radiusWithPadding;
        let normalizedPos = (worldPos / resolution) * 2.0 - 1.0;
        
        var output: VertexOutput;
        output.position = vec4<f32>(normalizedPos.x, -normalizedPos.y, 0.0, 1.0);
        output.color = color;
        output.uv = quadPosition;
        output.radius = displayRadius;
        output.alpha = alpha;
        return output;
      }
  
      @fragment
      fn fragmentMain(input: VertexOutput) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
        let dist = length(input.uv);
        let sdf = dist - 1.0;
        let pixelSize = devicePixelRatio / (input.radius * 2.0);
        let alpha = 1.0 - smoothstep(-pixelSize, pixelSize, sdf);
        let finalAlpha = input.color.a * alpha * input.alpha;
        return vec4<f32>(input.color.rgb, finalAlpha);
      }
    `;
  
    // Generate complete shader with centralized scale support
    const shaderCode = BaseGPURenderer.generateShader(dotsShaderCode);
  
    // Dots-specific vertex buffer layout
    const vertexBufferLayout = [{
      arrayStride: 2 * 4, // 2 floats * 4 bytes
      attributes: [{
        shaderLocation: 0,
        offset: 0,
        format: 'float32x2',
      }],
    }];
  
    // Create quad geometry for dot rendering
    function createQuadGeometry() {
      return new Float32Array([
        -1, -1,  // bottom-left
         1, -1,  // bottom-right
        -1,  1,  // top-left
         1, -1,  // bottom-right
         1,  1,  // top-right
        -1,  1,  // top-left
      ]);
    }
  
    // Initialize dots-specific geometry
    async function initializeDotsGeometry() {
      const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
      if (!device) {
        throw new Error('No WebGPU device available');
      }
  
      // Create quad geometry buffer
      const quadVertices = createQuadGeometry();
      quadGeometryBuffer = device.createBuffer({
        size: quadVertices.byteLength,
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.VERTEX | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
      device.queue.writeBuffer(quadGeometryBuffer, 0, quadVertices);
    }
  
    // Update instance data using base renderer's buffer management
    function updateInstanceData(arrowTable) {
      try {
        if (!arrowTable || arrowTable.numRows === 0 || !renderer?.resourcesCreated) {
          console.debug('⚠️ Skipping dots instance data update - no data or resources not ready');
          return;
        }
  
        const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
        if (!device) {
          throw new Error('No WebGPU device available');
        }
  
        const startTime = performance.now();
        const instanceCount = arrowTable.numRows;
  
        console.debug(`📊 Updating dots instance data for ${instanceCount} dots`);
  
        // Get Arrow columns with error checking
        const xColumn = arrowTable.getChild('x');
        const yColumn = arrowTable.getChild('y');
        const colorColumn = arrowTable.getChild('color');
  
        if (!xColumn || !yColumn) {
          throw new Error('Required x and y columns not found in Arrow table');
        }
  
        // Process data through BaseGPURenderer for proper scale handling
        const xValues = xColumn.toArray();
        const yValues = yColumn.toArray();
        
        // Use BaseGPURenderer's data processing for scale-aware conversion
        const xData = renderer.processDataForAxis(xValues, 'x');
        const yData = renderer.processDataForAxis(yValues, 'y');
  
        // Update position buffers using base renderer's smart buffer management
        xBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(xBuffer, xData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(xBuffer, 0, xData);
  
        yBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(yBuffer, yData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(yBuffer, 0, yData);
  
        // Create radius and alpha data
        const radiusData = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
        const alphaData = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
        
        if (typeof r === 'function' || typeof alpha === 'function') {
          for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
            radiusData[i] = typeof r === 'function' ? r({x: xValues[i], y: yValues[i]}) : r;
            alphaData[i] = typeof alpha === 'function' ? alpha({x: xValues[i], y: yValues[i]}) : alpha;
          }
        } else {
          radiusData.fill(r);
          alphaData.fill(alpha);
        }
  
        // Update attribute buffers
        radiusBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(radiusBuffer, radiusData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(radiusBuffer, 0, radiusData);
  
        alphaBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(alphaBuffer, alphaData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(alphaBuffer, 0, alphaData);
  
        // Handle colors with error checking
        const colorData = new Float32Array(instanceCount * 4);
        
        if (colorColumn) {
          const colorArray = colorColumn.toArray();
          for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
            const fillColor = colorScale(colorArray[i]);
            const rgba = renderer.parseColor(fillColor);
            colorData.set(rgba, i * 4);
          }
        } else {
          // Fallback to single color
          const rgba = renderer.parseColor(fill);
          for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
            colorData.set(rgba, i * 4);
          }
        }
  
        colorBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(colorBuffer, colorData);
        device.queue.writeBuffer(colorBuffer, 0, colorData);
  
        // Create bind group after all buffers are ready
        createDotsBindGroup();
  
        // Update instance count
        renderer.updateInstanceCount(instanceCount);
  
        const updateTime = performance.now() - startTime;
        console.debug(`✅ Dots instance data updated in ${updateTime.toFixed(2)}ms`);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to update dots instance data:', error);
        initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Create dots-specific bind group
    function createDotsBindGroup() {
      // Validate all buffers exist
      if (!xBuffer || !yBuffer || !radiusBuffer || !alphaBuffer || !colorBuffer) {
        console.warn('⚠️ Cannot create dots bind group - missing data buffers');
        return;
      }
  
      // Shape-specific bindings (bindings 1-5)
      const dotsBindings = [
        { binding: 1, resource: { buffer: xBuffer } },
        { binding: 2, resource: { buffer: yBuffer } },
        { binding: 3, resource: { buffer: radiusBuffer } },
        { binding: 4, resource: { buffer: alphaBuffer } },
        { binding: 5, resource: { buffer: colorBuffer } },
      ];
  
      // Use base renderer to create bind group (includes common bindings)
      renderer.createBindGroup(dotsBindings);
    }
  
    // Dots-specific draw function
    function drawDots(passEncoder) {
      try {
        if (!renderer?.pipeline || !renderer?.bindGroup || renderer.instanceCount === 0) {
          console.debug('⚠️ Skipping dots draw - resources not ready or no instances');
          return;
        }
  
        passEncoder.setPipeline(renderer.pipeline);
        passEncoder.setBindGroup(0, renderer.bindGroup);
        passEncoder.setVertexBuffer(0, quadGeometryBuffer);
        passEncoder.draw(quadVertexCount, renderer.instanceCount);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Dots draw operation failed:', error);
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Wait for WebGPU context with better error handling
    async function waitForWebGPU() {
      let attempts = 0;
      const maxAttempts = 100;
      
      while (!webGPUContext.isInitialized() && attempts < maxAttempts) {
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
        attempts++;
      }
      
      if (!webGPUContext.isInitialized()) {
        throw new Error(`WebGPU context failed to initialize within ${maxAttempts * 50}ms`);
      }
      
      await initializeDots();
    }
  
    // Initialize dots renderer
    async function initializeDots() {
      try {
        console.log('🚀 Initializing Dots component with centralized scale support');
  
        // Check GPU coordinator is available
        if (!gpuCoordinator) {
          throw new Error('GPU Coordinator context missing. Make sure setCoordinators() was called.');
        }
  
        // Create base renderer with scale type information
        renderer = new BaseGPURenderer('dots', webGPUContext, gpuCoordinator, c, { 
          zIndex,
          xScaleType: c.scaleTypes?.x || 'linear',
          yScaleType: c.scaleTypes?.y || 'linear'
        });
  
        // Initialize with dots-specific configuration using centralized shader
        await renderer.initialize({
          shaderCode, // This now includes universal scale support!
          vertexBufferLayout,
        });
  
        // Initialize dots-specific geometry
        await initializeDotsGeometry();
  
        // Update instance data if available
        if (data) {
          updateInstanceData(data);
        }
        
        // Register layer with base renderer
        renderer.registerLayer(drawDots);
  
        console.log('✅ Dots component initialized successfully with centralized scales');
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Dots component initialization failed:', error);
        initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    onMount(async () => {
      try {
        await waitForWebGPU();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Dots component mount failed:', error);
        initializationError = error.message;
      }
    });
  
    onDestroy(() => {
      console.log('🧹 Cleaning up Dots component');
      
      try {
        // Clean up dots-specific resources
        if (quadGeometryBuffer && !quadGeometryBuffer.destroyed) {
          quadGeometryBuffer.destroy();
        }
  
        // Base renderer handles all other cleanup
        if (renderer) {
          renderer.destroy();
        }
  
        console.log('✅ Dots component cleanup complete');
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Error during Dots component cleanup:', error);
      }
    });
  
    // Enhanced reactive effect for data updates
    $effect(() => {
      void dataVersion;
      
      untrack(() => {
        if (renderer?.resourcesCreated && !initializationError && data) {
          try {
            updateInstanceData(data);
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('❌ Dots data update effect failed:', error);
            initializationError = error.message;
          }
        }
      });
    });
  
    // Reactive effect for scale changes - BaseGPURenderer handles the heavy lifting
    $effect(() => {
      if (!renderer) return;
      
      // Access scale properties to make this reactive
      const xScale = c?.xScale;
      const yScale = c?.yScale;
      
      // Track scale domains and ranges
      void xScale?.domain?.();
      void yScale?.domain?.();
      void xScale?.range?.();
      void yScale?.range?.();
      
      // Track band scale properties for completeness
      void xScale?.bandwidth?.();
      void yScale?.bandwidth?.();
      
      untrack(() => {
        renderer.handleScaleChange();
      });
    });
  
    // Reactive effect for resolution changes
    $effect(() => {
      if (!renderer) return;
      
      const { width, height } = webGPUContext.getCanvasDimensions();
      
      untrack(() => {
        renderer.handleResolutionChange();
      });
    });
  </script>
  
  {#if initializationError}
    <div class="dots-error">
      <p>Dots component failed to initialize:</p>
      <p class="error-message">{initializationError}</p>
    </div>
  {/if}
  
  <style>
    .dots-error {
      position: absolute;
      top: 10px;
      left: 10px;

      border: 1px solid #ffcccb;

<src/lib/components/rendering/Dots copy.svelte>
  <script>
    import { getContext, onMount, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
    import { getScaleState } from '$lib/scales';
  
    let { 
      data, // This would be an Arrow Table
      dataVersion,
      r = 3,
      fill = 'blue',
      alpha = .5,
      zIndex = 0,
    } = $props();
  
    let colorScale = $derived(getScaleState('color', 'ordinal'));
    
    const c = getContext('chart');
    const webGPUContext = getContext('webgpu-canvas');
  
    // WebGPU resources
    let pipeline;
    let bindGroup;
    let resourcesCreated = false;
  
    // Separate buffers for Arrow columns
    let xBuffer;
    let yBuffer;
    let radiusBuffer;
    let alphaBuffer;
    let colorBuffer;
    let scaleParamsBuffer;
    let resolutionBuffer;
    let dprBuffer;
    let instanceCount = 0;
    
    let layerControl;
    let quadGeometryBuffer;
    const quadVertexCount = 6;
  
    let xDomain = $derived(c.xScale?.domain() ?? []);
    let yDomain = $derived(c.yScale?.domain() ?? []);
    let xRange = $derived(c.xScale?.range() ?? []);
    let yRange = $derived(c.yScale?.range() ?? []);
  
    const devicePixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  
    // Updated shader to work with separate buffers
    const shaderCode = `
      struct VertexOutput {
        @builtin(position) position: vec4<f32>,
        @location(0) color: vec4<f32>,
        @location(1) uv: vec2<f32>,
        @location(2) radius: f32,
        @location(3) alpha: f32,
      };
  
      struct ScaleParams {
        domainMin: vec2<f32>,
        domainMax: vec2<f32>,
        rangeMin: vec2<f32>,
        rangeMax: vec2<f32>,
      };
  
      @group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> resolution: vec2<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> xValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> yValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read> radiusValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(4) var<storage, read> alphaValues: array<f32>;
      @group(0) @binding(5) var<storage, read> colors: array<vec4<f32>>;
      @group(0) @binding(6) var<uniform> scaleParams: ScaleParams;
      @group(0) @binding(7) var<uniform> devicePixelRatio: f32;
      
      fn scaleValue(value: f32, domainMin: f32, domainMax: f32, rangeMin: f32, rangeMax: f32) -> f32 {
        let normalized = (value - domainMin) / (domainMax - domainMin);
        return rangeMin + normalized * (rangeMax - rangeMin);
      }
      
      @vertex
      fn vertexMain(
        @location(0) quadPosition: vec2<f32>,
        @builtin(instance_index) instanceIdx: u32,
      ) -> VertexOutput {
        // Get values from separate buffers
        let x = xValues[instanceIdx];
        let y = yValues[instanceIdx];
        let radius = radiusValues[instanceIdx];
        let alpha = alphaValues[instanceIdx];
        let color = colors[instanceIdx];
        
        // Apply scales on GPU
        let scaledX = scaleValue(x, scaleParams.domainMin.x, scaleParams.domainMax.x, 
                                 scaleParams.rangeMin.x, scaleParams.rangeMax.x);
        let scaledY = scaleValue(y, scaleParams.domainMin.y, scaleParams.domainMax.y, 
                                 scaleParams.rangeMin.y, scaleParams.rangeMax.y);
        
        let displayRadius = radius * devicePixelRatio;
        let radiusWithPadding = displayRadius * 1.2;
        let worldPos = vec2<f32>(scaledX * devicePixelRatio, scaledY * devicePixelRatio) + quadPosition * radiusWithPadding;
        let normalizedPos = (worldPos / resolution) * 2.0 - 1.0;
        
        var output: VertexOutput;
        output.position = vec4<f32>(normalizedPos.x, -normalizedPos.y, 0.0, 1.0);
        output.color = color;
        output.uv = quadPosition;
        output.radius = displayRadius;
        output.alpha = alpha;
        return output;
      }
  
      @fragment
      fn fragmentMain(input: VertexOutput) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
        let dist = length(input.uv);
        let sdf = dist - 1.0;
        let pixelSize = devicePixelRatio / (input.radius * 2.0);
        let alpha = 1.0 - smoothstep(-pixelSize, pixelSize, sdf);
        let finalAlpha = input.color.a * alpha * input.alpha;
        return vec4<f32>(input.color.rgb, finalAlpha);
      }
    `;
  
    function createQuadGeometry() {
      return new Float32Array([
        -1, -1,  // bottom-left
         1, -1,  // bottom-right
        -1,  1,  // top-left
         1, -1,  // bottom-right
         1,  1,  // top-right
        -1,  1,  // top-left
      ]);
    }
  
    async function createGPUResources(device) {
      const shaderModule = device.createShaderModule({
        code: shaderCode,
      });
      const msaaSampleCount = webGPUContext.getMsaaSampleCount();
  
      pipeline = device.createRenderPipeline({
        layout: 'auto',
        vertex: {
          module: shaderModule,
          entryPoint: 'vertexMain',
          buffers: [{
            arrayStride: 2 * 4,
            attributes: [{
              shaderLocation: 0,
              offset: 0,
              format: 'float32x2',
            }],
          }],
        },
        fragment: {
          module: shaderModule,
          entryPoint: 'fragmentMain',
          targets: [{
            format: webGPUContext.getPresentationFormat(),
            blend: {
              color: {
                srcFactor: 'src-alpha',
                dstFactor: 'one-minus-src-alpha',
                operation: 'add',
              },
              alpha: {
                srcFactor: 'one',
                dstFactor: 'one-minus-src-alpha',
                operation: 'add',
              },
            },
          }],
        },
        primitive: {
          topology: 'triangle-list',
        },
        multisample: {
          count: msaaSampleCount > 1 ? msaaSampleCount : 1  // Must be at least 1
        },
      });
  
      const quadVertices = createQuadGeometry();
      quadGeometryBuffer = device.createBuffer({
        size: quadVertices.byteLength,
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.VERTEX | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
      device.queue.writeBuffer(quadGeometryBuffer, 0, quadVertices);
  
      resolutionBuffer = device.createBuffer({
        size: 8,
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
  
      dprBuffer = device.createBuffer({
        size: 4,
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
  
      const { width, height } = webGPUContext.getCanvasDimensions();
      device.queue.writeBuffer(resolutionBuffer, 0, new Float32Array([width, height]));
      device.queue.writeBuffer(dprBuffer, 0, new Float32Array([devicePixelRatio]));
  
      resourcesCreated = true;
    }
  
    function updateInstanceData(device, arrowTable) {
      const tsStartUpdate = Date.now();
      if (!arrowTable || arrowTable.numRows === 0 || !resourcesCreated) return;
  
      instanceCount = arrowTable.numRows;
  
      // Get Arrow columns
      const xColumn = arrowTable.getChild('x');
      const yColumn = arrowTable.getChild('y');
      const colorColumn = arrowTable.getChild('color');
  
      // Convert Float64Array to Float32Array
      const xData = new Float32Array(xColumn.toArray());
      const yData = new Float32Array(yColumn.toArray());
  
      // Create or update buffers with proper size and alignment
      function createOrUpdateBuffer(buffer, data, minSize = 16384) {
        const size = Math.max(data.byteLength, minSize);
        if (!buffer || buffer.size < size) {
          if (buffer) buffer.destroy();
          return device.createBuffer({
            size,
            usage: GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
          });
        }
        return buffer;
      }
  
      // Update x buffer
      xBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(xBuffer, xData);
      device.queue.writeBuffer(xBuffer, 0, xData);
  
      // Update y buffer  
      yBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(yBuffer, yData);
      device.queue.writeBuffer(yBuffer, 0, yData);
  
      // Create radius and alpha data
      const radiusData = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
      const alphaData = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
      
      if (typeof r === 'function' || typeof alpha === 'function') {
        for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
          radiusData[i] = typeof r === 'function' ? r({x: xData[i], y: yData[i]}) : r;
          alphaData[i] = typeof alpha === 'function' ? alpha({x: xData[i], y: yData[i]}) : alpha;
        }
      } else {
        radiusData.fill(r);
        alphaData.fill(alpha);
      }
  
      // Update radius buffer
      radiusBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(radiusBuffer, radiusData);
      device.queue.writeBuffer(radiusBuffer, 0, radiusData);
  
      // Update alpha buffer
      alphaBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(alphaBuffer, alphaData);
      device.queue.writeBuffer(alphaBuffer, 0, alphaData);
  
      // Handle colors
      const colorData = new Float32Array(instanceCount * 4);
      const colorArray = colorColumn.toArray();
      
      for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
        const fillColor = colorScale(colorArray[i]);
        const rgba = parseColor(fillColor);
        colorData.set(rgba, i * 4);
      }
  
      colorBuffer = createOrUpdateBuffer(colorBuffer, colorData);
      device.queue.writeBuffer(colorBuffer, 0, colorData);
  
      // Create scale parameters buffer if it doesn't exist
      if (!scaleParamsBuffer) {
        scaleParamsBuffer = device.createBuffer({
          size: 32,
          usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
        });
      }
      updateScaleParameters(device);
  
      // Create bind group only after all buffers are ready
      if (!bindGroup) {
        createBindGroup(device);
      }
    }
  
    function createBindGroup(device) {
      // Make sure all buffers exist before creating bind group
      if (!xBuffer || !yBuffer || !radiusBuffer || !alphaBuffer || !colorBuffer || !scaleParamsBuffer || !resolutionBuffer || !dprBuffer || !pipeline) {
        console.warn('Not all buffers are ready for bind group creation');
        return;
      }
  
      bindGroup = device.createBindGroup({
        layout: pipeline.getBindGroupLayout(0),
        entries: [
          { binding: 0, resource: { buffer: resolutionBuffer } },
          { binding: 1, resource: { buffer: xBuffer } },
          { binding: 2, resource: { buffer: yBuffer } },
          { binding: 3, resource: { buffer: radiusBuffer } },
          { binding: 4, resource: { buffer: alphaBuffer } },
          { binding: 5, resource: { buffer: colorBuffer } },
          { binding: 6, resource: { buffer: scaleParamsBuffer } },
          { binding: 7, resource: { buffer: dprBuffer } },
        ],
      });
    }
  
    function updateScaleParameters(device) {
      if (!scaleParamsBuffer) return;
  
      const scaleParams = new Float32Array(8);
      scaleParams[0] = xDomain[0] ?? 0;
      scaleParams[1] = yDomain[0] ?? 0;
      scaleParams[2] = xDomain[1] ?? 1;
      scaleParams[3] = yDomain[1] ?? 1;
      scaleParams[4] = xRange[0] ?? 0;
      scaleParams[5] = yRange[0] ?? 0;
      scaleParams[6] = xRange[1] ?? 1;
      scaleParams[7] = yRange[1] ?? 1;
  
      device.queue.writeBuffer(scaleParamsBuffer, 0, scaleParams);
    }
  
    function draw(passEncoder, device, presentationFormat) {
      if (!pipeline || !bindGroup || instanceCount === 0) return;
  
      passEncoder.setPipeline(pipeline);
      passEncoder.setBindGroup(0, bindGroup);
      passEncoder.setVertexBuffer(0, quadGeometryBuffer);
      passEncoder.draw(quadVertexCount, instanceCount);
    }
  
    function parseColor(color) {
      if (color.startsWith('#')) {
        const r = parseInt(color.slice(1, 3), 16) / 255;
        const g = parseInt(color.slice(3, 5), 16) / 255;
        const b = parseInt(color.slice(5, 7), 16) / 255;
        return [r, g, b, 1.0];
      }
      return [0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0];
    }
  
    async function initialize() {
      const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
      if (device) {
        await createGPUResources(device);
        updateInstanceData(device, data);
        
        // Register this component as a WebGPU layer
        layerControl = webGPUContext.registerLayer({
          zIndex,
          draw,
          visible: true
        });
      }
    }
  
    // Wait for WebGPU to be initialized before setting up resources
    async function waitForWebGPU() {
      while (!webGPUContext.isInitialized()) {
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
      }
      await initialize();
    }
  
    onMount(async () => {
      await waitForWebGPU();
    });
  
    onDestroy(() => {
      // Clean up WebGPU resources
      if (quadGeometryBuffer) quadGeometryBuffer.destroy();
      if (xBuffer) xBuffer.destroy();
      if (yBuffer) yBuffer.destroy();
      if (radiusBuffer) radiusBuffer.destroy();
      if (alphaBuffer) alphaBuffer.destroy();
      if (colorBuffer) colorBuffer.destroy();
      if (scaleParamsBuffer) scaleParamsBuffer.destroy();
      if (resolutionBuffer) resolutionBuffer.destroy();
      if (dprBuffer) dprBuffer.destroy();
      
      // Clean up layer
      if (layerControl) {
        layerControl.remove();
      }
    });
  
    // Update instance data when dataVersion changes;
    $effect(() => {
      void dataVersion;
      
      untrack(() => {
        const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
        if (device && layerControl && resourcesCreated) {
          updateInstanceData(device, data);
          layerControl.invalidate();
        }
      });
    });
  
    // Update the scale change effect to only update scale parameters
    $effect(() => {
      void xDomain, yDomain, xRange, yRange;
      const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
      if (device && layerControl && resourcesCreated && scaleParamsBuffer) {
        // Only update scale parameters, not the entire bind group
        updateScaleParameters(device);
        layerControl.invalidate();
      }
    });
  
    // Update resolution when canvas size changes
    $effect(() => {
      // Trigger on width and height changes from the chart context
      // void c.innerWidth;
      // void c.innerHeight;
      const { width, height } = webGPUContext.getCanvasDimensions();
      const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
      if (resolutionBuffer && device && layerControl && resourcesCreated) {
        device.queue.writeBuffer(resolutionBuffer, 0, new Float32Array([width, height]));
        // layerControl.invalidate();
        // console.log('resolution buffer updated')
      }
    });
  </script>
</src/lib/components/rendering/Dots copy.svelte>

<src/lib/components/rendering/index.js>
  export { default as Canvas } from './Canvas.svelte';
  export { default as WebGPUCanvas } from './WebGPUCanvas.svelte';
  export { default as Dots } from './Dots.svelte';
</src/lib/components/rendering/index.js>

<src/lib/components/rendering/WebGPUCanvas.svelte>
  <script>
    import { onMount, onDestroy, setContext, getContext } from "svelte";
    import { getGPUContext } from '$lib/gpu';
  
    let {
      canvasComponents,
      msaaSampleCount = 4 
    } = $props();
  
    const c = getContext('chart');
    let width = $derived(c.innerWidth);
    let height = $derived(c.innerHeight);
    let margin = $derived(c.margin);
  
    let canvas;
    let context;
    let gpuCoordinator;
    let chartControl;
    let contextConfigured = $state(false);
    let initializationError = $state(null);
  
    const chartId = `chart-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
    const devicePixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    const layers = [];
  
    // Enhanced initialization with comprehensive error handling
    async function initWebGPU() {
      try {
        console.log(`🚀 Initializing WebGPUCanvas ${chartId}`);
        
        // Get shared GPU coordinator
        gpuCoordinator = getGPUContext();
        
        if (!gpuCoordinator) {
          throw new Error('GPUCoordinator not found. Ensure setCoordinators() was called in a parent component.');
        }
  
        // Wait for coordinator initialization with timeout
        let attempts = 0;
        const maxAttempts = 100; // 5 seconds at 50ms intervals
        
        while (!gpuCoordinator.isInitialized && attempts < maxAttempts) {
          await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
          attempts++;
        }
  
        if (!gpuCoordinator.isInitialized) {
          throw new Error(`GPUCoordinator failed to initialize within ${maxAttempts * 50}ms timeout`);
        }
  
        // Configure context with proper error handling
        await configureContext();
        
        // Register with coordinator after context is ready
        registerWithCoordinator();
        
        console.log(`✅ WebGPUCanvas ${chartId} initialized successfully`);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ WebGPUCanvas ${chartId} initialization failed:`, error);
        initializationError = error.message;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Separate context configuration with proper error handling
    async function configureContext() {
      if (!canvas) {
        throw new Error('Canvas element not available for context configuration');
      }
  
      try {
        // Get WebGPU context
        context = canvas.getContext('webgpu');
        if (!context) {
          throw new Error('Failed to get WebGPU context from canvas. WebGPU may not be supported.');
        }
  
        const device = gpuCoordinator.device;
        const presentationFormat = gpuCoordinator.presentationFormat;
  
        if (!device) {
          throw new Error('No WebGPU device available from coordinator');
        }
  
        // Configure context
        context.configure({
          device,
          format: presentationFormat,
          alphaMode: 'premultiplied',
        });
        
        contextConfigured = true;
        console.log(`🎨 Context configured successfully for chart ${chartId}`);
        
        // Setup initial canvas size
        setupCanvas();
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Context configuration failed for chart ${chartId}:`, error);
        contextConfigured = false;
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Clean registration with coordinator
    function registerWithCoordinator() {
      if (!contextConfigured) {
        console.error(`⚠️ Attempting to register chart ${chartId} before context is configured`);
        return;
      }
  
      console.log(`📋 Registering chart ${chartId} with coordinator`);
      
      try {
        chartControl = gpuCoordinator.registerChart(chartId, canvas, {
          drawFunction: drawLayers,
          zIndex: 0,
          markTypes: layers.map(layer => layer.markType || 'unknown'),
        });
  
        // Immediately notify coordinator that context is ready
        chartControl.update({ contextConfigured: true });
        console.log(`✅ Chart ${chartId} registered and ready`);
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Chart registration failed for ${chartId}:`, error);
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    // Robust canvas setup with dimension change detection
    function setupCanvas() {
      if (!canvas || !contextConfigured) {
        console.debug(`⚠️ setupCanvas called before canvas/context ready for ${chartId}`);
        return;
      }
      
      try {
        // Calculate new dimensions
        const canvasWidth = width * devicePixelRatio;
        const canvasHeight = height * devicePixelRatio;
        
        // Only update if dimensions actually changed to avoid unnecessary work
        const dimensionsChanged = 
          canvas.width !== canvasWidth || 
          canvas.height !== canvasHeight;
        
        const positionChanged = 
          canvas.style.transform !== `translate(${margin.left}px, ${margin.top}px)`;
  
        if (dimensionsChanged) {
          canvas.width = canvasWidth;
          canvas.height = canvasHeight;
          canvas.style.width = width + 'px';
          canvas.style.height = height + 'px';
          
          console.debug(`📐 Canvas ${chartId} resized to ${canvasWidth}x${canvasHeight}`);
        }
  
        if (positionChanged) {
          canvas.style.transform = `translate(${margin.left}px, ${margin.top}px)`;
          console.debug(`📍 Canvas ${chartId} repositioned to (${margin.left}, ${margin.top})`);
        }
  
        // Notify coordinator of changes
        if (chartControl && (dimensionsChanged || positionChanged)) {
          chartControl.update({ 
            canvasResized: dimensionsChanged,
            canvasRepositioned: positionChanged 
          });
        }
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Canvas setup failed for ${chartId}:`, error);
        initializationError = `Canvas setup failed: ${error.message}`;
      }
    }
  
    // Enhanced layer rendering with comprehensive error handling
    function drawLayers(passEncoder, sharedResources) {
      const visibleLayers = layers.filter(layer => layer.visible !== false);
      
      if (visibleLayers.length === 0) {
        console.debug(`📝 No visible layers to render for chart ${chartId}`);
        return;
      }
  
      console.debug(`🎨 Rendering ${visibleLayers.length} layers for chart ${chartId}`);
      
      let successfulLayers = 0;
      let failedLayers = 0;
      
      for (let i = 0; i < visibleLayers.length; i++) {
        const layer = visibleLayers[i];
        try {
          layer.draw(passEncoder, gpuCoordinator.device, gpuCoordinator.presentationFormat);
          successfulLayers++;
        } catch (error) {
          console.error(`❌ Layer ${i} (${layer.markType || 'unknown'}) render failed:`, error);
          failedLayers++;
          // Continue rendering other layers even if one fails
        }
      }
  
      if (failedLayers > 0) {
        console.warn(`⚠️ Chart ${chartId}: ${successfulLayers} layers rendered successfully, ${failedLayers} failed`);
      }
    }
  
    // Enhanced layer management with better tracking
    function createLayerControl(layer) {
      const layerId = `layer-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 6)}`;
      console.log(`📋 Registering layer ${layerId} (${layer.markType || 'unknown'}) for chart ${chartId}`);
      
      const zIndex = layer.zIndex || 0;
      let insertIndex = layers.findIndex(l => (l.zIndex || 0) > zIndex);
      if (insertIndex === -1) insertIndex = layers.length;
      
      const layerWithId = { ...layer, id: layerId, chartId };
      layers.splice(insertIndex, 0, layerWithId);
      
      console.log(`✅ Layer ${layerId} registered. Total layers for ${chartId}: ${layers.length}`);
      
      if (chartControl) {
        chartControl.update({ 
          markTypes: layers.map(l => l.markType || 'unknown')
        });
        chartControl.invalidate();
      }
      
      return {
        update(newProps) {
          const oldMarkType = layerWithId.markType;
          Object.assign(layerWithId, newProps);
          
          // Update chart registration if mark types changed
          if (newProps.markType && newProps.markType !== oldMarkType && chartControl) {
            chartControl.update({ 
              markTypes: layers.map(l => l.markType || 'unknown')
            });
          }
          
          chartControl?.invalidate();
        },
        
        remove() {
          const index = layers.indexOf(layerWithId);
          if (index !== -1) {
            layers.splice(index, 1);
            console.log(`🗑️ Layer ${layerId} removed. Total layers for ${chartId}: ${layers.length}`);
            
            if (chartControl) {
              chartControl.update({ 
                markTypes: layers.map(l => l.markType || 'unknown')
              });
              chartControl.invalidate();
            }
          }
        },
        
        invalidate() {
          chartControl?.invalidate();
        },
        
        // Additional debugging info
        getInfo() {
          return {
            layerId,
            chartId,
            markType: layerWithId.markType,
            zIndex: layerWithId.zIndex,
            visible: layerWithId.visible
          };
        }
      };
    }
  
    // Enhanced context with better state management and error handling
    setContext('webgpu-canvas', {
      registerLayer(layer) {
        try {
          return createLayerControl(layer);
        } catch (error) {
          console.error(`❌ Layer registration failed for chart ${chartId}:`, error);
          // Return a dummy control to prevent crashes
          return {
            update: () => {},
            remove: () => {},
            invalidate: () => {},
            getInfo: () => ({ error: error.message })
          };
        }
      },
      
      getCanvasDimensions() {
        return { 
          width: width * devicePixelRatio, 
          height: height * devicePixelRatio,
          cssWidth: width,
          cssHeight: height,
          devicePixelRatio
        };
      },
  
      getMsaaSampleCount() {
        return gpuCoordinator?.msaaSampleCount || msaaSampleCount;
      },
  
      getDevice() {
        return gpuCoordinator?.device;
      },
  
      getPresentationFormat() {
        return gpuCoordinator?.presentationFormat;
      },
  
      isInitialized() {
        return contextConfigured && gpuCoordinator?.isInitialized;
      },
      
      invalidate() {
        chartControl?.invalidate();
      },
  
      // Enhanced debugging support
      getDebugInfo() {
        return {
          chartId,
          contextConfigured,
          initializationError,
          layerCount: layers.length,
          layers: layers.map(l => ({
            id: l.id,
            markType: l.markType,
            zIndex: l.zIndex,
            visible: l.visible
          })),
          canvasDimensions: {
            width: canvas?.width,
            height: canvas?.height,
            cssWidth: canvas?.style.width,
            cssHeight: canvas?.style.height
          }
        };
      }
    });
  
    onMount(async () => {
      try {
        await initWebGPU();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ WebGPUCanvas ${chartId} mount failed:`, error);
        // Error is already stored in initializationError state
      }
    });
  
    onDestroy(() => {
      console.log(`🧹 Destroying WebGPUCanvas ${chartId}`);
      
      try {
        // Clean up chart registration
        if (chartControl) {
          chartControl.remove();
          chartControl = null;
        }
        
        // Context cleanup happens automatically when canvas is removed
        contextConfigured = false;
        
        // Clear references
        gpuCoordinator = null;
        context = null;
        
        console.log(`✅ WebGPUCanvas ${chartId} destroyed successfully`);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Error during WebGPUCanvas ${chartId} destruction:`, error);
      }
    });
  
    // Reactive effect for dimension changes with proper debouncing
    let resizeTimeoutId = null;
    
    $effect(() => {
      // Only react to meaningful dimension changes
      if (width > 0 && height > 0 && contextConfigured) {
        // Debounce rapid resize events
        if (resizeTimeoutId) {
          clearTimeout(resizeTimeoutId);
        }
        
        resizeTimeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
          setupCanvas();
          resizeTimeoutId = null;
        }, 8); // ~120fps throttling
      }
    });
  
    // Cleanup resize timeout on destroy
    onDestroy(() => {
      if (resizeTimeoutId) {
        clearTimeout(resizeTimeoutId);
      }
    });
  </script>
  
  {#if initializationError}
    <div class="webgpu-error">
      <p>WebGPU initialization failed for chart {chartId}:</p>
      <p class="error-message">{initializationError}</p>
      <p class="error-help">
        Please ensure your browser supports WebGPU and try refreshing the page.
      </p>
    </div>
  {:else}
    <canvas bind:this={canvas}></canvas>
    {#if contextConfigured}
      {@render canvasComponents?.()}
    {:else}
      <div class="webgpu-loading">
        <p>Initializing WebGPU context for chart {chartId}...</p>
      </div>
    {/if}
  {/if}
  
  <style>
    canvas {
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
    }
  
    .webgpu-error,
    .webgpu-loading {
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
