  // src/lib/gpu/errors.js
  
  /**
   * Base class for GPU-related errors
   */
  export class GPUError extends Error {
    constructor(message, context = {}) {
      super(message);
      this.name = 'GPUError';
      this.context = context;
      this.timestamp = Date.now();
    }
  
    toJSON() {
      return {
        name: this.name,
        message: this.message,
        context: this.context,
        timestamp: this.timestamp,
        stack: this.stack
      };
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Error thrown when WebGPU is not supported
   */
  export class WebGPUNotSupportedError extends GPUError {
    constructor(reason = 'Unknown') {
      super(`WebGPU is not supported: ${reason}`, { reason });
      this.name = 'WebGPUNotSupportedError';
    }
  
    static detect() {
      if (typeof navigator === 'undefined') {
        return new WebGPUNotSupportedError('Running in non-browser environment');
      }
      
      if (!navigator.gpu) {
        return new WebGPUNotSupportedError('navigator.gpu not available');
      }
      
      return null; // WebGPU appears to be supported
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Error thrown when GPU resource creation fails
   */
  export class ResourceCreationError extends GPUError {
    constructor(resourceType, details = {}) {
      super(`Failed to create GPU resource: ${resourceType}`, { 
        resourceType, 
        ...details 
      });
      this.name = 'ResourceCreationError';
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Error thrown when GPU context configuration fails
   */
  export class ContextConfigurationError extends GPUError {
    constructor(details = {}) {
      super('Failed to configure WebGPU context', details);
      this.name = 'ContextConfigurationError';
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Error thrown when render operations fail
   */
  export class RenderError extends GPUError {
    constructor(stage, details = {}) {
      super(`Render error in stage: ${stage}`, { stage, ...details });
      this.name = 'RenderError';
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Error recovery strategies
   */
  export const ErrorRecoveryStrategies = {
    RETRY: 'retry',
    FALLBACK: 'fallback',
    DISABLE: 'disable',
    ABORT: 'abort'
  };
  
  /**
   * Enhanced error handler with recovery strategies
   */
  export function handleGPUError(error, context = {}) {
    const errorInfo = {
      error: error instanceof GPUError ? error.toJSON() : {
        name: error.name || 'UnknownError',
        message: error.message || 'Unknown error occurred',
        stack: error.stack
      },
      context,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      userAgent: typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.userAgent : 'unknown',
      webgpuSupported: typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? !!navigator.gpu : false
    };
  
    // Log error with context
    console.error('🚨 GPU Error:', errorInfo);
  
    // Determine recovery strategy based on error type
    let strategy = ErrorRecoveryStrategies.ABORT;
    
    if (error instanceof WebGPUNotSupportedError) {
      strategy = ErrorRecoveryStrategies.FALLBACK;
    } else if (error instanceof ResourceCreationError) {
      strategy = ErrorRecoveryStrategies.RETRY;
    } else if (error instanceof ContextConfigurationError) {
      strategy = ErrorRecoveryStrategies.RETRY;
    } else if (error instanceof RenderError) {
      strategy = ErrorRecoveryStrategies.FALLBACK;
    }
  
    // Apply recovery strategy
    const recovery = {
      strategy,
      shouldRetry: strategy === ErrorRecoveryStrategies.RETRY,
      shouldFallback: strategy === ErrorRecoveryStrategies.FALLBACK,
      shouldDisable: strategy === ErrorRecoveryStrategies.DISABLE,
      shouldAbort: strategy === ErrorRecoveryStrategies.ABORT,
      
      // Retry with exponential backoff
      async retry(fn, maxAttempts = 3) {
        if (!this.shouldRetry) return false;
        
        for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
          try {
            await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.pow(2, attempt) * 100));
            await fn();
            console.log(`✅ Recovery successful after ${attempt} attempts`);
            return true;
          } catch (retryError) {
            console.warn(`⚠️ Retry attempt ${attempt} failed:`, retryError.message);
            if (attempt === maxAttempts) {
              console.error(`❌ All ${maxAttempts} retry attempts failed`);
              return false;
            }
          }
        }
        return false;
      },
      
      // Fallback to alternative rendering
      fallback(fallbackFn) {
        if (!this.shouldFallback) return false;
        
        try {
          console.log('🔄 Attempting fallback strategy...');
          fallbackFn();
          console.log('✅ Fallback strategy successful');
          return true;
        } catch (fallbackError) {
          console.error('❌ Fallback strategy failed:', fallbackError);
          return false;
        }
      }
    };
  
    // Send error to monitoring service in production
    if (context.reportErrors && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      try {
        // This would integrate with your error reporting service
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('diveplot-error', {
          detail: errorInfo
        }));
      } catch (reportingError) {
        console.warn('Failed to report error:', reportingError);
      }
    }
  
    return {
      errorInfo,
      recovery
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Error boundary utility for components
   */
  export class GPUErrorBoundary {
    constructor(componentName, options = {}) {
      this.componentName = componentName;
      this.maxRetries = options.maxRetries || 3;
      this.retryCount = 0;
      this.lastError = null;
      this.fallbackFn = options.fallbackFn;
      this.onError = options.onError;
    }
  
    async execute(fn) {
      try {
        const result = await fn();
        this.retryCount = 0; // Reset on success
        return result;
      } catch (error) {
        this.lastError = error;
        const handled = handleGPUError(error, { 
          component: this.componentName,
          retryCount: this.retryCount 
        });
  
        // Call custom error handler if provided
        if (this.onError) {
          this.onError(error, handled);
        }
  
        // Attempt recovery
        if (handled.recovery.shouldRetry && this.retryCount < this.maxRetries) {
          this.retryCount++;
          console.log(`🔄 Retrying ${this.componentName} (attempt ${this.retryCount}/${this.maxRetries})`);
          
          // Wait before retry
          await new Promise(resolve => 
            setTimeout(resolve, Math.pow(2, this.retryCount) * 100)
          );
          
          return this.execute(fn);
        }
  
        // Try fallback if available
        if (handled.recovery.shouldFallback && this.fallbackFn) {
          console.log(`🔄 Using fallback for ${this.componentName}`);
          return this.fallbackFn();
        }
  
        // Re-throw if no recovery possible
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    reset() {
      this.retryCount = 0;
      this.lastError = null;
    }
  
    getStatus() {
      return {
        componentName: this.componentName,
        retryCount: this.retryCount,
        maxRetries: this.maxRetries,
        hasError: !!this.lastError,
        lastError: this.lastError?.message
      };
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Development error utilities
   */
  export const devErrorUtils = {
    /**
     * Simulate various GPU errors for testing
     */
    simulate: {
      webgpuNotSupported() {
        throw new WebGPUNotSupportedError('Simulated for testing');
      },
      
      resourceCreationFailed(resourceType = 'buffer') {
        throw new ResourceCreationError(resourceType, { 
          simulated: true,
          reason: 'Testing error handling' 
        });
      },
      
      contextConfigurationFailed() {
        throw new ContextConfigurationError({ 
          simulated: true,
          reason: 'Testing context configuration error' 
        });
      },
      
      renderFailed(stage = 'draw') {
        throw new RenderError(stage, { 
          simulated: true,
          reason: 'Testing render error' 
        });
      }
    },
  
    /**
     * Test error recovery mechanisms
     */
    testRecovery: {
      async retryMechanism() {
        console.log('🧪 Testing retry mechanism...');
        
        let attempts = 0;
        const testFn = () => {
          attempts++;
          if (attempts < 3) {
            throw new Error('Simulated failure');
          }
          return 'Success!';
        };
  
        const boundary = new GPUErrorBoundary('TestComponent', { maxRetries: 3 });
        
        try {
          const result = await boundary.execute(testFn);
          console.log(`✅ Retry test completed: ${result} (took ${attempts} attempts)`);
        } catch (error) {
          console.log(`❌ Retry test failed after ${attempts} attempts:`, error.message);
        }
      },
  
      fallbackMechanism() {
        console.log('🧪 Testing fallback mechanism...');
        
        const boundary = new GPUErrorBoundary('TestComponent', {
          fallbackFn: () => 'Fallback result'
        });
        
        boundary.execute(() => {
          throw new RenderError('test-stage');
        }).then(result => {
          console.log(`✅ Fallback test completed: ${result}`);
        }).catch(error => {
          console.log(`❌ Fallback test failed:`, error.message);
        });
      }
    }
  };
  
  // Development-only global error utilities
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
    window.DivePlotErrorUtils = devErrorUtils;
  }
</src/lib/gpu/errors.js>

<src/lib/gpu/GPUCoordinator.svelte.js>
  // src/lib/gpu/GPUCoordinator.svelte.js
  import { setContext, getContext } from 'svelte';
  
  /**
   * GPU Coordination Debugger for development and monitoring
   */
  class GPUCoordinationDebugger {
    constructor(coordinator) {
      this.coordinator = coordinator;
      this.renderStats = {
        frameCount: 0,
        totalRenderTime: 0,
        averageRenderTime: 0,
        lastFrameTime: 0,
        renderErrors: []
      };
      this.renderStartTime = null;
      
      // Only enable in development
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
        window.__DEBUG_GPU_COORDINATOR = coordinator;
        window.__DEBUG_GPU_DEBUGGER = this;
      }
    }
  
    startRenderTiming() {
      this.renderStartTime = performance.now();
    }
  
    endRenderTiming() {
      if (!this.renderStartTime) return;
      
      const renderTime = performance.now() - this.renderStartTime;
      this.renderStats.frameCount++;
      this.renderStats.totalRenderTime += renderTime;
      this.renderStats.lastFrameTime = renderTime;
      this.renderStats.averageRenderTime = this.renderStats.totalRenderTime / this.renderStats.frameCount;
      
      // Warn about slow frames
      if (renderTime > 16.67) { // > 60fps threshold
        console.warn(`🐌 Slow render frame: ${renderTime.toFixed(2)}ms`);
      }
      
      this.renderStartTime = null;
    }
  
    analyzeResourceUsage() {
      const stats = this.coordinator.getPerformanceStats();
      const analysis = {
        efficiency: 'unknown',
        recommendations: [],
        warnings: []
      };
  
      // Analyze pipeline sharing efficiency
      const totalPipelines = stats.resourceStats.pipelines.length;
      const averageRefs = totalPipelines > 0 
        ? stats.resourceStats.pipelines.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.refCount, 0) / totalPipelines
        : 0;
  
      if (averageRefs < 2) {
        analysis.warnings.push('Low pipeline sharing - consider consolidating mark types');
        analysis.efficiency = 'poor';
      } else if (averageRefs > 5) {
        analysis.efficiency = 'excellent';
      } else {
        analysis.efficiency = 'good';
      }
  
      // Check for resource leaks
      const oldResources = stats.resourceStats.pipelines.filter(p => p.ageMs > 300000); // 5 minutes
      if (oldResources.length > 0) {
        analysis.warnings.push(`${oldResources.length} old resources detected - potential memory leak`);
      }
  
      // Performance recommendations
      if (this.renderStats.averageRenderTime > 8) {
        analysis.recommendations.push('Consider reducing mark complexity or data volume');
      }
  
      if (stats.renderBatches.markTypeBatches > 5) {
        analysis.recommendations.push('Too many mark types - consider consolidation');
      }
  
      return analysis;
    }
  
    logCoordinationState() {
      const stats = this.coordinator.getPerformanceStats();
      const analysis = this.analyzeResourceUsage();
      
      console.group('🔍 GPU Coordination Debug Report');
      
      console.log('📊 Performance Stats:', {
        ...this.renderStats,
        efficiency: analysis.efficiency
      });
      
      console.log('🔧 Resource Usage:', stats.resourceStats);
      console.log('🎨 Render Batches:', stats.renderBatches);
      
      if (analysis.warnings.length > 0) {
        console.warn('⚠️ Warnings:', analysis.warnings);
      }
      
      if (analysis.recommendations.length > 0) {
        console.info('💡 Recommendations:', analysis.recommendations);
      }
      
      console.groupEnd();
    }
  
    recordRenderError(error, context) {
      const errorRecord = {
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        error: error.message,
        context,
        stack: error.stack
      };
      
      this.renderStats.renderErrors.push(errorRecord);
      
      // Keep only last 50 errors
      if (this.renderStats.renderErrors.length > 50) {
        this.renderStats.renderErrors.shift();
      }
    }
  
    debugChart(chartId) {
      const chartConfig = this.coordinator._registeredCharts.get(chartId);
      if (!chartConfig) {
        console.error(`Chart ${chartId} not found`);
        return;
      }
  
      console.group(`🎯 Chart Debug: ${chartId}`);
      console.log('Chart Config:', {
        visible: chartConfig.visible,
        zIndex: chartConfig.zIndex,
        markTypes: chartConfig.markTypes,
        viewport: chartConfig.viewport,
        contextConfigured: chartConfig.contextConfigured
      });
      
      if (chartConfig.canvas) {
        console.log('Canvas State:', {
          width: chartConfig.canvas.width,
          height: chartConfig.canvas.height,
          style: chartConfig.canvas.style.cssText
        });
      }
      
      console.groupEnd();
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Enhanced GPU Coordinator with resource sharing, batching, and debugging
   */
  export class GPUCoordinator {
    // WebGPU core resources
    #device = $state(null);
    #presentationFormat = $state(null);
    #initialized = $state(false);
  
    // Enhanced shared resources with reference counting
    #sharedResources = $state({
      pipelines: new Map(),           // 'dots' -> { pipeline, refCount, createdAt }
      uniformBuffers: new Map(),      // 'devicePixelRatio' -> { buffer, refCount, createdAt }
      shaderModules: new Map(),       // 'dots' -> { module, refCount, createdAt }
      bindGroupLayouts: new Map()     // 'dots' -> { layout, refCount, createdAt }
    });
  
    // Chart registration and management
    #registeredCharts = $state(new Map());
    #renderQueue = $state([]);
    #needsRedraw = $state(false);
    #rafId = null;
    #lastRenderTime = 0;
  
    // Enhanced render batching
    #renderBatches = $state({
      byMarkType: new Map(), // 'dots' -> [chart1, chart2, ...]
      byZIndex: new Map(),   // 0 -> [charts...], 1 -> [charts...]
      needsRebuild: true
    });
  
    // Configuration
    #msaaSampleCount = 4;
    #enableDebugging = true;
  
    constructor(config = {}) {
      this.#msaaSampleCount = config.msaaSampleCount ?? 4;
      this.#enableDebugging = config.enableDebugging !== false;
      
      // Initialize debugging
      if (this.#enableDebugging) {
        this.debugger = new GPUCoordinationDebugger(this);
      }
    }
  
    // ===== INITIALIZATION =====
  
    async initialize() {
      if (this.#initialized) {
        console.warn('GPUCoordinator already initialized');
        return;
      }
  
      try {
        await this.#initWebGPU();
        await this.#createSharedResources();
        this.#initialized = true;
        this.#setupRenderLoop();
        console.log('✅ GPUCoordinator initialized successfully');
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ GPUCoordinator initialization failed:', error);
        this.debugger?.recordRenderError(error, 'initialization');
        throw error;
      }
    }
  
    async #initWebGPU() {
      // Double-check we're on client-side
      if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof navigator === 'undefined') {
        throw new Error('WebGPU initialization attempted server-side');
      }
  
      if (!navigator.gpu) {
        throw new Error('WebGPU not supported');
      }
  
      const adapter = await navigator.gpu.requestAdapter();
      if (!adapter) {
        throw new Error('No appropriate GPUAdapter found');
      }
  
      this.#device = await adapter.requestDevice();
      this.#presentationFormat = navigator.gpu.getPreferredCanvasFormat();
    }
  
    async #createSharedResources() {
      const device = this.#device;
  
      // Create shared uniform buffers
      const dprBuffer = device.createBuffer({
        size: 4, // f32
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
  
      this.#sharedResources.uniformBuffers.set('devicePixelRatio', {
        buffer: dprBuffer,
        refCount: 0, // Will be incremented when used
        createdAt: Date.now()
      });
  
      // Update initial values
      this.#updateSharedUniforms();
    }
  
    #updateSharedUniforms() {
      if (!this.#device) return;
  
      const devicePixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
      const dprResource = this.#sharedResources.uniformBuffers.get('devicePixelRatio');
  
      if (dprResource?.buffer) {
        this.#device.queue.writeBuffer(dprResource.buffer, 0, new Float32Array([devicePixelRatio]));
      }
    }
  
    // ===== ENHANCED RESOURCE SHARING =====
  
    async getOrCreateSharedPipeline(markType, createFn) {
      const existing = this.#sharedResources.pipelines.get(markType);
      
      if (existing) {
        existing.refCount++;
        console.log(`♻️ Reusing shared pipeline for ${markType} (refs: ${existing.refCount})`);
        return existing.pipeline;
      }
  
      console.log(`🆕 Creating new shared pipeline for ${markType}`);
      const pipeline = await createFn();
      
      this.#sharedResources.pipelines.set(markType, {
        pipeline,
        refCount: 1,
        createdAt: Date.now()
      });
  
      return pipeline;
    }
  
    releaseSharedPipeline(markType) {
      const resource = this.#sharedResources.pipelines.get(markType);
      if (!resource) return;
  
      resource.refCount--;
      console.log(`🔄 Released shared pipeline for ${markType} (refs: ${resource.refCount})`);
  
      if (resource.refCount <= 0) {
        console.log(`🗑️ Destroying unused shared pipeline for ${markType}`);
        this.#sharedResources.pipelines.delete(markType);
      }
    }
  
    async getOrCreateSharedShader(markType, shaderCode) {
      const existing = this.#sharedResources.shaderModules.get(markType);
      
      if (existing) {
        existing.refCount++;
        console.log(`♻️ Reusing shared shader for ${markType} (refs: ${existing.refCount})`);
        return existing.module;
      }
  
      console.log(`🆕 Creating new shared shader for ${markType}`);
      const module = this.#device.createShaderModule({ code: shaderCode });
      
      this.#sharedResources.shaderModules.set(markType, {
        module,
        refCount: 1,
        createdAt: Date.now()
      });
  
      return module;
    }
  
    releaseSharedShader(markType) {
      const resource = this.#sharedResources.shaderModules.get(markType);
      if (!resource) return;
  
      resource.refCount--;
      console.log(`🔄 Released shared shader for ${markType} (refs: ${resource.refCount})`);
  
      if (resource.refCount <= 0) {
        console.log(`🗑️ Destroying unused shared shader for ${markType}`);
        this.#sharedResources.shaderModules.delete(markType);
      }
    }
  
    getSharedUniformBuffer(name) {
      const resource = this.#sharedResources.uniformBuffers.get(name);
      if (resource) {
        resource.refCount++;
        return resource.buffer;
      }
      return null;
    }
  
    releaseSharedUniformBuffer(name) {
      const resource = this.#sharedResources.uniformBuffers.get(name);
      if (resource) {
        resource.refCount--;
      }
    }
  
    // Legacy getters for backward compatibility
    getSharedPipeline(markType) {
      return this.#sharedResources.pipelines.get(markType)?.pipeline;
    }
  
    setSharedPipeline(markType, pipeline) {
      this.#sharedResources.pipelines.set(markType, {
        pipeline,
        refCount: 1,
        createdAt: Date.now()
      });
    }
  
    getSharedShaderModule(markType) {
      return this.#sharedResources.shaderModules.get(markType)?.module;
    }
  
    setSharedShaderModule(markType, shaderModule) {
      this.#sharedResources.shaderModules.set(markType, {
        module: shaderModule,
        refCount: 1,
        createdAt: Date.now()
      });
    }
  
    // ===== CHART REGISTRATION =====
  
    registerChart(chartId, canvas, config) {
      const {
        drawFunction,
        zIndex = 0,
        markTypes = [],
      } = config;
  
      if (this.#registeredCharts.has(chartId)) {
        console.warn(`Chart ${chartId} already registered, updating config`);
      }
  
      // Calculate viewport from canvas position
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const viewport = {
        x: rect.left,
        y: rect.top,
        width: canvas.width,
        height: canvas.height
      };
  
      const chartConfig = {
        chartId,
        canvas,
        viewport,
        drawFunction,
        zIndex,
        markTypes,
        visible: true,
        needsRedraw: true,
        context: canvas.getContext('webgpu'),
        contextConfigured: false
      };
  
      this.#registeredCharts.set(chartId, chartConfig);
      this.#renderBatches.needsRebuild = true; // Mark for rebuild
      this.#updateRenderQueue();
  
      console.log(`📊 Registered chart: ${chartId} with marks: [${markTypes.join(', ')}]`);
  
      // Return control object for the chart
      return {
        update: (newConfig) => {
          const existingConfig = this.#registeredCharts.get(chartId);
          if (existingConfig) {
            Object.assign(existingConfig, newConfig);
            
            // Update viewport if canvas size changed
            if (newConfig.canvasResized) {
              const rect = existingConfig.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
              existingConfig.viewport = {
                x: rect.left,
                y: rect.top,
                width: existingConfig.canvas.width,
                height: existingConfig.canvas.height
              };
            }
  
            // Mark context as configured when WebGPUCanvas tells us
            if (newConfig.contextConfigured) {
              existingConfig.contextConfigured = true;
            }
  
            // Check if mark types changed (affects batching)
            if (newConfig.markTypes) {
              this.#renderBatches.needsRebuild = true;
            }
            
            this.#updateRenderQueue();
            this.requestRedraw();
          }
        },
        
        setVisible: (visible) => {
          const config = this.#registeredCharts.get(chartId);
          if (config) {
            config.visible = visible;
            this.requestRedraw();
          }
        },
  
        invalidate: () => {
          const config = this.#registeredCharts.get(chartId);
          if (config) {
            config.needsRedraw = true;
            this.requestRedraw();
          }
        },
  
        remove: () => {
          this.unregisterChart(chartId);
        }
      };
    }
  
    unregisterChart(chartId) {
      if (this.#registeredCharts.has(chartId)) {
        this.#registeredCharts.delete(chartId);
        this.#renderBatches.needsRebuild = true;
        this.#updateRenderQueue();
        this.requestRedraw();
        console.log(`📊 Unregistered chart: ${chartId}`);
      }
    }
  
    #updateRenderQueue() {
      this.#renderQueue = Array.from(this.#registeredCharts.values())
        .filter(config => config.visible)
        .sort((a, b) => a.zIndex - b.zIndex);
    }
  
    // ===== ENHANCED RENDERING WITH BATCHING =====
  
    #setupRenderLoop() {
      this.requestRedraw();
    }
  
    requestRedraw() {
      if (this.#rafId !== null || !this.#initialized) return;
      
      this.#needsRedraw = true;
      this.#rafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => this.#render());
    }
  
    #render() {
      if (!this.#device || !this.#needsRedraw) {
        this.#rafId = null;
        return;
      }
  
      try {
        this.debugger?.startRenderTiming();
  
        // Use live data instead of cached render queue
        const liveRenderQueue = Array.from(this.#registeredCharts.values())
          .filter(config => config.visible)
          .sort((a, b) => a.zIndex - b.zIndex);
        
        // Only include charts with properly configured contexts
        const chartsNeedingRender = liveRenderQueue.filter(chart => 
          chart.visible && 
          chart.needsRedraw && 
          chart.drawFunction &&
          chart.contextConfigured
        );
  
        if (chartsNeedingRender.length === 0) {
          this.#needsRedraw = false;
          this.#rafId = null;
          return;
        }
  
        // Enhanced coordinated rendering with batching
        this.#coordinatedRender(chartsNeedingRender);
        this.#lastRenderTime = Date.now();
  
        this.debugger?.endRenderTiming();
  
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('GPUCoordinator render error:', error);
        this.debugger?.recordRenderError(error, 'main-render-loop');
      } finally {
        this.#needsRedraw = false;
        this.#rafId = null;
      }
    }
  
    #coordinatedRender(charts) {
      // Rebuild batches if needed
      if (this.#renderBatches.needsRebuild) {
        this.#rebuildRenderBatches(charts);
      }
  
      try {
        // Use mark-type batching for maximum efficiency
        this.#renderByMarkTypeBatches(charts);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Coordinated render failed:', error);
        this.debugger?.recordRenderError(error, 'coordinated-render');
        // Fallback to individual chart rendering
        this.#fallbackIndividualRender(charts);
      }
    }
  
    #renderByMarkTypeBatches(charts) {
      const commandEncoders = [];
      
      // Process each mark type batch
      for (const [markType, chartsForType] of this.#renderBatches.byMarkType) {
        if (chartsForType.length === 0) continue;
        
        console.debug(`🎨 Batch rendering ${chartsForType.length} charts with ${markType} marks`);
        
        // Get shared pipeline for this mark type
        const sharedResource = this.#sharedResources.pipelines.get(markType);
        
        for (const chartConfig of chartsForType) {
          if (!charts.includes(chartConfig)) continue; // Skip if not in current render list
          
          try {
            const context = chartConfig.context;
            if (!context) continue;
            
            const textureView = context.getCurrentTexture().createView();
            const commandEncoder = this.#device.createCommandEncoder({
              label: `${chartConfig.chartId}-${markType}-encoder`
            });
            
            const renderPassDescriptor = this.#createRenderPassDescriptor(textureView, chartConfig.canvas);
            const passEncoder = commandEncoder.beginRenderPass(renderPassDescriptor);
            
            // Pass enhanced shared resources for efficiency
            chartConfig.drawFunction(passEncoder, {
              pipelines: this.#sharedResources.pipelines,
              uniformBuffers: this.#sharedResources.uniformBuffers,
              shaderModules: this.#sharedResources.shaderModules,
              currentMarkType: markType,
              sharedPipeline: sharedResource?.pipeline
            });
            
            passEncoder.end();
            commandEncoders.push(commandEncoder.finish());
            chartConfig.needsRedraw = false;
            
          } catch (error) {
            console.error(`❌ Batch render failed for chart ${chartConfig.chartId}:`, error);
            this.debugger?.recordRenderError(error, `batch-render-${chartConfig.chartId}`);
          }
        }
      }
  
      // Submit all commands together for maximum GPU efficiency
      if (commandEncoders.length > 0) {
        this.#device.queue.submit(commandEncoders);
        console.debug(`✅ Submitted ${commandEncoders.length} render commands in batch`);
      }
    }
  
    #rebuildRenderBatches(charts) {
      console.debug(`🔄 Rebuilding render batches for ${charts.length} charts`);
      
      // Clear existing batches
      this.#renderBatches.byMarkType.clear();
      this.#renderBatches.byZIndex.clear();
      
      for (const chart of charts) {
        // Group by mark types
        for (const markType of chart.markTypes || ['unknown']) {
          if (!this.#renderBatches.byMarkType.has(markType)) {
            this.#renderBatches.byMarkType.set(markType, []);
          }
          this.#renderBatches.byMarkType.get(markType).push(chart);
        }
        
        // Group by z-index
        const zIndex = chart.zIndex || 0;
        if (!this.#renderBatches.byZIndex.has(zIndex)) {
          this.#renderBatches.byZIndex.set(zIndex, []);
        }
        this.#renderBatches.byZIndex.get(zIndex).push(chart);
      }
      
      this.#renderBatches.needsRebuild = false;
      
      console.debug(`✅ Render batches rebuilt:`, {
        markTypes: this.#renderBatches.byMarkType.size,
        zLevels: this.#renderBatches.byZIndex.size
      });
    }
  
    #fallbackIndividualRender(charts) {
      console.warn(`⚠️ Falling back to individual chart rendering`);
      
      for (const chartConfig of charts) {
        try {
          const context = chartConfig.context;
          if (!context) continue;
          
          const textureView = context.getCurrentTexture().createView();
          const commandEncoder = this.#device.createCommandEncoder();
          const renderPassDescriptor = this.#createRenderPassDescriptor(textureView, chartConfig.canvas);
          const passEncoder = commandEncoder.beginRenderPass(renderPassDescriptor);
  
          chartConfig.drawFunction(passEncoder, {
            pipelines: this.#sharedResources.pipelines,
            uniformBuffers: this.#sharedResources.uniformBuffers,
            shaderModules: this.#sharedResources.shaderModules
          });
          
          passEncoder.end();
          this.#device.queue.submit([commandEncoder.finish()]);
          chartConfig.needsRedraw = false;
          
        } catch (error) {
          console.error(`❌ Individual render failed for ${chartConfig.chartId}:`, error);
          this.debugger?.recordRenderError(error, `individual-render-${chartConfig.chartId}`);
        }
      }
    }
  
    #createRenderPassDescriptor(textureView, canvas) {
      if (this.#msaaSampleCount > 1) {
        const multisampleTexture = this.#device.createTexture({
          size: {
            width: canvas.width,
            height: canvas.height
          },
          sampleCount: this.#msaaSampleCount,
          format: this.#presentationFormat,
          usage: GPUTextureUsage.RENDER_ATTACHMENT
        });
  
        return {
          colorAttachments: [{
            view: multisampleTexture.createView(),
            resolveTarget: textureView,
            clearValue: { r: 0.0, g: 0.0, b: 0.0, a: 0.0 },
            loadOp: 'clear',
            storeOp: 'store',
          }],
        };
      } else {
        return {
          colorAttachments: [{
            view: textureView,
            clearValue: { r: 0.0, g: 0.0, b: 0.0, a: 0.0 },
            loadOp: 'clear',
            storeOp: 'store',
          }],
        };
      }
    }
  
    // ===== PUBLIC GETTERS =====
  
    get device() { return this.#device; }
    get presentationFormat() { return this.#presentationFormat; }
    get msaaSampleCount() { return this.#msaaSampleCount; }
    get isInitialized() { return this.#initialized; }
  
    // Debug getters (development only)
    get _registeredCharts() { return this.#registeredCharts; }
    get _sharedResources() { return this.#sharedResources; }
    get _renderQueue() { return this.#renderQueue; }
    get _needsRedraw() { return this.#needsRedraw; }
    get _rafId() { return this.#rafId; }
  
    // ===== MONITORING AND DEBUGGING =====
  
    getResourceStats() {
      return {
        pipelines: Array.from(this.#sharedResources.pipelines.entries()).map(([type, resource]) => ({
          type,
          refCount: resource.refCount,
          ageMs: Date.now() - resource.createdAt
        })),
        shaders: Array.from(this.#sharedResources.shaderModules.entries()).map(([type, resource]) => ({
          type,
          refCount: resource.refCount,
          ageMs: Date.now() - resource.createdAt
        })),
        uniformBuffers: Array.from(this.#sharedResources.uniformBuffers.entries()).map(([name, resource]) => ({
          name,
          refCount: resource.refCount,
          ageMs: Date.now() - resource.createdAt
        }))
      };
    }
  
    getPerformanceStats() {
      return {
        resourceStats: this.getResourceStats(),
        renderBatches: {
          markTypeBatches: this.#renderBatches.byMarkType.size,
          zIndexBatches: this.#renderBatches.byZIndex.size,
          needsRebuild: this.#renderBatches.needsRebuild
        },
        registeredCharts: this.#registeredCharts.size,
        lastRenderTime: this.#lastRenderTime
      };
    }
  
    debug() {
      if (!this.debugger) {
        console.warn('Debugging not enabled for this coordinator');
        return {};
      }
  
      return {
        logState: () => this.debugger.logCoordinationState(),
        analyzeUsage: () => this.debugger.analyzeResourceUsage(),
        debugChart: (chartId) => this.debugger.debugChart(chartId),
        getStats: () => this.getPerformanceStats()
      };
    }
  
    // ===== CLEANUP =====
  
    destroy() {
      if (this.#rafId !== null) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(this.#rafId);
        this.#rafId = null;
      }
  
      // Destroy shared resources
      for (const resource of this.#sharedResources.uniformBuffers.values()) {
        resource.buffer.destroy();
      }
  
      this.#sharedResources.uniformBuffers.clear();
      this.#sharedResources.pipelines.clear();
      this.#sharedResources.shaderModules.clear();
  
      if (this.#device) {
        this.#device.destroy();
      }
  
      this.#registeredCharts.clear();
      this.#renderQueue = [];
      this.#initialized = false;
  
      console.log('🧹 GPUCoordinator destroyed');
    }
  }
  
  // ===== CONTEXT FUNCTIONS =====
  
  export function setGPUCoordinator(config = {}) {
    const coordinator = new GPUCoordinator(config);
    
    // Only initialize on client-side
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      coordinator.initialize().catch(error => {
        console.error('Failed to initialize GPU coordinator:', error);
      });
    }
    
    return setContext('gpu-coordinator', coordinator);
  }
  
  export function getGPUContext() {
    const coordinator = getContext('gpu-coordinator');
    
    if (!coordinator) {
      console.error('GPU Coordinator context missing. Make sure to call setGPUCoordinator() in a parent component.');
      return null;
    }
    
    return coordinator;
  }
  
  // Development helper for browser console
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
    window.debugGPUCoordination = () => {
      const coordinator = window.__DEBUG_GPU_COORDINATOR;
      if (coordinator) {
        return coordinator.debug();
      } else {
        console.error('No GPU coordinator found. Make sure debugging is enabled.');
      }
    };
  }

<src/lib/gpu/resourceUtils.js>
  // src/lib/gpu/resourceUtils.js
  
  import { ResourceCreationError } from './errors.js';
  
  /**
   * Resource lifecycle states
   */
  export const ResourceState = {
    CREATING: 'creating',
    READY: 'ready',
    DESTROYING: 'destroying',
    DESTROYED: 'destroyed',
    ERROR: 'error'
  };
  
  /**
   * Enhanced resource wrapper with lifecycle management
   */
  export class ManagedResource {
    constructor(id, resource, metadata = {}) {
      this.id = id;
      this.resource = resource;
      this.metadata = metadata;
      this.state = ResourceState.READY;
      this.refCount = 0;
      this.createdAt = Date.now();
      this.lastUsed = Date.now();
      this.dependencies = new Set();
      this.dependents = new Set();
    }
  
    addRef() {
      if (this.state !== ResourceState.READY) {
        console.warn(`⚠️ Adding reference to resource ${this.id} in state ${this.state}`);
      }
      this.refCount++;
      this.lastUsed = Date.now();
      return this.refCount;
    }
  
    removeRef() {
      if (this.refCount > 0) {
        this.refCount--;
        this.lastUsed = Date.now();
      }
      return this.refCount;
    }
  
    addDependency(resourceId) {
      this.dependencies.add(resourceId);
    }
  
    addDependent(resourceId) {
      this.dependents.add(resourceId);
    }
  
    canDestroy() {
      return this.refCount === 0 && this.dependents.size === 0;
    }
  
    destroy() {
      if (this.state === ResourceState.DESTROYED) {
        console.warn(`⚠️ Resource ${this.id} already destroyed`);
        return;
      }
  
      if (!this.canDestroy()) {
        console.warn(`⚠️ Cannot destroy resource ${this.id}: refCount=${this.refCount}, dependents=${this.dependents.size}`);
        return false;
      }
  
      this.state = ResourceState.DESTROYING;
      
      try {
        // WebGPU resources don't always have explicit destroy methods
        if (this.resource && typeof this.resource.destroy === 'function') {
          this.resource.destroy();
        }
        this.state = ResourceState.DESTROYED;
        console.debug(`🗑️ Resource ${this.id} destroyed successfully`);
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        this.state = ResourceState.ERROR;
        console.error(`❌ Failed to destroy resource ${this.id}:`, error);
        return false;
      }
    }
  
    getInfo() {
      return {
        id: this.id,
        type: this.metadata.type || 'unknown',
        state: this.state,
        refCount: this.refCount,
        age: Date.now() - this.createdAt,
        timeSinceLastUse: Date.now() - this.lastUsed,
        dependencies: Array.from(this.dependencies),
        dependents: Array.from(this.dependents),
        canDestroy: this.canDestroy()
      };
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Advanced resource manager with dependency tracking and lifecycle management
   */
  export class ResourceManager {
    constructor(name) {
      this.name = name;
      this.resources = new Map();
      this.factories = new Map();
      this.cleanupInterval = null;
      this.cleanupIntervalMs = 30000; // 30 seconds
      this.maxAge = 300000; // 5 minutes
      this.enabled = true;
    }
  
    /**
     * Register a factory function for creating resources of a specific type
     */
    registerFactory(type, factoryFn, dependencies = []) {
      this.factories.set(type, {
        create: factoryFn,
        dependencies
      });
    }
  
    /**
     * Create or retrieve a shared resource with automatic dependency management
     */
    async createSharedResource(id, type, factoryOptions = {}) {
      const existing = this.resources.get(id);
      
      if (existing) {
        if (existing.state === ResourceState.READY) {
          existing.addRef();
          console.debug(`♻️ Reusing resource ${id} (refs: ${existing.refCount})`);
          return existing.resource;
        } else {
          console.warn(`⚠️ Resource ${id} exists but is in state ${existing.state}`);
        }
      }
  
      const factory = this.factories.get(type);
      if (!factory) {
        throw new ResourceCreationError(type, { 
          reason: `No factory registered for type: ${type}`,
          id 
        });
      }
  
      console.debug(`🆕 Creating new resource ${id} of type ${type}`);
  
      // Create managed resource wrapper
      const managedResource = new ManagedResource(id, null, {
        type,
        factoryOptions,
        ...factoryOptions.metadata
      });
      
      managedResource.state = ResourceState.CREATING;
      this.resources.set(id, managedResource);
  
      try {
        // Create dependencies first
        const dependencyResources = {};
        for (const depType of factory.dependencies) {
          const depId = `${depType}-shared`;
          dependencyResources[depType] = await this.createSharedResource(depId, depType);
          managedResource.addDependency(depId);
          
          // Add reverse dependency
          const depResource = this.resources.get(depId);
          if (depResource) {
            depResource.addDependent(id);
          }
        }
  
        // Create the actual resource
        const resource = await factory.create(factoryOptions, dependencyResources);
        
        managedResource.resource = resource;
        managedResource.state = ResourceState.READY;
        managedResource.addRef();
  
        console.debug(`✅ Resource ${id} created successfully`);
        return resource;
  
      } catch (error) {
        managedResource.state = ResourceState.ERROR;
        this.resources.delete(id);
        
        console.error(`❌ Failed to create resource ${id}:`, error);
        throw new ResourceCreationError(type, { 
          reason: error.message,
          id,
          originalError: error 
        });
      }
    }
  
    /**
     * Release a reference to a shared resource
     */
    releaseSharedResource(id) {
      const resource = this.resources.get(id);
      if (!resource) {
        console.warn(`⚠️ Attempted to release unknown resource: ${id}`);
        return false;
      }
  
      const refCount = resource.removeRef();
      console.debug(`🔄 Released resource ${id} (refs: ${refCount})`);
  
      // Don't immediately destroy - let cleanup cycle handle it
      return true;
    }
  
    /**
     * Force immediate cleanup of unused resources
     */
    cleanup(force = false) {
      const now = Date.now();
      const toDestroy = [];
  
      for (const [id, resource] of this.resources) {
        const shouldDestroy = force || (
          resource.canDestroy() && 
          (now - resource.lastUsed) > this.maxAge
        );
  
        if (shouldDestroy) {
          toDestroy.push(id);
        }
      }
  
      let destroyedCount = 0;
      for (const id of toDestroy) {
        const resource = this.resources.get(id);
        if (resource && resource.destroy()) {
          this.resources.delete(id);
          destroyedCount++;
          
          // Clean up dependency references
          for (const depId of resource.dependencies) {
            const depResource = this.resources.get(depId);
            if (depResource) {
              depResource.dependents.delete(id);
            }
          }
        }
      }
  
      if (destroyedCount > 0) {
        console.debug(`🧹 Cleaned up ${destroyedCount} unused resources`);
      }
  
      return destroyedCount;
    }
  
    /**
     * Start automatic cleanup cycle
     */
    startCleanup() {
      if (this.cleanupInterval) {
        console.warn(`⚠️ Cleanup already running for ${this.name}`);
        return;
      }
  
      this.cleanupInterval = setInterval(() => {
        this.cleanup();
      }, this.cleanupIntervalMs);
  
      console.debug(`🧹 Started automatic cleanup for ${this.name}`);
    }
  
    /**
     * Stop automatic cleanup cycle
     */
    stopCleanup() {
      if (this.cleanupInterval) {
        clearInterval(this.cleanupInterval);
        this.cleanupInterval = null;
        console.debug(`⏹️ Stopped automatic cleanup for ${this.name}`);
      }
    }
  
    /**
     * Get comprehensive resource statistics
     */
    getStats() {
      const stats = {
        totalResources: this.resources.size,
        byState: {},
        byType: {},
        totalRefs: 0,
        orphanedResources: 0,
        oldResources: 0,
        memoryEstimate: 0
      };
  
      const now = Date.now();
  
      for (const resource of this.resources.values()) {
        const info = resource.getInfo();
        
        // Count by state
        stats.byState[info.state] = (stats.byState[info.state] || 0) + 1;
        
        // Count by type
        stats.byType[info.type] = (stats.byType[info.type] || 0) + 1;
        
        // Total references
        stats.totalRefs += info.refCount;
        
        // Orphaned resources (no references)
        if (info.refCount === 0) {
          stats.orphanedResources++;
        }
        
        // Old resources
        if (info.age > this.maxAge) {
          stats.oldResources++;
        }
        
        // Memory estimate (rough)
        if (resource.metadata.estimatedSize) {
          stats.memoryEstimate += resource.metadata.estimatedSize;
        }
      }
  
      return stats;
    }
  
    /**
     * Get detailed information about all resources
     */
    getDetailedInfo() {
      return Array.from(this.resources.values()).map(resource => resource.getInfo());
    }
  
    /**
     * Destroy all resources and cleanup
     */
    destroy() {
      this.stopCleanup();
      
      const forcedCleanup = this.cleanup(true);
      console.log(`🧹 ${this.name} destroyed, cleaned up ${forcedCleanup} resources`);
      
      this.resources.clear();
      this.factories.clear();
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Convenience functions for common resource operations
   */
  
  let globalResourceManager = null;
  
  export function getGlobalResourceManager() {
    if (!globalResourceManager) {
      globalResourceManager = new ResourceManager('global');
      globalResourceManager.startCleanup();
    }
    return globalResourceManager;
  }
  
  export async function createSharedResource(id, type, options = {}) {
    const manager = getGlobalResourceManager();
    return manager.createSharedResource(id, type, options);
  }
  
  export function releaseSharedResource(id) {
    const manager = getGlobalResourceManager();
    return manager.releaseSharedResource(id);
  }
  
  export function trackResourceUsage() {
    const manager = getGlobalResourceManager();
    return manager.getStats();
  }
  
  /**
   * Resource usage monitoring utility
   */
  export class ResourceMonitor {
    constructor(options = {}) {
      this.enabled = options.enabled !== false;
      this.interval = options.interval || 10000; // 10 seconds
      this.maxHistory = options.maxHistory || 100;
      this.history = [];
      this.intervalId = null;
      this.onAlert = options.onAlert;
      this.thresholds = {
        orphanedResources: options.orphanedThreshold || 10,
        oldResources: options.oldResourceThreshold || 5,
        totalResources: options.totalResourceThreshold || 50,
        ...options.thresholds
      };
    }
  
    start() {
      if (!this.enabled || this.intervalId) return;
  
      this.intervalId = setInterval(() => {
        this.checkResources();
      }, this.interval);
  
      console.debug('📊 Resource monitoring started');
    }
  
    stop() {
      if (this.intervalId) {
        clearInterval(this.intervalId);
        this.intervalId = null;
        console.debug('📊 Resource monitoring stopped');
      }
    }
  
    checkResources() {
      const stats = trackResourceUsage();
      const timestamp = Date.now();
      
      this.history.push({ ...stats, timestamp });
      
      // Keep history size manageable
      if (this.history.length > this.maxHistory) {
        this.history.shift();
      }
  
      // Check for alerts
      this.checkAlerts(stats);
    }
  
    checkAlerts(stats) {
      const alerts = [];
  
      if (stats.orphanedResources > this.thresholds.orphanedResources) {
        alerts.push({
          type: 'orphaned_resources',
          message: `High number of orphaned resources: ${stats.orphanedResources}`,
          severity: 'warning',
          value: stats.orphanedResources,
          threshold: this.thresholds.orphanedResources
        });
      }
  
      if (stats.oldResources > this.thresholds.oldResources) {
        alerts.push({
          type: 'old_resources',
          message: `High number of old resources: ${stats.oldResources}`,
          severity: 'warning',
          value: stats.oldResources,
          threshold: this.thresholds.oldResources
        });
      }
  
      if (stats.totalResources > this.thresholds.totalResources) {
        alerts.push({
          type: 'total_resources',
          message: `High total resource count: ${stats.totalResources}`,
          severity: 'error',
          value: stats.totalResources,
          threshold: this.thresholds.totalResources
        });
      }
  
      if (alerts.length > 0 && this.onAlert) {
        this.onAlert(alerts, stats);
      }
    }
  
    getHistory() {
      return [...this.history];
    }
  
    getLatestStats() {
      return this.history[this.history.length - 1] || null;
    }
  }
  
  // Development utilities
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
    window.DivePlotResourceManager = getGlobalResourceManager();
  }

  // lib/layout/ChartLayout.svelte.js
  
  /**
   * ChartLayout manages all chart dimensions, margins, and coordinate transformations.
   * Extracted from ChartContainer.svelte to improve modularity and testability.
   */
  export class ChartLayout {
    // ✅ Class field declaration pattern (from docs)
    width = $state();
    height = $state();
  
    
    constructor(config = {}) {
      // Default configuration
      const defaults = {
        margin: { top: 30, right: 50, bottom: 35, left: 75 },
        height: 250,
        clipPadding: 0
      };
  
      // Merge user config with defaults
      this.config = { ...defaults, ...config };
      
      // ✅ First assignment in constructor pattern (from docs)
      this.height = this.config.height;
      
      // Non-reactive properties
      this.margin = this.config.margin;
      this.chartId = crypto.randomUUID();
    }
  
    // Computed properties using $derived would go here if this was a .svelte file
    // Since this is .js, we use getters that will be reactive when accessed from Svelte context
    
    /**
     * Inner chart width (total width minus left/right margins)
     */
    get innerWidth() {
      return this.width ? Math.max(0, this.width - this.margin.left - this.margin.right) : 0;
    }
  
    /**
     * Inner chart height (total height minus top/bottom margins)
     */
    get innerHeight() {
      return Math.max(0, this.height - this.margin.top - this.margin.bottom);
    }
  
    /**
     * Check if layout has valid dimensions for rendering
     */
    get isReady() {
      return this.width !== null && this.width > 0 && this.height > 0 && 
             this.innerWidth > 0 && this.innerHeight > 0;
    }
  
    /**
     * SVG transform string for translating to inner chart area
     */
    get innerTransform() {
      return `translate(${this.margin.left}, ${this.margin.top})`;
    }
  
    /**
     * Clip path ID for masking chart content to inner area
     */
    get clipPathId() {
      return `mask-${this.chartId}`;
    }
  
    /**
     * CSS/SVG clip path URL reference
     */
    get clipPathUrl() {
      return `url(#${this.clipPathId})`;
    }
  
    /**
     * Complete dimensions object for easy access
     */
    get dimensions() {
      return {
        width: this.width,
        height: this.height,
        innerWidth: this.innerWidth,
        innerHeight: this.innerHeight,
        margin: this.margin
      };
    }
  
    // Methods (using arrow functions to preserve 'this' binding per docs)
    updateWidth = (newWidth) => {
      this.width = newWidth;
    }
  
    updateHeight = (newHeight) => {
      this.height = newHeight;
    }
  
    updateMargin = (newMargin) => {
      this.margin = { ...this.margin, ...newMargin };
    }
  
    reset = () => {
      this.width = null;
      this.height = this.config.height;
      this.margin = { ...this.config.margin };
    }
  
    toInnerCoordinates = (point) => {
      return {
        x: point.x - this.margin.left,
        y: point.y - this.margin.top
      };
    }
  
    toScreenCoordinates = (innerPoint) => {
      return {
        x: innerPoint.x + this.margin.left,
        y: innerPoint.y + this.margin.top
      };
    }
  
    isWithinInnerBounds = (innerPoint) => {
      return {
        x: innerPoint.x >= 0 && innerPoint.x <= this.innerWidth,
        y: innerPoint.y >= 0 && innerPoint.y <= this.innerHeight,
        both: innerPoint.x >= 0 && innerPoint.x <= this.innerWidth && 
              innerPoint.y >= 0 && innerPoint.y <= this.innerHeight
      };
    }
  
    getActiveAxes = (point) => {
      const innerPoint = this.toInnerCoordinates(point);
      const bounds = this.isWithinInnerBounds(innerPoint);
      
      return {
        x: bounds.x,
        y: bounds.y, 
        innerPoint
      };
    }
  
    getDebugInfo = () => {
      return {
        config: this.config,
        width: this.width,
        height: this.height,
        margin: this.margin,
        innerWidth: this.innerWidth,
        innerHeight: this.innerHeight,
        isReady: this.isReady,
        chartId: this.chartId
      };
    }
  
    // Context data for reactive merging
  
    /**
     * Get context data with reactive getters for merging into chart context.
     * This is used by mergeReactive() to create the chart context object.
     * @returns {object} Object with getters that preserve reactivity
     */
    getContextData() {
      // Capture 'this' in closure to avoid binding issues in getters
      const layout = this;
      
      return {
        // Reactive properties - these are getters that will be called each time accessed
        get width() { return layout.width; },
        get height() { return layout.height; },
        get innerWidth() { return layout.innerWidth; },
        get innerHeight() { return layout.innerHeight; },
        get margin() { return layout.margin; },
        get chartId() { return layout.chartId; },
        get clipPathId() { return layout.clipPathId; },
        get clipPathUrl() { return layout.clipPathUrl; },
        get isReady() { return layout.isReady; },
        get innerTransform() { return layout.innerTransform; },
        get dimensions() { return layout.dimensions; },
        
        // Methods (bound to preserve 'this' context)
        updateWidth: layout.updateWidth.bind(layout),
        updateHeight: layout.updateHeight.bind(layout),
        updateMargin: layout.updateMargin.bind(layout),
        toInnerCoordinates: layout.toInnerCoordinates.bind(layout),
        toScreenCoordinates: layout.toScreenCoordinates.bind(layout),
        isWithinInnerBounds: layout.isWithinInnerBounds.bind(layout),
        getActiveAxes: layout.getActiveAxes.bind(layout),
        reset: layout.reset.bind(layout),
        getDebugInfo: layout.getDebugInfo.bind(layout)
      };
    }
  }

<src/lib/scales/ScalePriorities.js>
  // src/lib/scales/ScalePriorities.js
  
  /**
   * Scale type priority levels for resolving conflicts between marks
   */
  export const SCALE_PRIORITIES = {
    MANDATE: 3,    // Mark absolutely requires this type (e.g., barY needs band)
    PREFER: 2,     // Mark works best with this type (e.g., dot prefers linear)
    ACCEPT: 1,     // Mark can work with this type (fallback compatibility)
    DEFAULT: 0     // System fallback when no specific requirements
  };
  
  /**
   * Resolve scale type conflicts using priority system
   * @param {Array} requirements - Array of {type, priority, markType} objects
   * @returns {string} - Resolved scale type
   */
  export function resolveScaleType(requirements) {
    if (!requirements || requirements.length === 0) {
      return 'linear'; // Default fallback
    }
    
    // Group by priority level (highest first)
    const byPriority = requirements.reduce((acc, req) => {
      const priority = req.priority || SCALE_PRIORITIES.DEFAULT;
      if (!acc[priority]) acc[priority] = [];
      acc[priority].push(req);
      return acc;
    }, {});
    
    // Get highest priority level
    const priorities = Object.keys(byPriority)
      .map(p => parseInt(p))
      .sort((a, b) => b - a); // Descending
      
    const highestPriority = priorities[0];
    const topRequirements = byPriority[highestPriority];
    
    // Check for conflicts at highest priority level
    const uniqueTypes = [...new Set(topRequirements.map(req => req.type))];
    
    if (uniqueTypes.length > 1) {
      const markTypes = topRequirements.map(req => req.markType).join(', ');
      const conflictTypes = uniqueTypes.join(' vs ');
      
      throw new Error(
        `Scale type conflict: marks [${markTypes}] require incompatible scale types [${conflictTypes}]. ` +
        `Consider using separate ChartContainers or different mark types.`
      );
    }
    
    return uniqueTypes[0];
  }
</src/lib/scales/ScalePriorities.js>

<src/lib/scales/LinearScaleState.svelte.js>
  import { Tween } from 'svelte/motion';
  import { cubicOut, cubicInOut } from 'svelte/easing';
  import { scaleLinear } from 'd3-scale';
  import { extent as d3Extent } from 'd3-array';
  
  // current zoom tweens, extents, and filtering?
  const tweenOptions = {
    duration: 300,
    easing: cubicOut,
  };
  
  export class LinearScaleState {
    // Basic scale properties
    name = $state('');  // e.g., 'x', 'y', 'color'
  
    #initExtent = [];
    #extent = [];
    #extents = new Map(); // Stores all extents in map by mark identifier
    // domain = new Tween([0, 100], tweenOptions);
    domainLimits = {
      min: 0,
      max: 100,
      minWidth: 1
    };
    zoomLimits = {
      max: 10,
      min: 100
    }
  
    // Syncing crosshair and labels for mouse position
    currentDataPoint = $state();
  
    // Freeze/debounce functionality
    #enableUpdates = $state(true);
    #frozenExtent = null; // Stores the frozen extent when updates are disabled
  
    constructor(name, extent = [0, 100], type = 'linear', zoomLimits = [10, 100], options = {}) {
      this.name = name;
      this.#initExtent = extent;
      this.#extent = extent;
      this.type = type;
      this.zoomLimits.max = zoomLimits[0]
      this.zoomLimits.min = zoomLimits[1]
      this.ready = $state(false);
      this.domain = new Tween(extent, tweenOptions);
  
      this.zoomInEffect = {
        enabled: options.zoomInEffect ?? true,
        factor: options.zoomInFactor ?? 5,
        duration: options.zoomInDuration ?? 600, //need to match regular
        easing: options.zoomInEasing ?? cubicOut
      };
    }
  
    setReady() {
      this.ready = true;
    }
  
    /**
     * Enable or disable scale updates (freeze scale when disabled)
     * @param {boolean} enabled - Whether to enable scale updates
     */
    setUpdatesEnabled(enabled) {
      const wasEnabled = this.#enableUpdates;
      this.#enableUpdates = enabled;
      
      if (wasEnabled && !enabled) {
        // Just disabled - freeze current extent
        this.#frozenExtent = [...this.#extent];
      } else if (!wasEnabled && enabled) {
        // Just enabled - unfreeze and recalculate if needed
        this.#frozenExtent = null;
        this.updateExtentFromExtents();
      }
    }
  
    /**
     * Get current updates state
     * @returns {boolean} Whether updates are enabled
     */
    get updatesEnabled() {
      return this.#enableUpdates;
    }
  
    /**
     * Get the effective extent (frozen or current)
     * @returns {Array} The extent being used
     */
    get effectiveExtent() {
      return this.#enableUpdates ? this.#extent : this.#frozenExtent || this.#extent;
    }
  
    addMark(range, identifier) {
      let processedRange;
      
      // Process based on array length
      if (range.length === 2) {
        // For length 2, ensure first value is smaller than second
        processedRange = [Math.min(range[0], range[1]), Math.max(range[0], range[1])];
      } else if (range.length > 2) {
        // For larger arrays, run d3.extent
        processedRange = d3Extent(range);
      } else if (range.length === 1) {
        // Handle single value case - create a range with the same value
        processedRange = [range[0], range[0]];
      } else {
        // Empty array case - fail silently or with minimal logging
        return this;
      }
      
      // Store the processed range with its identifier
      this.#extents.set(identifier, processedRange);
  
      if (this.#enableUpdates) {
        this.updateExtentFromExtents();
      }
      return this;
    }
  
    removeMark(identifier) {
      if (this.#extents.has(identifier)) {
        this.#extents.delete(identifier);
  
        if (this.#enableUpdates) {
          this.updateExtentFromExtents();
        }
      }
      return this;
    }
  
    // Update the extent based on all ranges in the map
    updateExtentFromExtents(nice=true) {
      // Don't update if frozen
      if (!this.#enableUpdates) {
        return;
      }
  
      if (this.#extents.size === 0) {
        // Default value when no ranges are present
        this.#extent = [0, 0];
        return;
      }
      
      const extents = Array.from(this.#extents.values());
      
      // Find the min and max value
      const minStart = Math.min(...extents.map(range => range[0]));
      const maxEnd = Math.max(...extents.map(range => range[1]));
      
      // console.log(`Computing extent from extents: min=${minStart}, max=${maxEnd}`);
      if (nice) {
        // TODO include min ticks per chart instance here? Slight mis-match otherwise.
        // Want the same domain across instances so makes sense to keep calculation here
        this.#extent = scaleLinear().domain([minStart, maxEnd]).nice(5).domain();
      } else {
        this.#extent = [minStart, maxEnd];
      }
  
      this.resetDomainLimits();
      this.resetZoom();
    }
  
    getExtents() {
      return Object.fromEntries(this.#extents);
    }
  
    getBaseScale(axis, innerWidthOrHeight) {
      if (axis === 'x') {
        return scaleLinear().range([0, innerWidthOrHeight])
      } else if (axis === 'y') {
        return scaleLinear().range([innerWidthOrHeight, 0])
      }
    }
  
    applyZoomDomain(innerPoint, scaleFactor) {
  
      const [min, max] = this.domain.target;
      const minLimit = this.domainLimits.min;
      const maxLimit = this.domainLimits.max;
      const limitWidth = maxLimit - minLimit;
      const minDomainWidth = this.domainLimits.minWidth;
  
      // Calculate how far the mouse is along the scale (0 to 1)
      const mouseRatio = (innerPoint - min) / (max - min);
  
      // Calculate the new domain range (current range divided by zoom factor)
      const newDomainWidth = (max - min) / scaleFactor;
      const effectiveDomainWidth = Math.max(newDomainWidth, minDomainWidth);
  
      let newMin = innerPoint - (mouseRatio * effectiveDomainWidth);
      let newMax = newMin + effectiveDomainWidth;
  
      // Check if new domain width exceeds limit width
      if (effectiveDomainWidth > limitWidth) {
        // Set to full available range if zoom out is too extreme
        newMin = minLimit;
        newMax = maxLimit;
        this.currentDataPoint = null; // crosshair gets offset until new mouse move
  
      } else {
        // Ensure the domain stays within the limits while preserving width
        if (newMin < minLimit) {
          newMin = minLimit;
          newMax = newMin + effectiveDomainWidth; // Preserve width
          this.currentDataPoint = null; // crosshair gets offset until new mouse move
        }
        
        if (newMax > maxLimit) {
          newMax = maxLimit;
          newMin = newMax - effectiveDomainWidth; // Preserve width
          this.currentDataPoint = null; // crosshair gets offset until new mouse move
          
          // Double-check if this pushed us below minLimit
          if (newMin < minLimit) {
            newMin = minLimit;
            // We can't preserve the width in this case
          }
        }
      }
  
      this.domain.target = [newMin, newMax];
    }
  
    applyPanDomain(dx) {
      const [min, max] = this.domain.current;
      const range = max - min;
      
      // First check if we'd exceed either limit
      if (min + dx < this.domainLimits.min) {
          // Would exceed minimum limit, snap to minimum
          this.domain.target = [this.domainLimits.min, this.domainLimits.min + range];
      } else if (max + dx > this.domainLimits.max) {
          // Would exceed maximum limit, snap to maximum
          this.domain.target = [this.domainLimits.max - range, this.domainLimits.max];
      } else {
          // Within bounds, apply the pan normally
          this.domain.target = [min + dx, max + dx];
      }
    }
  
    updateScaleDomain(scale, current = true) {
      // Use effective extent for scale domain calculation
      const targetDomain = current ? this.domain.current : this.domain.target;
      return { scale: scale.domain(targetDomain) }
    }
  
    resetZoom() {
      const [currentMin, currentMax] = this.domain.target;
      const [targetMin, targetMax] = this.effectiveExtent;
      
      // Use the magnitude of the data to determine tolerance
      const dataScale = Math.max(
        Math.abs(targetMin), 
        Math.abs(targetMax), 
        Math.abs(targetMax - targetMin)
      );
      
      // Tolerance is 0.01% of the data scale (or minimum epsilon for very small numbers)
      const tolerance = Math.max(dataScale * 0.0001, Number.EPSILON * 1000);
      
      const alreadyTargetingCorrectExtent = 
        Math.abs(currentMin - targetMin) < tolerance && 
        Math.abs(currentMax - targetMax) < tolerance;
        
      if (alreadyTargetingCorrectExtent) {
        return;
      }
    
      if (!this.ready && this.zoomInEffect.enabled) {
        const [min, max] = this.effectiveExtent;
        
        if (min === max || !isFinite(min) || !isFinite(max)) {
          console.warn(`⚠️ Scale ${this.name} skipping zoom effect - invalid extent:`, [min, max]);
          this.domain.set(this.effectiveExtent, { duration: 0 });
          return;
        }
        
        const center = (min + max) / 2;
        const range = max - min;
        const expandedRange = range * this.zoomInEffect.factor;
        const expandedExtent = [
          center - expandedRange / 2, 
          center + expandedRange / 2
        ];
    
    
        this.domain.set(expandedExtent, { duration: 0 });
        this.domain.set(this.effectiveExtent, { 
          duration: this.zoomInEffect.duration,
          easing: this.zoomInEffect.easing
        });
        
      } else if (!this.ready) {
        this.domain.set(this.effectiveExtent, { duration: 0 });
        
      } else {
        this.domain.set(this.effectiveExtent, {duration: 600, easing: cubicInOut});
      }
    }
  
    resetDomainLimits() {
      // Use effective extent for domain limits
      const [min, max] = this.effectiveExtent;
      const width = max - min;
  
      this.domainLimits.min = min - this.zoomLimits.max * width
      this.domainLimits.max = max + this.zoomLimits.max * width
      this.domainLimits.minWidth = width / this.zoomLimits.min;
    }
      
    zoomToRange([min, max]) {
      this.domain.target = [min, max];
    }
  
    /**
     * Get debug information about current state
     * @returns {Object} Debug information
     */
    getDebugInfo() {
      return {
        updatesEnabled: this.#enableUpdates,
        currentExtent: [...this.#extent],
        frozenExtent: this.#frozenExtent ? [...this.#frozenExtent] : null,
        effectiveExtent: [...this.effectiveExtent],
        extentsCount: this.#extents.size,
        extents: Object.fromEntries(this.#extents)
      };
    }
  
    destroy() {
      this.#frozenExtent = null;
    }
  }
</src/lib/scales/LinearScaleState.svelte.js>

<src/lib/scales/index.js>
  export {
    setScaleRegistry,
    getScaleState,
  } from './ScaleRegistry.svelte.js'
  
  export { LinearScaleState } from './LinearScaleState.svelte.js'
  export { BandScaleState } from './BandScaleState.svelte.js'
  export { SCALE_PRIORITIES } from './ScalePriorities.js';
