<src/lib/scales/ScaleRegistry.svelte.js>
  import { setContext, getContext, hasContext } from 'svelte';
  import { LinearScaleState } from "./LinearScaleState.svelte";
  import { BandScaleState } from "./BandScaleState.svelte";
  import { scaleOrdinal } from 'd3-scale';
  
  // Define the Scale Registry state class
  class ScaleRegistry {
    constructor() {
      this.scales = {
        linear: new Map(),
        band: new Map(),
        ordinal: new Map(),
      }
    }
  
    getOrCreateScale(name, type='linear') {
      if (!this.scales[type].has(name)) {
        if (type === 'linear') {
          this.scales[type].set(name, new LinearScaleState(name));
        } else if (type === 'band') {
          this.scales[type].set(name, new BandScaleState(name));
        } else if (type === 'ordinal') {
          // Default color scale - this could be made configurable
          this.scales[type].set(name,  scaleOrdinal()
        	  .range(['#EFB605', '#FFF84A', '#FF0266'])
        	  .domain(['Adelie', 'Gentoo', 'Chinstrap']));
        }
      } else {
        // console.log(`Found existing scale: ${name}`);
      }
      return this.scales[type].get(name);
    }
    
  }
  
  // Create and set the Scale Registry context
  export function setScaleRegistry() {
    const state = new ScaleRegistry();
    return setContext('scale-registry', state);
  }
  
  // Retrieve a specific scale from the Scale Registry context
  export function getScaleState(name, type='linear') {
    const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
    
    if (!scaleRegistry) {
      console.error('Scale Registry context missing. Make sure to call setScaleRegistry() in a parent component.');
      return null;
    }
    
    return scaleRegistry.getOrCreateScale(name, type);
  }
</src/lib/scales/ScaleRegistry.svelte.js>

<src/lib/scales/ChartScales.svelte.js>
  import { getScaleState } from '$lib/scales';
  import { resolveScaleType, SCALE_PRIORITIES } from './ScalePriorities.js';
  
  export class ChartScales {
    // ✅ UPDATED: Store requirements as objects with priority info
    scaleRequirements = $state({
      x: [], // [{ type: 'band', priority: 3, markType: 'bar-abc123' }, ...]
      y: []  // [{ type: 'linear', priority: 2, markType: 'dot-def456' }, ...]
    });
    
    active = $state(false);
  
    constructor(config) {
      this.config = config;
    }
  
    // ✅ Reactive getters for config access
    get xLabel() { return this.config.xLabel; }
    get yLabel() { return this.config.yLabel; }
    get layout() { return this.config.layout; }
  
    // ✅ UPDATED: Resolve scale types using priority system
    scaleTypes = $derived({
      x: resolveScaleType(this.scaleRequirements.x),
      y: resolveScaleType(this.scaleRequirements.y)
    });
  
    // ✅ UPDATED: Use resolved scale types instead of hardcoded 'linear'
    xScaleState = $derived(getScaleState(this.xLabel, this.scaleTypes.x));
    yScaleState = $derived(getScaleState(this.yLabel, this.scaleTypes.y));
  
    // ✅ Base scales (unchanged)
    baseXScale = $derived(
      this.layout.isReady 
        ? this.xScaleState.getBaseScale('x', this.layout.innerWidth) 
        : null
    );
  
    baseYScale = $derived(
      this.layout.isReady 
        ? this.yScaleState.getBaseScale('y', this.layout.innerHeight) 
        : null
    );
  
    // ✅ Scales (unchanged)
    xScale = $derived(
      this.layout.isReady && this.baseXScale
        ? this.xScaleState.updateScaleDomain(this.baseXScale) 
        : null
    );
  
    yScale = $derived(
      this.layout.isReady && this.baseYScale
        ? this.yScaleState.updateScaleDomain(this.baseYScale) 
        : null
    );
  
    // ✅ Target scales (unchanged)
    xTargetScale = $derived(
      this.layout.isReady && this.baseXScale
        ? this.xScaleState.updateScaleDomain(this.baseXScale, false) 
        : null
    );
  
    yTargetScale = $derived(
      this.layout.isReady && this.baseYScale
        ? this.yScaleState.updateScaleDomain(this.baseYScale, false) 
        : null
    );
  
    // ✅ Current data points (unchanged)
    currentXDataPoint = $derived(this.xScaleState.currentDataPoint);
    currentYDataPoint = $derived(this.yScaleState.currentDataPoint);
  
    // ✅ Screen coordinates (unchanged)
    currentXScreenPoint = $derived(this.xScale?.scale(this.currentXDataPoint));
    currentYScreenPoint = $derived(this.yScale?.scale(this.currentYDataPoint));
  
    // ✅ Scales ready (unchanged)
    scalesReady = $derived(
      this.xScaleState?.ready && this.yScaleState?.ready
    );
  
    // ✅ NEW: Enhanced registerScaleType with priority support
    registerScaleType = (dimension, type, priority = SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER, markId = 'unknown') => {
      const requirements = this.scaleRequirements[dimension];
      
      // Check if we already have this exact requirement
      const existing = requirements.find(req => 
        req.type === type && 
        req.priority === priority && 
        req.markId === markId
      );
      
      if (!existing) {
        // Add new requirement
        requirements.push({ type, priority, markId });
        
        // console.log(`📊 Registered scale requirement: ${dimension} = ${type} (priority: ${priority}, mark: ${markId})`);
        
        // Trigger reactivity by reassigning the array
        this.scaleRequirements[dimension] = [...requirements];
      }
    }
  
    // ✅ NEW: Remove scale requirements for a specific mark
    unregisterScaleType = (dimension, markId) => {
      const requirements = this.scaleRequirements[dimension];
      const filtered = requirements.filter(req => req.markId !== markId);
      
      if (filtered.length !== requirements.length) {
        console.log(`📊 Removed scale requirements for ${markId} on ${dimension} axis`);
        this.scaleRequirements[dimension] = filtered;
      }
    }
  
    // ✅ NEW: Get current scale requirements summary for debugging
    getScaleRequirementsSummary = () => {
      return {
        x: {
          resolved: this.scaleTypes.x,
          requirements: this.scaleRequirements.x.map(req => 
            `${req.markId}:${req.type}(${req.priority})`
          )
        },
        y: {
          resolved: this.scaleTypes.y,
          requirements: this.scaleRequirements.y.map(req => 
            `${req.markId}:${req.type}(${req.priority})`
          )
        }
      };
    }
  
    // ✅ All interaction methods remain unchanged
    clearCurrentDataPoints = () => {
      this.xScaleState.currentDataPoint = null;
      this.yScaleState.currentDataPoint = null;
    }
  
    updateCurrentDataPoints = (innerPoint) => {
      if (this.scaleTypes.x === 'linear') {
        this.xScaleState.currentDataPoint = this.xScale?.scale.invert(innerPoint.x);
      }
      if (this.scaleTypes.y === 'linear') {
        this.yScaleState.currentDataPoint = this.yScale?.scale.invert(innerPoint.y);
      }
    }
  
    resetZoom = (axes = { x: true, y: true }) => {
      if (axes.x && this.scaleTypes.x === 'linear') {
        this.xScaleState.resetZoom();
      }
      if (axes.y && this.scaleTypes.y === 'linear') {
        this.yScaleState.resetZoom();
      }
    }
  
    applyZoom = (innerPoint, scaleFactor, doX, doY) => {
      if (doX && this.scaleTypes.x === 'linear') {
        this.xScaleState.applyZoomDomain(
          this.xScale.scale.invert(innerPoint.x), 
          scaleFactor, 
          'x', 
          this.layout.innerWidth
        );
      }
      if (doY && this.scaleTypes.y === 'linear') {
        this.yScaleState.applyZoomDomain(
          this.yScale.scale.invert(innerPoint.y), 
          scaleFactor, 
          'y', 
          this.layout.innerHeight
        );
      }
    }
  
    applyPan = (dx, dy, activePanX, activePanY) => {
      if (activePanX && this.scaleTypes.x === 'linear') {
        this.xScaleState.applyPanDomain(-dx);
      }
      if (activePanY && this.scaleTypes.y === 'linear') {
        this.yScaleState.applyPanDomain(-dy);
      }
    }
  
    calculatePanningAnchor = (innerPoint) => {
      return {
        x: this.scaleTypes.x === 'linear' ? this.xScale.scale.invert(innerPoint.x) : 0,
        y: this.scaleTypes.y === 'linear' ? this.yScale.scale.invert(innerPoint.y) : 0
      };
    }
  
    calculatePanDeltas = (innerPoint, panningAnchorPoint) => {
      return {
        dx: this.scaleTypes.x === 'linear' ? this.xScale.scale.invert(innerPoint.x) - panningAnchorPoint.x : 0,
        dy: this.scaleTypes.y === 'linear' ? this.yScale.scale.invert(innerPoint.y) - panningAnchorPoint.y : 0
      };
    }
  
    // ✅ UPDATED: Context data includes new methods
    getContextData() {
      const coordinator = this;
      
      return {
        get xScale() { return coordinator.xScale?.scale; },
        get yScale() { return coordinator.yScale?.scale; },
        get xTargetScale() { return coordinator.xTargetScale?.scale; },
        get yTargetScale() { return coordinator.yTargetScale?.scale; },
        
        get xLabel() { return coordinator.xLabel; },
        get yLabel() { return coordinator.yLabel; },
        
        get currentXDataPoint() { return coordinator.currentXDataPoint; },
        get currentYDataPoint() { return coordinator.currentYDataPoint; },
        get currentXScreenPoint() { return coordinator.currentXScreenPoint; },
        get currentYScreenPoint() { return coordinator.currentYScreenPoint; },
        
        get xScaleState() { return coordinator.xScaleState; },
        get yScaleState() { return coordinator.yScaleState; },
        
        get scaleTypes() { return coordinator.scaleTypes; },
        get scalesReady() { return coordinator.scalesReady; },
        get active() { return coordinator.active; },
        
        // ✅ NEW: Enhanced registration methods
        registerScaleType: coordinator.registerScaleType,
        unregisterScaleType: coordinator.unregisterScaleType,
        getScaleRequirementsSummary: coordinator.getScaleRequirementsSummary
      };
    }
  }
</src/lib/scales/ChartScales.svelte.js>

<src/lib/scales/BandScaleState.svelte.js>
  import { scaleBand } from 'd3-scale';
  
  export class BandScaleState {
    // Basic scale properties
    name = $state('');
    type = 'band';
    ready = $state(false);
  
    #domain = $state([]);
    #extents = new Map(); // Stores all domains from different marks
  
    // Band-specific properties
    #padding = 0.1;
    #paddingInner = null;
    #paddingOuter = null;
  
    // Freeze/debounce functionality
    #enableUpdates = $state(true);
    #frozenDomain = null;
  
    // ✅ NEW: Reactive category→index mapping cache
    #categoryIndexMap = $derived.by(() => {
      // Make dependencies explicit for proper reactivity tracking
      const domain = this.#enableUpdates ? this.#domain : this.#frozenDomain || this.#domain;
      
      const map = new Map();
      domain.forEach((category, index) => {
        map.set(category, index);
      });
      return map;
    });
  
    constructor(name, domain = [], options = {}) {
      this.name = name;
      this.#domain = [...domain];
      this.#padding = options.padding ?? 0.1;
      this.#paddingInner = options.paddingInner ?? null;
      this.#paddingOuter = options.paddingOuter ?? null;
    }
  
    setReady() {
      this.ready = true;
    }
  
    /**
     * Enable or disable scale updates
     */
    setUpdatesEnabled(enabled) {
      const wasEnabled = this.#enableUpdates;
      this.#enableUpdates = enabled;
      
      if (wasEnabled && !enabled) {
        this.#frozenDomain = [...this.#domain];
      } else if (!wasEnabled && enabled) {
        this.#frozenDomain = null;
        this.updateDomainFromExtents();
      }
    }
  
    get updatesEnabled() {
      return this.#enableUpdates;
    }
  
    get effectiveDomain() {
      return this.#enableUpdates ? this.#domain : this.#frozenDomain || this.#domain;
    }
  
    /**
     * Check if the scale is ready for GPU processing
     * @returns {boolean} True if scale has categories and mapping is ready
     */
    get isReadyForGPU() {
      return this.ready && 
             this.effectiveDomain.length > 0 && 
             this.#categoryIndexMap instanceof Map &&
             this.#extents.size > 0; // Ensure we have received data from marks
    }
  
    /**
     * Add categorical data from a mark
     */
    addMark(categories, identifier) {
      // Get unique categories, preserving order of first appearance
      const uniqueCategories = [...new Set(categories.filter(c => c != null))];
      
      this.#extents.set(identifier, uniqueCategories);
  
      if (this.#enableUpdates) {
        this.updateDomainFromExtents();
      }
      return this;
    }
  
    removeMark(identifier) {
      if (this.#extents.has(identifier)) {
        this.#extents.delete(identifier);
  
        if (this.#enableUpdates) {
          this.updateDomainFromExtents();
        }
      }
      return this;
    }
  
    /**
     * Update domain based on all categories from all marks
     */
    updateDomainFromExtents() {
      if (!this.#enableUpdates) {
        return;
      }
  
      if (this.#extents.size === 0) {
        this.#domain = [];
        return;
      }
      
      // Collect all unique categories while preserving order
      const allCategories = [];
      const seen = new Set();
      
      for (const categories of this.#extents.values()) {
        for (const category of categories) {
          if (!seen.has(category)) {
            seen.add(category);
            allCategories.push(category);
          }
        }
      }
      
      this.#domain = allCategories;
    }
  
    // ✅ NEW: Process categorical data for GPU consumption
    /**
     * Convert categorical data to indices for GPU processing
     * This is the authoritative source for category→index conversion
     * @param {Array} categoryData - Array of category values from mark data
     * @param {string} markId - Identifier for the mark (for debugging)
     * @returns {Float32Array} Array of indices corresponding to categories
     */
    processDataForGPU(categoryData, markId = 'unknown') {
      const categoryMap = this.#categoryIndexMap;
      
      // Defensive check - ensure we have a valid Map
      if (!(categoryMap instanceof Map)) {
        console.error(`🚨 BandScale "${this.name}": categoryMap is not a Map:`, categoryMap);
        console.error('Domain state:', {
          enableUpdates: this.#enableUpdates,
          domain: this.#domain,
          frozenDomain: this.#frozenDomain,
          effectiveDomain: this.effectiveDomain
        });
        // Fallback: create a temporary mapping
        const fallbackMap = new Map();
        this.effectiveDomain.forEach((category, index) => {
          fallbackMap.set(category, index);
        });
        return this.#processWithMap(categoryData, fallbackMap, markId);
      }
      
      return this.#processWithMap(categoryData, categoryMap, markId);
    }
  
    /**
     * Helper method to process data with a given category map
     * @private
     */
    #processWithMap(categoryData, categoryMap, markId) {
      const unmappedCategories = new Set();
      
      const indices = new Float32Array(categoryData.map(category => {
        const index = categoryMap.get(category);
        if (index !== undefined) {
          return index;
        } else {
          unmappedCategories.add(category);
          return 0; // Default to first category index
        }
      }));
  
      // Warn about unmapped categories in development with full context
      if (unmappedCategories.size > 0 && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
        console.warn(
          `🔶 BandScale "${this.name}": Unmapped categories for mark "${markId}":`,
          Array.from(unmappedCategories).slice(0, 5), // Limit output for readability
          unmappedCategories.size > 5 ? `... and ${unmappedCategories.size - 5} more` : '',
          `\nValid categories:`, $state.snapshot(this.effectiveDomain)
        );
      }
  
      return indices;
    }
  
    /**
     * Get the current category→index mapping (for debugging)
     * @returns {Map} Current category→index mapping
     */
    getCategoryIndexMap() {
      return new Map(this.#categoryIndexMap);
    }
  
    getExtents() {
      return Object.fromEntries(this.#extents);
    }
  
    /**
     * Create base D3 band scale
     */
    getBaseScale(axis, innerWidthOrHeight) {
      const scale = scaleBand().range(
        axis === 'x' ? [0, innerWidthOrHeight] : [innerWidthOrHeight, 0]
      );
  
      // Apply padding configuration
      if (this.#paddingInner !== null && this.#paddingOuter !== null) {
        scale.paddingInner(this.#paddingInner).paddingOuter(this.#paddingOuter);
      } else {
        scale.padding(this.#padding);
      }
  
      return scale;
    }
  
    /**
     * Update scale domain - bands don't animate like linear scales
     */
    updateScaleDomain(scale) {
      return { scale: scale.domain(this.effectiveDomain) };
    }
  
    /**
     * Get positioning information for a category
     * Used by the axis renderer for proper tick positioning
     */
    getPosition(category) {
      // This would be used by axes to position ticks correctly
      const index = this.effectiveDomain.indexOf(category);
      if (index === -1) return { current: 0 };
      
      // Return normalized position (0-1) - will be converted to pixels by the scale
      return { 
        current: index / Math.max(1, this.effectiveDomain.length - 1) 
      };
    }
  
    /**
     * Band scales don't typically support zoom/pan interactions
     * These methods exist for API consistency but are no-ops
     */
    applyZoomDomain() {
      // Bands don't zoom
      return;
    }
  
    applyPanDomain() {
      // Bands don't pan
      return;
    }
  
    resetZoom() {
      // No zoom to reset for bands
      return;
    }
  
    /**
     * Get debug information
     */
    getDebugInfo() {
      return {
        updatesEnabled: this.#enableUpdates,
        currentDomain: [...this.#domain],
        frozenDomain: this.#frozenDomain ? [...this.#frozenDomain] : null,
        effectiveDomain: [...this.effectiveDomain],
        extentsCount: this.#extents.size,
        extents: Object.fromEntries(this.#extents),
        padding: this.#padding,
        categoryIndexMap: Object.fromEntries(this.#categoryIndexMap), // Show current mapping
        categoryCount: this.effectiveDomain.length
      };
    }
  
    destroy() {
      this.#frozenDomain = null;
      this.#extents.clear();
    }
  }
</src/lib/scales/BandScaleState.svelte.js>

<src/routes/flights_200k/+page.svelte>
  $$render<script>
    import { Coordinator, coordinator, wasmConnector } from "@uwdata/mosaic-core";
    import { loadCSV, loadParquet } from "@uwdata/mosaic-sql";
    // import { setScaleRegistry } from "$lib/scales";
    import { setCoordinators } from "$lib";
    import { onMount, onDestroy, setContext } from "svelte";
    import MainView from "./MainView.svelte";
    
    // setScaleRegistry();
    setCoordinators();
  
    let coord;
    // setContext('mosaic-coordinator', coord);
  
    let isLoaded = $state(false);
    let loadError = $state(null);
  
    const logs = [];
    const queryLogger = {
      ...console,
  
      debug(...values) {
        if (values.length > 0 && typeof values[0] === 'string' && values[0].includes('Query')) {
          const queryLog = values[1];
  
          if (queryLog && queryLog.sql) {
            const timestamp = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('sv-SE', { hour12: false});
            console.log(`[${timestamp}] \n${queryLog.sql}`);
            logs.push({ query: queryLog.sql, time: new Date()})
          }
        }
      },
      getLogs() {
        return logs;
      }
    };
  
    onMount(async () => {
      try {
        const wasm = wasmConnector();
        coord = new Coordinator();
        coord.databaseConnector(wasm);
  
        coord.manager.logQueries(true);
        coord.manager.logger(queryLogger);
      
        setContext('mosaic-coordinator', coord);
  
  
        await coord.exec(
          loadParquet("flights", `${window.location.origin}/data/flights-200k.parquet`)
  
        );
  
        isLoaded = true
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Initialization error:", error);
        loadError = error.message;
      }
    });
  </script>
  
  <div class="app-container">
    {#if loadError}
      <div class="error-container">Error loading data: {loadError}</div>
    {:else if !isLoaded}
      <div class="loading-container">Loading parquet data...</div>
    {:else}
      <MainView />
    {/if}
  </div>
  
  
  <style>
    .app-container {
      width: 100%;
      min-height: 100vh;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
    }
  
    .loading-container,
    .error-container {
      display: flex;
      justify-content: center;
      align-items: center;
      min-height: 200px;
      padding: 2rem;
    }
  </style>
</src/routes/flights_200k/+page.svelte>
